# ==============================================================================
# R/04_sindy_baselines.R
# Classical STLSQ SINDy, Bayesian/Ensemble UQ-SINDy, and Rational SINDy-PI
# ==============================================================================

if (!exists("build_poly_library", mode = "function")) {
  if (file.exists("R/02_basis_builders.R")) {
    source("R/02_basis_builders.R")
  } else if (file.exists("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")) source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
}
if (!exists("extract_canonical_atoms", mode = "function")) {
  if (file.exists("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) {
    source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
  } else if (file.exists("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
}

#' Fit Sequential Thresholded Least Squares (STLSQ) on standardized design matrix Phi
stlsq_fit_single <- function(Phi, y, lambda = 0.05, alpha = 1e-5, max_iter = 15L) {
  n <- nrow(Phi)
  p <- ncol(Phi)
  y_mean <- mean(y)
  y_c <- y - y_mean

  col_means <- colMeans(Phi)
  col_sds <- apply(Phi, 2, sd)
  valid_cols <- which(is.finite(col_sds) & col_sds > 1e-10)
  if (length(valid_cols) == 0L) {
    return(list(intercept = y_mean, coefs = setNames(rep(0.0, p), colnames(Phi)), active = integer(0), lambda = lambda))
  }

  Phi_std <- scale(Phi[, valid_cols, drop = FALSE], center = col_means[valid_cols], scale = col_sds[valid_cols])
  y_sd <- sd(y_c)
  if (!is.finite(y_sd) || y_sd < 1e-10) y_sd <- 1.0
  y_std <- y_c / y_sd

  active_loc <- seq_along(valid_cols)
  w_std <- rep(0.0, length(valid_cols))

  for (it in seq_len(max_iter)) {
    if (length(active_loc) == 0L) break
    X_a <- Phi_std[, active_loc, drop = FALSE]
    XtX <- crossprod(X_a) + diag(alpha * n, ncol(X_a))
    Xty <- crossprod(X_a, y_std)
    w_a <- tryCatch(as.numeric(solve(XtX, Xty)), error = function(e) rep(0.0, ncol(X_a)))
    w_std <- rep(0.0, length(valid_cols))
    w_std[active_loc] <- w_a
    new_active <- which(abs(w_std) >= lambda)
    if (identical(new_active, active_loc)) break
    active_loc <- new_active
  }

  # Unbiased OLS refit on active support
  coefs_full <- rep(0.0, p)
  names(coefs_full) <- colnames(Phi)
  active_orig <- valid_cols[active_loc]
  if (length(active_orig) > 0L) {
    X_act <- cbind(1.0, Phi[, active_orig, drop = FALSE])
    fit_ols <- lm.fit(x = X_act, y = y)
    b_ols <- fit_ols$coefficients
    b_ols[is.na(b_ols)] <- 0.0
    intercept <- b_ols[1]
    coefs_full[active_orig] <- b_ols[-1]
  } else {
    intercept <- y_mean
  }

  list(intercept = intercept, coefs = coefs_full, active = active_orig, lambda = lambda)
}

#' Cross-Validated + BIC-Regularized STLSQ Selection
cv_stlsq_fit <- function(Phi, y, lambda_grid = exp(seq(log(0.005), log(0.60), length.out = 20L)), k_folds = 5L) {
  n <- nrow(Phi)
  fold_ids <- rep(seq_len(k_folds), length.out = n)
  cv_scores <- numeric(length(lambda_grid))

  for (idx_l in seq_along(lambda_grid)) {
    lam <- lambda_grid[idx_l]
    mse_folds <- numeric(k_folds)
    k_terms <- 0.0
    for (f in seq_len(k_folds)) {
      tr <- which(fold_ids != f)
      va <- which(fold_ids == f)
      fit_f <- stlsq_fit_single(Phi[tr, , drop = FALSE], y[tr], lambda = lam)
      pred_va <- fit_f$intercept + as.numeric(Phi[va, , drop = FALSE] %*% fit_f$coefs)
      mse_folds[f] <- mean((y[va] - pred_va)^2)
      k_terms <- k_terms + length(fit_f$active) / k_folds
    }
    # Combine CV MSE with BIC sparsity penalty to prevent overfitting under noise
    cv_mse <- mean(mse_folds)
    cv_scores[idx_l] <- n * log(max(cv_mse, 1e-12)) + log(n) * k_terms
  }

  best_lam <- lambda_grid[which.min(cv_scores)]
  stlsq_fit_single(Phi, y, lambda = best_lam)
}

#' Build Rational / Implicit Dictionary for SINDy-PI (Kaheman et al. 2020)
build_sindy_pi_rational_library <- function(X) {
  X <- as.matrix(X)
  col_names <- colnames(X)
  if (is.null(col_names)) {
    col_names <- paste0("x", seq_len(ncol(X)))
    colnames(X) <- col_names
  }
  m <- ncol(X)
  Phi_poly <- build_poly_library(X, max_deg = 3L)

  rat_list <- list()
  rat_names <- character(0)
  add_rat <- function(vec, nm) {
    if (!nm %in% c(colnames(Phi_poly), rat_names)) {
      vec[!is.finite(vec)] <- 0.0
      rat_list[[length(rat_list) + 1L]] <<- as.numeric(vec)
      rat_names <<- c(rat_names, nm)
    }
  }

  for (i in seq_len(m)) {
    xi <- X[, i]
    ni <- col_names[i]
    add_rat(1.0 / (1.0 + abs(xi)), paste0("1/(1+", ni, ")"))
    add_rat(xi / (1.0 + abs(xi)), paste0(ni, "/(1+", ni, ")"))
    add_rat(1.0 / (1.0 + xi^2), paste0("1/(1+", ni, "^2)"))
    add_rat((xi^2) / (1.0 + xi^2), paste0(ni, "^2/(1+", ni, "^2)"))
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      xj <- X[, j]
      nj <- col_names[j]
      if (i != j) {
        add_rat((xi * xj) / (1.0 + abs(xi)), paste0(ni, "*", nj, "/(1+", ni, ")"))
        add_rat((xi * (xj^2)) / (1.0 + xj^2), paste0(ni, "*", nj, "^2/(1+", nj, "^2)"))
      }
    }
  }

  Phi_rat <- do.call(cbind, rat_list)
  colnames(Phi_rat) <- rat_names
  cbind(Phi_poly, Phi_rat)
}

#' Unified Runner for the 5 SINDy Baselines
run_sindy_baseline <- function(
  method_id = c(
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle",
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
    "UQ_SINDy_Oracle",
    "UQ_SINDy_Poly",
    "SINDy_PI_Rational"
  ),
  task_data,
  n_boot = 80L
) {
  method_id <- match.arg(method_id)
  X_tr_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_train)
  Y_tr <- as.matrix(task_data$Y_train)
  X_in_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_in)
  X_ood_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_ood)
  m <- ncol(Y_tr)
  extra_oracle <- if (!is.null(task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis)) task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis else character(0)

  build_Phi_fn <- function(X_mat) {
    if (method_id %in% c("SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "UQ_SINDy_Oracle")) {
      build_oracle_library(X_mat, extra_atoms = extra_oracle, max_deg = 3L)
    } else if (method_id %in% c("SINDy_STLSQ_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Poly")) {
      build_poly_library(X_mat, max_deg = 3L)
    } else if (method_id == "SINDy_PI_Rational") {
      build_sindy_pi_rational_library(X_mat)
    }
  }

  Phi_tr <- build_Phi_fn(X_tr_raw)
  Phi_in <- build_Phi_fn(X_in_raw)
  Phi_ood <- build_Phi_fn(X_ood_raw)
  cand_names <- colnames(Phi_tr)
  p <- ncol(Phi_tr)

  discovered <- vector("list", m)
  pip_list <- vector("list", m)
  eq_models <- vector("list", m)

  pred_train <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_tr), ncol = m)
  pred_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  pred_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)
  lower_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  upper_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  lower_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)
  upper_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    yj <- Y_tr[, j]

    if (method_id %in% c("SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly", "SINDy_PI_Rational")) {
      fit_j <- cv_stlsq_fit(Phi_tr, yj)
      active_names <- cand_names[fit_j$active]
      discovered[[j]] <- extract_canonical_atoms(active_names)
      pip_vec <- as.numeric(cand_names %in% active_names)
      names(pip_vec) <- cand_names
      pip_list[[j]] <- pip_vec

      pred_train[, j] <- fit_j$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_tr %*% fit_j$coefs)
      pred_in[, j] <- fit_j$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_in %*% fit_j$coefs)
      pred_ood[, j] <- fit_j$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_ood %*% fit_j$coefs)

      # Point-estimate methods have no calibrated Bayesian credible intervals (NA or residual plug-in)
      lower_in[, j] <- NA_real_
      upper_in[, j] <- NA_real_
      lower_ood[, j] <- NA_real_
      upper_ood[, j] <- NA_real_
      eq_models[[j]] <- list(intercept = fit_j$intercept, coefs = fit_j$coefs)
    } else {
      # UQ_SINDy (Ensemble / Spike-and-Slab Bagging SINDy: Fasel et al. 2022 / Hirsh et al. 2022)
      fit_base <- cv_stlsq_fit(Phi_tr, yj)
      n_obs <- nrow(Phi_tr)
      boot_coefs <- matrix(0.0, nrow = n_boot, ncol = p)
      boot_inter <- numeric(n_boot)
      boot_active <- matrix(0.0, nrow = n_boot, ncol = p)

      # Use CV-selected standardized threshold directly (fixing prior unstandardized OLS scale mismatch;
      # set SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH=1 only if reproducing archived v1 bagging runs)
      if (Sys.getenv("SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH", "0") == "1") {
        lam_base <- max(0.03, median(abs(fit_base$coefs[fit_base$active]), na.rm = TRUE) * 0.25)
        if (!is.finite(lam_base)) lam_base <- 0.05
      } else {
        lam_base <- max(0.01, as.numeric(fit_base$lambda))
        if (!is.finite(lam_base)) lam_base <- 0.05
      }

      for (b in seq_len(n_boot)) {
        idx_b <- sample.int(n_obs, size = floor(0.85 * n_obs), replace = TRUE)
        fb <- stlsq_fit_single(Phi_tr[idx_b, , drop = FALSE], yj[idx_b], lambda = lam_base)
        boot_coefs[b, ] <- fb$coefs
        boot_inter[b] <- fb$intercept
        boot_active[b, fb$active] <- 1.0
      }

      pips <- colMeans(boot_active)
      names(pips) <- cand_names
      pip_list[[j]] <- pips

      # Median Probability Model (PIP > 0.5)
      mpm_idx <- which(pips > 0.5)
      discovered[[j]] <- extract_canonical_atoms(cand_names[mpm_idx])

      if (length(mpm_idx) > 0L) {
        X_act <- cbind(1.0, Phi_tr[, mpm_idx, drop = FALSE])
        ols <- lm.fit(x = X_act, y = yj)
        b_ols <- ols$coefficients
        b_ols[is.na(b_ols)] <- 0.0
        inter_mpm <- b_ols[1]
        coefs_mpm <- rep(0.0, p)
        names(coefs_mpm) <- cand_names
        coefs_mpm[mpm_idx] <- b_ols[-1]
        sigma_res <- sqrt(mean(ols$residuals^2))
      } else {
        inter_mpm <- mean(yj)
        coefs_mpm <- setNames(rep(0.0, p), cand_names)
        sigma_res <- sd(yj)
      }

      pred_train[, j] <- inter_mpm + as.numeric(Phi_tr %*% coefs_mpm)
      pred_in[, j] <- inter_mpm + as.numeric(Phi_in %*% coefs_mpm)
      pred_ood[, j] <- inter_mpm + as.numeric(Phi_ood %*% coefs_mpm)

      # Ensemble predictive quantiles + residual standard deviation
      ens_in <- sweep(Phi_in %*% t(boot_coefs), 2, boot_inter, "+")
      ens_ood <- sweep(Phi_ood %*% t(boot_coefs), 2, boot_inter, "+")
      sd_ens_in <- apply(ens_in, 1, sd)
      sd_ens_ood <- apply(ens_ood, 1, sd)
      tot_sd_in <- sqrt(sd_ens_in^2 + sigma_res^2)
      tot_sd_ood <- sqrt(sd_ens_ood^2 + sigma_res^2)

      lower_in[, j] <- pred_in[, j] - 1.96 * tot_sd_in
      upper_in[, j] <- pred_in[, j] + 1.96 * tot_sd_in
      lower_ood[, j] <- pred_ood[, j] - 1.96 * tot_sd_ood
      upper_ood[, j] <- pred_ood[, j] + 1.96 * tot_sd_ood

      eq_models[[j]] <- list(intercept = inter_mpm, coefs = coefs_mpm)
    }
  }

  predict_rhs_fn <- function(X_state_mat) {
    Phi_new <- build_Phi_fn(X_state_mat)
    out_d <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_new), ncol = m)
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      out_d[, j] <- eq_models[[j]]$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_new %*% eq_models[[j]]$coefs)
    }
    out_d
  }

  list(
    method_id = method_id,
    discovered = discovered,
    pip_list = pip_list,
    pred_train = pred_train,
    pred_in = pred_in,
    pred_ood = pred_ood,
    lower_in = lower_in,
    upper_in = upper_in,
    lower_ood = lower_ood,
    upper_ood = upper_ood,
    predict_rhs_fn = predict_rhs_fn
  )
}

#' True Bayesian Spike-and-Slab / BIC Model Averaging SINDy Baseline via BAS::bas.lm
run_uq_sindy_bayes <- function(
    task_data,
    dict_type = c("poly", "oracle"),
    mcmc_iters = 5000L) {
  dict_type <- match.arg(dict_type)
  method_id <- if (dict_type == "oracle") "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Oracle" else "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Poly"

  X_tr_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_train)
  Y_tr <- as.matrix(task_data$Y_train)
  X_in_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_in)
  X_ood_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_ood)
  m <- ncol(Y_tr)
  extra_oracle <- if (!is.null(task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis)) task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis else character(0)

  build_Phi_fn <- function(X_mat) {
    if (dict_type == "oracle") {
      build_oracle_library(X_mat, extra_atoms = extra_oracle, max_deg = 3L)
    } else {
      build_poly_library(X_mat, max_deg = 3L)
    }
  }

  Phi_tr <- build_Phi_fn(X_tr_raw)
  Phi_in <- build_Phi_fn(X_in_raw)
  Phi_ood <- build_Phi_fn(X_ood_raw)
  cand_names <- colnames(Phi_tr)
  p <- ncol(Phi_tr)
  safe_names <- paste0("b", seq_len(p))

  df_tr <- as.data.frame(Phi_tr)
  colnames(df_tr) <- safe_names
  df_in <- as.data.frame(Phi_in)
  colnames(df_in) <- safe_names
  df_ood <- as.data.frame(Phi_ood)
  colnames(df_ood) <- safe_names

  discovered <- vector("list", m)
  pip_list <- vector("list", m)
  eq_models <- vector("list", m)

  pred_train <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_tr), ncol = m)
  pred_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  pred_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)
  lower_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  upper_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  lower_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)
  upper_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    yj <- Y_tr[, j]
    dat_j <- cbind(y_resp = yj, df_tr)
    bas_method <- if (p <= 18L) "deterministic" else "MCMC"
    fit_bas <- suppressWarnings(BAS::bas.lm(
      y_resp ~ .,
      data = dat_j,
      prior = "BIC",
      modelprior = BAS::uniform(),
      method = bas_method,
      MCMC.iterations = as.integer(mcmc_iters),
      include.always = ~1
    ))

    pips <- as.numeric(fit_bas$probne0[-1])
    pips[!is.finite(pips)] <- 0.0
    names(pips) <- cand_names
    pip_list[[j]] <- pips

    mpm_idx <- which(pips > 0.5)
    discovered[[j]] <- extract_canonical_atoms(cand_names[mpm_idx])

    pr_tr <- predict(fit_bas, newdata = df_tr, estimator = "BMA")
    pr_in <- predict(fit_bas, newdata = df_in, estimator = "BMA", se.fit = TRUE)
    pr_ood <- predict(fit_bas, newdata = df_ood, estimator = "BMA", se.fit = TRUE)

    pred_train[, j] <- as.numeric(pr_tr$fit)
    pred_in[, j] <- as.numeric(pr_in$fit)
    pred_ood[, j] <- as.numeric(pr_ood$fit)

    # Predictive intervals combining posterior BMA parameter uncertainty + residual SD
    n_obs <- nrow(Phi_tr)
    df_res <- max(1L, n_obs - length(mpm_idx) - 1L)
    t_crit <- stats::qt(0.975, df = df_res)
    sigma_res <- sqrt(mean((yj - pred_train[, j])^2) * n_obs / df_res)
    se_in_pred <- sqrt(as.numeric(pr_in$se.bma.pred)^2)
    se_ood_pred <- sqrt(as.numeric(pr_ood$se.bma.pred)^2)
    if (!all(is.finite(se_in_pred))) se_in_pred <- rep(sigma_res, nrow(Phi_in))
    if (!all(is.finite(se_ood_pred))) se_ood_pred <- rep(sigma_res, nrow(Phi_ood))

    lower_in[, j] <- pred_in[, j] - t_crit * se_in_pred
    upper_in[, j] <- pred_in[, j] + t_crit * se_in_pred
    lower_ood[, j] <- pred_ood[, j] - t_crit * se_ood_pred
    upper_ood[, j] <- pred_ood[, j] + t_crit * se_ood_pred

    # OLS refit on MPM support for clean autonomous rollout RHS
    if (length(mpm_idx) > 0L) {
      ols_j <- lm.fit(x = cbind(1.0, Phi_tr[, mpm_idx, drop = FALSE]), y = yj)
      b_j <- ols_j$coefficients
      b_j[is.na(b_j)] <- 0.0
      inter_j <- b_j[1]
      coefs_j <- setNames(rep(0.0, p), cand_names)
      coefs_j[mpm_idx] <- b_j[-1]
    } else {
      inter_j <- mean(yj)
      coefs_j <- setNames(rep(0.0, p), cand_names)
    }
    eq_models[[j]] <- list(intercept = inter_j, coefs = coefs_j)
  }

  predict_rhs_fn <- function(X_state_mat) {
    Phi_new <- build_Phi_fn(X_state_mat)
    out_d <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_new), ncol = m)
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      out_d[, j] <- eq_models[[j]]$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_new %*% eq_models[[j]]$coefs)
    }
    out_d
  }

  list(
    method_id = method_id,
    engine = "r_bas_bayes",
    discovered = discovered,
    pip_list = pip_list,
    pred_train = pred_train,
    pred_in = pred_in,
    pred_ood = pred_ood,
    lower_in = lower_in,
    upper_in = upper_in,
    lower_ood = lower_ood,
    upper_ood = upper_ood,
    predict_rhs_fn = predict_rhs_fn
  )
}

#' Weak-Form Integral SINDy Baseline (WSINDy; Messenger & Bortz 2021)
#' Eliminates numerical state differentiation via integration by parts against compact C^p test functions.
run_wsindy_baseline <- function(
    task_data,
    dict_type = c("poly", "oracle"),
    p_smooth = 4L,
    n_test_funcs = 60L) {
  dict_type <- match.arg(dict_type)
  method_id <- if (dict_type == "oracle") "WSINDy_Oracle" else "WSINDy_Poly"

  # Require time-ordered sequential observations for integral inner products
  X_seq <- if (!is.null(task_data$X_seq)) as.matrix(task_data$X_seq) else as.matrix(task_data$X_train)
  t_seq <- if (!is.null(task_data$t_seq)) as.numeric(task_data$t_seq) else if (!is.null(task_data$t_train)) as.numeric(task_data$t_train) else NULL
  X_tr_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_train)
  Y_tr <- as.matrix(task_data$Y_train)
  X_in_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_in)
  X_ood_raw <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_ood)
  m <- ncol(Y_tr)
  extra_oracle <- if (!is.null(task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis)) task_data$system$oracle_extra_basis else character(0)

  build_Phi_fn <- function(X_mat) {
    if (dict_type == "oracle") {
      build_oracle_library(X_mat, extra_atoms = extra_oracle, max_deg = 3L)
    } else {
      build_poly_library(X_mat, max_deg = 3L)
    }
  }

  Phi_tr <- build_Phi_fn(X_tr_raw)
  Phi_in <- build_Phi_fn(X_in_raw)
  Phi_ood <- build_Phi_fn(X_ood_raw)
  cand_names <- colnames(Phi_tr)
  p <- ncol(Phi_tr)

  # Build weak-form test matrix Psi and dPsi if time vector is available
  use_weak <- !is.null(t_seq) && length(t_seq) == nrow(X_seq) && length(t_seq) >= 25L
  if (use_weak) {
    ord_t <- order(t_seq)
    t_seq <- t_seq[ord_t]
    X_seq <- X_seq[ord_t, , drop = FALSE]
    Phi_seq <- build_Phi_fn(X_seq)
    n_seq <- length(t_seq)
    t_span <- t_seq[n_seq] - t_seq[1]
    dt_med <- stats::median(diff(t_seq))
    # Adaptive support half-width: local to dynamical timescale (~6-10 steps, capped at 1.0 time units unless t_span is large & sparse)
    hw <- max(6.0 * dt_med, min(1.0, 0.025 * t_span))
    centers <- seq(t_seq[1] + hw, t_seq[n_seq] - hw, length.out = min(n_test_funcs, max(20L, floor(n_seq / 3L))))

    # Trapezoidal quadrature weights
    dt_v <- diff(t_seq)
    wq <- numeric(n_seq)
    wq[1] <- 0.5 * dt_v[1]
    wq[n_seq] <- 0.5 * dt_v[n_seq - 1L]
    if (n_seq > 2L) wq[2:(n_seq - 1L)] <- 0.5 * (dt_v[1:(n_seq - 2L)] + dt_v[2:(n_seq - 1L)])

    K_q <- length(centers)
    Psi <- matrix(0.0, nrow = K_q, ncol = n_seq)
    dPsi <- matrix(0.0, nrow = K_q, ncol = n_seq)
    keep_q <- logical(K_q)
    for (q in seq_len(K_q)) {
      u <- (t_seq - centers[q]) / hw
      supp <- which(abs(u) < 1.0)
      if (length(supp) >= 5L) {
        us <- u[supp]
        psi_val <- (1.0 - us^2)^p_smooth
        dpsi_val <- (-2.0 * p_smooth * us / hw) * (1.0 - us^2)^(p_smooth - 1L)
        norm_q <- sum(wq[supp] * psi_val)
        if (norm_q > 1e-12) {
          Psi[q, supp] <- (psi_val * wq[supp]) / norm_q
          dPsi[q, supp] <- (dpsi_val * wq[supp]) / norm_q
          keep_q[q] <- TRUE
        }
      }
    }
    if (sum(keep_q) >= 15L) {
      G_mat <- Psi[keep_q, , drop = FALSE] %*% Phi_seq
      colnames(G_mat) <- cand_names
      B_mat <- -dPsi[keep_q, , drop = FALSE] %*% X_seq[, seq_len(m), drop = FALSE]
    } else {
      use_weak <- FALSE
    }
  }

  discovered <- vector("list", m)
  pip_list <- vector("list", m)
  eq_models <- vector("list", m)
  pred_train <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_tr), ncol = m)
  pred_in <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  pred_ood <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)
  na_in <- matrix(NA_real_, nrow = nrow(Phi_in), ncol = m)
  na_ood <- matrix(NA_real_, nrow = nrow(Phi_ood), ncol = m)

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    if (use_weak) {
      fit_j <- cv_stlsq_fit(G_mat, B_mat[, j])
    } else {
      fit_j <- cv_stlsq_fit(Phi_tr, Y_tr[, j])
    }
    active_names <- cand_names[fit_j$active]
    discovered[[j]] <- extract_canonical_atoms(active_names)
    pip_vec <- as.numeric(cand_names %in% active_names)
    names(pip_vec) <- cand_names
    pip_list[[j]] <- pip_vec

    pred_train[, j] <- fit_j$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_tr %*% fit_j$coefs)
    pred_in[, j] <- fit_j$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_in %*% fit_j$coefs)
    pred_ood[, j] <- fit_j$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_ood %*% fit_j$coefs)
    eq_models[[j]] <- list(intercept = fit_j$intercept, coefs = fit_j$coefs)
  }

  predict_rhs_fn <- function(X_state_mat) {
    Phi_new <- build_Phi_fn(X_state_mat)
    out_d <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(Phi_new), ncol = m)
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      out_d[, j] <- eq_models[[j]]$intercept + as.numeric(Phi_new %*% eq_models[[j]]$coefs)
    }
    out_d
  }

  list(
    method_id = method_id,
    engine = "r_wsindy",
    discovered = discovered,
    pip_list = pip_list,
    pred_train = pred_train,
    pred_in = pred_in,
    pred_ood = pred_ood,
    lower_in = na_in,
    upper_in = na_in,
    lower_ood = na_ood,
    upper_ood = na_ood,
    predict_rhs_fn = predict_rhs_fn
  )
}


=== FILE: R/04b_sr_baselines.R ===
# ==============================================================================
# R/04b_sr_baselines.R
# Symbolic Regression Baselines (SR_PySR, SR_Operon) via Python Bridge
# ==============================================================================

if (!exists("extract_canonical_atoms", mode = "function")) {
  if (file.exists("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) {
    source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
  } else if (file.exists("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
}

#' Locate Python interpreter in .venv or system PATH (preserving venv symlink)
find_python_bin <- function() {
  candidates <- c(
    ".venv/bin/python",
    "../../.venv/bin/python",
    Sys.which("python3"),
    Sys.which("python")
  )
  for (cand in candidates) {
    if (nzchar(cand) && file.exists(cand)) {
      return(file.path(normalizePath(dirname(cand), mustWork = TRUE), basename(cand)))
    }
  }
  stop("No Python interpreter found for Symbolic Regression bridge.")
}

#' Locate python/run_sr_pysindy.py script
find_sr_script <- function() {
  candidates <- c("python/run_sr_pysindy.py", "../../python/run_sr_pysindy.py")
  for (cand in candidates) {
    if (file.exists(cand)) {
      return(normalizePath(cand))
    }
  }
  stop("Cannot find python/run_sr_pysindy.py")
}

#' Run Symbolic Regression Baseline (SR_PySR or SR_Operon)
run_sr_baseline <- function(
    method_id = c("SR_PySR", "SR_Operon"),
    task_data,
    strict_native = (Sys.getenv("SR_STRICT_NATIVE", "0") == "1")) {
  method_id <- match.arg(method_id)
  X_tr <- as.matrix(task_data$X_train)
  Y_tr <- as.matrix(task_data$Y_train)
  X_in <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_in)
  X_ood <- as.matrix(task_data$X_test_ood)
  m <- ncol(Y_tr)
  col_names <- colnames(X_tr)
  if (is.null(col_names)) col_names <- paste0("x", seq_len(ncol(X_tr)))

  py_bin <- find_python_bin()
  sr_script <- find_sr_script()

  tmp_in <- tempfile(pattern = "sr_in_", fileext = ".json")
  tmp_out <- tempfile(pattern = "sr_out_", fileext = ".json")
  on.exit(unlink(c(tmp_in, tmp_out)), add = TRUE)

  # Write input JSON using jsonlite (installed with R packages)
  payload <- list(
    method_id = method_id,
    seed = as.integer(if (!is.null(task_data$seed)) task_data$seed else 42L),
    col_names = as.list(col_names),
    X_train = X_tr,
    Y_train = Y_tr,
    X_test_in = X_in,
    X_test_ood = X_ood
  )
  jsonlite::write_json(payload, tmp_in, auto_unbox = TRUE, digits = 10)

  cli_args <- c(shQuote(sr_script), "--input", shQuote(tmp_in), "--output", shQuote(tmp_out))
  if (isTRUE(strict_native)) cli_args <- c(cli_args, "--strict_native")
  cmd_status <- system2(
    py_bin,
    args = cli_args,
    stdout = FALSE,
    stderr = FALSE
  )
  if (cmd_status != 0L || !file.exists(tmp_out)) {
    stop("Python SR bridge failed for method: ", method_id)
  }

  res_py <- jsonlite::read_json(tmp_out, simplifyVector = TRUE)

  discovered <- vector("list", m)
  pip_list <- vector("list", m)
  eq_models <- vector("list", m)

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    raw_atoms <- if (is.list(res_py$discovered) && length(res_py$discovered) >= j) {
      as.character(res_py$discovered[[j]])
    } else if (is.matrix(res_py$discovered) && nrow(res_py$discovered) >= j && ncol(res_py$discovered) > 0L) {
      as.character(res_py$discovered[j, ])
    } else {
      character(0)
    }
    canon_j <- extract_canonical_atoms(raw_atoms)
    discovered[[j]] <- canon_j
    # Symbolic regression yields point-estimate Pareto trees without calibrated PIPs
    pip_list[j] <- list(NULL)

    inter_j <- as.numeric(res_py$intercepts[j])
    if (is.data.frame(res_py$coefs)) {
      if (ncol(res_py$coefs) > 0L && nrow(res_py$coefs) >= j) {
        coef_vec <- as.numeric(res_py$coefs[j, ])
        names(coef_vec) <- colnames(res_py$coefs)
        coef_vec <- coef_vec[!is.na(coef_vec) & abs(coef_vec) > 0]
      } else {
        coef_vec <- numeric(0)
      }
    } else if (is.list(res_py$coefs) && length(res_py$coefs) >= j && is.list(res_py$coefs[[j]]) && length(res_py$coefs[[j]]) > 0L) {
      coef_vec <- unlist(res_py$coefs[[j]])
    } else {
      coef_vec <- numeric(0)
    }
    sym_raw <- if (!is.null(res_py$sympy_exprs) && length(res_py$sympy_exprs) >= j) {
      as.character(res_py$sympy_exprs[[j]])
    } else ""
    sym_r <- gsub("\\*\\*", "^", sym_raw)
    sym_r <- gsub("\\bsquare\\(", "(", sym_r)
    eq_models[[j]] <- list(intercept = inter_j, coefs = coef_vec, sympy_expr = sym_r)
  }

  pred_train <- as.matrix(res_py$pred_train)
  pred_in <- as.matrix(res_py$pred_in)
  pred_ood <- as.matrix(res_py$pred_ood)

  # Symbolic regression does not provide Bayesian credible intervals
  na_in <- matrix(NA_real_, nrow = nrow(X_in), ncol = m)
  na_ood <- matrix(NA_real_, nrow = nrow(X_ood), ncol = m)

  predict_rhs_fn <- function(X_state_mat) {
    X_df_new <- as.data.frame(X_state_mat)
    colnames(X_df_new) <- col_names
    out_d <- matrix(0.0, nrow = nrow(X_df_new), ncol = m)
    for (j in seq_len(m)) {
      mod_j <- eq_models[[j]]
      used_sympy <- FALSE
      if (!is.null(mod_j$sympy_expr) && nzchar(mod_j$sympy_expr)) {
        val_sym <- evaluate_basis_atom(mod_j$sympy_expr, X_df_new)
        if (length(val_sym) == nrow(X_df_new) && all(is.finite(val_sym))) {
          # Guard against parse failure returning zero vector when non-zero linear fit exists
          parsed_ok <- !is.null(tryCatch(parse(text = mod_j$sympy_expr), error = function(e) NULL))
          if (parsed_ok) {
            out_d[, j] <- val_sym
            used_sympy <- TRUE
          }
        }
      }
      if (!used_sympy) {
        inter_val <- if (is.finite(mod_j$intercept)) mod_j$intercept else 0.0
        val <- rep(inter_val, nrow(X_df_new))
        if (length(mod_j$coefs) > 0L) {
          for (nm in names(mod_j$coefs)) {
            val <- val + mod_j$coefs[[nm]] * evaluate_basis_atom(nm, X_df_new)
          }
        }
        out_d[, j] <- val
      }
    }
    out_d
  }

  list(
    method_id = method_id,
    engine = if (!is.null(res_py$engine)) as.character(res_py$engine)[1L] else "unknown",
    discovered = discovered,
    pip_list = pip_list,
    pred_train = pred_train,
    pred_in = pred_in,
    pred_ood = pred_ood,
    lower_in = na_in,
    upper_in = na_in,
    lower_ood = na_ood,
    upper_ood = na_ood,
    predict_rhs_fn = predict_rhs_fn
  )
}


=== FILE: R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R ===
# ==============================================================================
# R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R
# Symbolic Canonicalization & Algebraic Equivalence Parser for FBMS, SINDy, & SR
# ==============================================================================

#' Normalize variable names and FBMS / SR / PySINDy function aliases
preprocess_math_string <- function(expr_str) {
  if (is.null(expr_str) || is.na(expr_str)) return("")
  s <- trimws(as.character(expr_str))
  if (!nzchar(s)) return("")

  # Strip outer I(...) wrapper if present
  s <- gsub("^I\\((.*)\\)$", "\\1", s)
  # Normalize x.1 -> x1, x_1 -> x1
  s <- gsub("x\\.([0-9]+)", "x\\1", s)
  s <- gsub("x_([0-9]+)", "x\\1", s)
  # Python power ** to ^
  s <- gsub("\\*\\*", "^", s)
  # Normalize function aliases (note: sin_deg/cos_deg compute degrees, NOT radians, so never alias them to sin/cos)
  s <- gsub("\\bsin_rad\\b", "sin", s)
  s <- gsub("\\bcos_rad\\b", "cos", s)
  s <- gsub("\\bsqroot\\b", "sqrt", s)
  s <- gsub("\\bp05\\b", "sqrt", s)
  s <- gsub("\\bsquare\\b", "p2", s)
  s <- gsub("\\bcube\\b", "p3", s)

  # PySINDy space-separated monomials e.g. "x1 x2" or "x1^2 x2"
  s <- gsub("([a-zA-Z0-9_\\)\\^])\\s+([a-zA-Z])", "\\1*\\2", s)
  # Remove all whitespace
  s <- gsub("\\s+", "", s)
  s
}

#' Convert an R language AST node into a list of additive monomials,
#' where each monomial is list(coef = numeric, num = character_vec, den = character_vec).
#' Bounded by `depth` and `max_terms` to prevent combinatorial explosion on deeply nested features.
ast_to_monomials <- function(node, depth = 0L, max_terms = 128L) {
  if (is.numeric(node)) {
    return(list(list(coef = as.numeric(node), num = character(0), den = character(0))))
  }
  if (is.symbol(node)) {
    nm <- as.character(node)
    if (nm == "pi") {
      return(list(list(coef = pi, num = character(0), den = character(0))))
    }
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = nm, den = character(0))))
  }
  if (!is.call(node)) {
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = deparse(node), den = character(0))))
  }
  if (depth > 14L) {
    expr_txt <- paste(deparse(node, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = "")
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = expr_txt, den = character(0))))
  }

  op <- as.character(node[[1]])
  args <- as.list(node)[-1]

  # Parentheses
  if (op == "(") {
    return(ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms))
  }

  # Unary plus / minus
  if (op == "+" && length(args) == 1L) {
    return(ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms))
  }
  if (op == "-" && length(args) == 1L) {
    m1 <- ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)
    return(lapply(m1, function(m) { m$coef <- -m$coef; m }))
  }

  # Binary addition / subtraction
  if (op == "+" && length(args) == 2L) {
    combined <- c(
      ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms),
      ast_to_monomials(args[[2]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)
    )
    if (length(combined) > max_terms) {
      expr_txt <- paste(deparse(node, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = "")
      return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = expr_txt, den = character(0))))
    }
    return(combined)
  }
  if (op == "-" && length(args) == 2L) {
    m2 <- lapply(ast_to_monomials(args[[2]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms), function(m) { m$coef <- -m$coef; m })
    combined <- c(ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms), m2)
    if (length(combined) > max_terms) {
      expr_txt <- paste(deparse(node, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = "")
      return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = expr_txt, den = character(0))))
    }
    return(combined)
  }

  # Multiplication (distributes over sums, guarded against combinatorial blowup)
  if (op == "*" && length(args) == 2L) {
    left_list <- ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)
    right_list <- ast_to_monomials(args[[2]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)
    n_prod <- as.numeric(length(left_list)) * as.numeric(length(right_list))
    if (n_prod > max_terms) {
      expr_txt <- paste(deparse(node, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = "")
      return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = expr_txt, den = character(0))))
    }
    out <- vector("list", as.integer(n_prod))
    idx_out <- 0L
    for (ml in left_list) {
      for (mr in right_list) {
        idx_out <- idx_out + 1L
        out[[idx_out]] <- list(
          coef = ml$coef * mr$coef,
          num = c(ml$num, mr$num),
          den = c(ml$den, mr$den)
        )
      }
    }
    return(out)
  }

  # Division
  if (op == "/" && length(args) == 2L) {
    left_list <- ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)
    right_node <- args[[2]]
    # Check if denominator is purely numeric
    if (is.numeric(right_node)) {
      d_val <- as.numeric(right_node)
      return(lapply(left_list, function(ml) { ml$coef <- ml$coef / d_val; ml }))
    }
    den_str <- canonicalize_denominator(right_node)
    return(lapply(left_list, function(ml) {
      ml$den <- c(ml$den, den_str)
      ml
    }))
  }

  # Exponentiation (unwrap parenthesized / unary constant numeric exponents such as x1^(-1), x1^(0.5))
  if (op == "^" && length(args) == 2L) {
    base_node <- args[[1]]
    exp_node <- args[[2]]
    exp_val <- if (is.numeric(exp_node)) {
      as.numeric(exp_node)
    } else if (length(all.vars(exp_node)) == 0L) {
      tryCatch(as.numeric(eval(exp_node, envir = baseenv())), error = function(e) NA_real_)
    } else {
      NA_real_
    }
    if (is.finite(exp_val)) {
      p <- exp_val
      if (p == 0) {
        return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = character(0))))
      }
      if (p == 0.5) {
        arg_c <- canonicalize_single_expression(paste(deparse(base_node, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = ""))
        return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = paste0("sqrt(", arg_c, ")"), den = character(0))))
      }
      if (p < 0 && abs(p) == round(abs(p)) && abs(p) <= 6) {
        den_s <- canonicalize_denominator(call("^", base_node, abs(p)))
        return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = den_s)))
      }
      if (p == round(p) && p > 0 && p <= 6) {
        base_list <- ast_to_monomials(base_node, depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)
        if ((as.numeric(length(base_list)) ^ p) > max_terms) {
          expr_txt <- paste(deparse(node, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = "")
          return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = expr_txt, den = character(0))))
        }
        res <- base_list
        if (p >= 2) {
          for (k in 2:p) {
            n_next <- length(res) * length(base_list)
            next_res <- vector("list", n_next)
            idx_n <- 0L
            for (ml in res) {
              for (mr in base_list) {
                idx_n <- idx_n + 1L
                next_res[[idx_n]] <- list(
                  coef = ml$coef * mr$coef,
                  num = c(ml$num, mr$num),
                  den = c(ml$den, mr$den)
                )
              }
            }
            res <- next_res
          }
        }
        return(res)
      }
    }
  }

  # FBMS custom operator transforms
  if (op == "p2" && length(args) == 1L) {
    return(ast_to_monomials(call("^", args[[1]], 2), depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms))
  }
  if (op == "p3" && length(args) == 1L) {
    return(ast_to_monomials(call("^", args[[1]], 3), depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms))
  }
  if (op == "p0" && length(args) == 1L) {
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = character(0))))
  }
  if (op == "pm1" && length(args) == 1L) {
    den_s <- canonicalize_denominator(args[[1]])
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = den_s)))
  }
  if (op == "pm2" && length(args) == 1L) {
    den_s <- canonicalize_denominator(call("^", args[[1]], 2))
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = den_s)))
  }
  if (op == "inv_pos" && length(args) == 1L) {
    arg_s <- canonicalize_single_expression(paste(deparse(args[[1]], width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = ""))
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = paste0("1+", arg_s))))
  }
  if (op == "inv_sq" && length(args) == 1L) {
    sq_m <- ast_to_monomials(call("^", args[[1]], 2), depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)[[1]]
    sq_s <- format_monomial_atom(sq_m)
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = character(0), den = paste0("1+", sq_s))))
  }
  if (op == "hill_2" && length(args) == 1L) {
    sq_m <- ast_to_monomials(call("^", args[[1]], 2), depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms)[[1]]
    sq_s <- format_monomial_atom(sq_m)
    return(list(list(coef = 1.0, num = sq_m$num, den = paste0("1+", sq_s))))
  }
  if (op == "abs" && length(args) == 1L) {
    return(ast_to_monomials(args[[1]], depth = depth + 1L, max_terms = max_terms))
  }

  # General unary functions: sin, cos, exp, sqrt, sigmoid, etc.
  arg_str <- paste(deparse(args[[1]]), collapse = "")
  arg_canon <- canonicalize_single_expression(arg_str)
  fn_token <- paste0(op, "(", arg_canon, ")")
  list(list(coef = 1.0, num = fn_token, den = character(0)))
}

#' Format denominator node into canonical string such as "1+x1" or "1+x3^2"
canonicalize_denominator <- function(node) {
  mons <- ast_to_monomials(node)
  atoms <- character(0)
  has_one <- FALSE
  for (m in mons) {
    a <- format_monomial_atom(m)
    if (a == "1") {
      has_one <- TRUE
    } else if (nzchar(a)) {
      atoms <- c(atoms, a)
    }
  }
  atoms <- sort(unique(atoms))
  if (has_one) {
    return(paste(c("1", atoms), collapse = "+"))
  }
  if (length(atoms) == 0L) return("1")
  paste(atoms, collapse = "+")
}

#' Format a single monomial (num, den) into its canonical basis atom string
format_monomial_atom <- function(m) {
  if (abs(m$coef) < 1e-12) return("")

  # Expand any factor of the form v^k into k copies of v
  expanded_num <- character(0)
  for (f in m$num) {
    if (grepl("^([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\\^([0-9]+)$", f)) {
      base_v <- sub("^([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\\^([0-9]+)$", "\\1", f)
      pwr <- as.integer(sub("^([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\\^([0-9]+)$", "\\2", f))
      expanded_num <- c(expanded_num, rep(base_v, pwr))
    } else if (f != "1" && nzchar(f)) {
      expanded_num <- c(expanded_num, f)
    }
  }

  if (length(expanded_num) == 0L) {
    num_str <- "1"
  } else {
    tbl <- table(sort(expanded_num))
    parts <- character(0)
    for (nm in sort(names(tbl))) {
      cnt <- tbl[[nm]]
      if (cnt == 1L) {
        parts <- c(parts, nm)
      } else {
        parts <- c(parts, paste0(nm, "^", cnt))
      }
    }
    num_str <- paste(parts, collapse = "*")
  }

  den_clean <- m$den[nzchar(m$den) & m$den != "1"]
  if (length(den_clean) == 0L) {
    return(num_str)
  }
  den_sorted <- sort(den_clean)
  den_str <- paste(paste0("(", den_sorted, ")"), collapse = "*")
  paste0(num_str, "/", den_str)
}

#' Canonicalize a single feature / basis expression string
canonicalize_single_expression <- function(expr_str) {
  atoms <- extract_canonical_atoms(expr_str)
  if (length(atoms) == 0L) return("1")
  paste(atoms, collapse = "+")
}

#' Normalize rational complements modulo intercept symmetrically across all methods:
#' x^2 / (1 + x^2) = 1 - 1 / (1 + x^2) and x / (1 + x) = 1 - 1 / (1 + x).
normalize_modulo_intercept <- function(atoms) {
  if (length(atoms) == 0L) return(character(0))
  out <- vapply(atoms, function(a) {
    a1 <- sub("^(x[0-9]+)\\^2/\\(1\\+\\1\\^2\\)$", "1/(1+\\1^2)", a)
    a2 <- sub("^(x[0-9]+)/\\(1\\+\\1\\)$", "1/(1+\\1)", a1)
    a2
  }, character(1), USE.NAMES = FALSE)
  sort(unique(out))
}

#' Extract canonical basis atoms (excluding intercept "1") from a feature or equation string
extract_canonical_atoms <- function(expr_strings, include_intercept = FALSE) {
  if (length(expr_strings) == 0L) return(character(0))
  all_atoms <- character(0)

  for (raw_s in expr_strings) {
    s <- preprocess_math_string(raw_s)
    if (!nzchar(s) || s %in% c("1", "(Intercept)", "Intercept")) {
      if (include_intercept) all_atoms <- c(all_atoms, "1")
      next
    }
    if (nchar(s) > 500L) {
      all_atoms <- c(all_atoms, substr(s, 1L, 500L))
      next
    }
    parsed <- tryCatch(parse(text = s)[[1]], error = function(e) NULL)
    if (is.null(parsed)) {
      all_atoms <- c(all_atoms, s)
      next
    }
    mons <- tryCatch(ast_to_monomials(parsed), error = function(e) NULL)
    if (is.null(mons)) {
      all_atoms <- c(all_atoms, s)
      next
    }
    for (m in mons) {
      atom <- format_monomial_atom(m)
      if (nzchar(atom)) {
        if (atom == "1" && !include_intercept) next
        all_atoms <- c(all_atoms, atom)
      }
    }
  }
  sort(unique(all_atoms))
}

#' Prune negligible basis terms based on relative L2 energy ||beta_k g_k(X)||_2 / ||Y||_2 < tol_rel
prune_negligible_terms <- function(atom_strings, coefs, X, y, tol_rel = 1e-3) {
  if (length(atom_strings) == 0L) return(character(0))
  y_norm <- sqrt(sum(y^2))
  if (!is.finite(y_norm) || y_norm < 1e-12) y_norm <- 1.0

  keep <- logical(length(atom_strings))
  for (k in seq_along(atom_strings)) {
    atom <- atom_strings[k]
    if (atom %in% c("1", "(Intercept)", "Intercept")) {
      keep[k] <- FALSE
      next
    }
    beta_k <- if (length(coefs) >= k && is.finite(coefs[k])) coefs[k] else 1.0
    if (abs(beta_k) < 1e-10) {
      keep[k] <- FALSE
      next
    }
    gk <- evaluate_basis_atom(atom, X)
    term_norm <- abs(beta_k) * sqrt(sum(gk^2))
    keep[k] <- (term_norm / y_norm) >= tol_rel
  }
  extract_canonical_atoms(atom_strings[keep])
}

#' Count total mathematical operations and variables (Operation Count OC) in discovered atoms
compute_operation_count <- function(atom_strings) {
  atoms <- extract_canonical_atoms(atom_strings)
  if (length(atoms) == 0L) return(0L)
  total_oc <- 0L
  for (a in atoms) {
    # Count variables x1..xm
    vars <- gregexpr("x[0-9]+", a)[[1]]
    n_vars <- if (vars[1] == -1L) 0L else length(vars)
    # Count binary operators *, /, +, ^
    ops <- gregexpr("[\\*\\/\\+\\^]", a)[[1]]
    n_ops <- if (ops[1] == -1L) 0L else length(ops)
    # Count unary functions sin, cos, exp, sqrt, sigmoid
    fns <- gregexpr("(sin|cos|exp|sqrt|sigmoid)", a)[[1]]
    n_fns <- if (fns[1] == -1L) 0L else length(fns)
    total_oc <- total_oc + n_vars + n_ops + n_fns
  }
  as.integer(total_oc)
}



=== FILE: R/06_metrics_consistency.R ===
# ==============================================================================
# R/06_metrics_consistency.R
# Selection Consistency, Stability (Jaccard & Nogueira), PIP Calibration, & Rollout
# ==============================================================================

if (!exists("extract_canonical_atoms", mode = "function")) {
  if (file.exists("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) {
    source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
  } else if (file.exists("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")) {
    source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
  }
}

#' Compute structural identification metrics for a single equation or full ODE system
#' @param discovered_list List of character vectors of discovered basis strings per equation
#' @param ground_truth_list List of character vectors of true basis strings per equation
compute_structure_metrics <- function(discovered_list, ground_truth_list) {
  m <- length(ground_truth_list)
  stopifnot(length(discovered_list) == m)

  # tag each canonical atom with its equation index "eqj::atom" for exact system-wide matching
  S_hat <- character(0)
  S_star <- character(0)
  tpr_eq <- numeric(m)
  fdr_eq <- numeric(m)
  exact_eq <- logical(m)
  oc_total <- 0L

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    hat_j_raw <- extract_canonical_atoms(discovered_list[[j]])
    oc_total <- oc_total + compute_operation_count(hat_j_raw)
    hat_j <- normalize_modulo_intercept(hat_j_raw)
    star_j <- normalize_modulo_intercept(extract_canonical_atoms(ground_truth_list[[j]]))

    tp_j <- length(intersect(hat_j, star_j))
    fp_j <- length(setdiff(hat_j, star_j))
    fn_j <- length(setdiff(star_j, hat_j))

    tpr_eq[j] <- if (length(star_j) > 0L) tp_j / length(star_j) else 1.0
    fdr_eq[j] <- if (length(hat_j) > 0L) fp_j / length(hat_j) else 0.0
    exact_eq[j] <- (fp_j == 0L && fn_j == 0L)

    if (length(hat_j) > 0L) S_hat <- c(S_hat, paste0("eq", j, "::", hat_j))
    if (length(star_j) > 0L) S_star <- c(S_star, paste0("eq", j, "::", star_j))
  }

  tp <- length(intersect(S_hat, S_star))
  fp <- length(setdiff(S_hat, S_star))
  fn <- length(setdiff(S_star, S_hat))

  tpr <- if (length(S_star) > 0L) tp / length(S_star) else 1.0
  fdr <- if (length(S_hat) > 0L) fp / length(S_hat) else 0.0
  precision <- 1.0 - fdr
  f1 <- if ((tpr + precision) > 0) (2.0 * tpr * precision) / (tpr + precision) else 0.0
  shd <- as.integer(fp + fn)
  exact_recovery <- as.integer(fp == 0L && fn == 0L)

  list(
    TPR = tpr,
    FDR = fdr,
    F1 = f1,
    Exact_Recovery = exact_recovery,
    SHD = shd,
    OC = oc_total,
    n_active_terms = length(S_hat),
    TPR_eq_mean = mean(tpr_eq),
    FDR_eq_mean = mean(fdr_eq),
    S_hat = S_hat,
    S_star = S_star
  )
}

#' Compute Posterior Inclusion Probability (PIP) Brier Score across candidate features
#' @param pip_list List of named numeric vectors (names = feature strings, values = PIP in [0,1]) per equation
#' @param ground_truth_list List of true basis atom vectors per equation
compute_pip_brier_score <- function(pip_list, ground_truth_list) {
  if (is.null(pip_list) || length(pip_list) == 0L) return(NA_real_)
  sq_errors <- numeric(0)

  for (j in seq_along(ground_truth_list)) {
    if (j > length(pip_list)) next
    pips <- pip_list[[j]]
    if (is.null(pips) || length(pips) == 0L) next
    star_j <- extract_canonical_atoms(ground_truth_list[[j]])

    # Map candidate feature names to canonical atoms and aggregate max PIP per canonical atom
    canon_names <- vapply(names(pips), function(nm) {
      ca <- extract_canonical_atoms(nm)
      if (length(ca) == 0L) "" else paste(ca, collapse = "+")
    }, character(1))
    valid <- nzchar(canon_names)
    pips <- pips[valid]
    canon_names <- canon_names[valid]

    all_atoms <- union(unique(canon_names), star_j)
    for (a in all_atoms) {
      prob_a <- if (a %in% canon_names) max(pips[canon_names == a], na.rm = TRUE) else 0.0
      prob_a <- min(max(prob_a, 0.0), 1.0)
      truth_a <- as.numeric(a %in% star_j)
      sq_errors <- c(sq_errors, (prob_a - truth_a)^2)
    }
  }
  if (length(sq_errors) == 0L) return(NA_real_)
  mean(sq_errors)
}

#' Compute denominator-invariant PIP calibration metrics (SSPE, AUROC, AUPRC, and 10-bin reliability)
compute_pip_calibration_metrics <- function(pip_list, ground_truth_list, n_bins = 10L) {
  empty_res <- list(
    PIP_Brier = NA_real_, PIP_SSPE = NA_real_,
    PIP_AUROC = NA_real_, PIP_AUPRC = NA_real_,
    reliability = data.frame(
      bin_mid = numeric(0), mean_pip = numeric(0),
      emp_freq = numeric(0), count = integer(0)
    )
  )
  if (is.null(pip_list) || length(pip_list) == 0L) return(empty_res)

  probs_all <- numeric(0)
  truth_all <- numeric(0)
  for (j in seq_along(ground_truth_list)) {
    if (j > length(pip_list)) next
    pips <- pip_list[[j]]
    if (is.null(pips) || length(pips) == 0L) next
    star_j <- normalize_modulo_intercept(extract_canonical_atoms(ground_truth_list[[j]]))

    canon_names <- vapply(names(pips), function(nm) {
      ca <- normalize_modulo_intercept(extract_canonical_atoms(nm))
      if (length(ca) == 0L) "" else paste(ca, collapse = "+")
    }, character(1))
    valid <- nzchar(canon_names)
    pips <- pips[valid]
    canon_names <- canon_names[valid]

    all_atoms <- union(unique(canon_names), star_j)
    for (a in all_atoms) {
      prob_a <- if (a %in% canon_names) max(pips[canon_names == a], na.rm = TRUE) else 0.0
      prob_a <- min(max(prob_a, 0.0), 1.0)
      probs_all <- c(probs_all, prob_a)
      truth_all <- c(truth_all, as.numeric(a %in% star_j))
    }
  }
  if (length(probs_all) == 0L) return(empty_res)

  sq_err <- (probs_all - truth_all)^2
  brier <- mean(sq_err)
  sspe <- sum(sq_err)

  # AUROC via Mann-Whitney U
  n_pos <- sum(truth_all == 1.0)
  n_neg <- sum(truth_all == 0.0)
  auroc <- NA_real_
  auprc <- NA_real_
  if (n_pos > 0L && n_neg > 0L) {
    rk <- rank(probs_all, ties.method = "average")
    auroc <- (sum(rk[truth_all == 1.0]) - n_pos * (n_pos + 1.0) / 2.0) / (n_pos * n_neg)
    ord <- order(probs_all, decreasing = TRUE)
    t_ord <- truth_all[ord]
    tp_cum <- cumsum(t_ord)
    prec_k <- tp_cum / seq_along(t_ord)
    rec_k <- tp_cum / n_pos
    rec_prev <- c(0.0, rec_k[-length(rec_k)])
    auprc <- sum((rec_k - rec_prev) * prec_k)
  }

  breaks <- seq(0.0, 1.0, length.out = n_bins + 1L)
  bin_idx <- cut(probs_all, breaks = breaks, include.lowest = TRUE, labels = FALSE)
  rel_rows <- lapply(seq_len(n_bins), function(b) {
    sel <- which(bin_idx == b)
    if (length(sel) == 0L) return(NULL)
    data.frame(
      bin_id = b,
      bin_mid = 0.5 * (breaks[b] + breaks[b + 1L]),
      mean_pip = mean(probs_all[sel]),
      emp_freq = mean(truth_all[sel]),
      count = length(sel)
    )
  })
  rel_df <- do.call(rbind, Filter(Negate(is.null), rel_rows))
  if (is.null(rel_df)) rel_df <- empty_res$reliability

  list(
    PIP_Brier = brier,
    PIP_SSPE = sspe,
    PIP_AUROC = auroc,
    PIP_AUPRC = auprc,
    reliability = rel_df
  )
}

#' Compute R^2, clipped R^2, and NRMSE between predicted and true derivatives/trajectories
compute_prediction_metrics <- function(Y_pred, Y_true, lower_95 = NULL, upper_95 = NULL) {
  Y_pred <- as.matrix(Y_pred)
  Y_true <- as.matrix(Y_true)
  m <- ncol(Y_true)

  r2_raw_vec <- numeric(m)
  nrmse_vec <- numeric(m)
  rmse_vec <- numeric(m)

  for (j in seq_len(m)) {
    yp <- Y_pred[, j]
    yt <- Y_true[, j]
    ok <- is.finite(yp) & is.finite(yt)
    if (sum(ok) < 3L) {
      r2_raw_vec[j] <- 0.0
      nrmse_vec[j] <- 1.0
      rmse_vec[j] <- NA_real_
      next
    }
    yp <- yp[ok]; yt <- yt[ok]
    ss_res <- sum((yt - yp)^2)
    ss_tot <- sum((yt - mean(yt))^2)
    rmse_j <- sqrt(mean((yt - yp)^2))
    sd_y <- sd(yt)
    if (!is.finite(sd_y) || sd_y < 1e-12) sd_y <- 1.0

    r2_sse <- 1.0 - (ss_res / max(ss_tot, 1e-12))
    r2_cor <- suppressWarnings(cor(yp, yt)^2)
    if (!is.finite(r2_cor)) r2_cor <- 0.0
    # Store standard R2 (with correlation R2 fallback if constant offset)
    r2_raw_vec[j] <- r2_sse
    nrmse_vec[j] <- rmse_j / sd_y
    rmse_vec[j] <- rmse_j
  }

  r2_raw <- mean(r2_raw_vec, na.rm = TRUE)
  r2_clipped <- mean(pmax(0.0, pmin(1.0, r2_raw_vec)), na.rm = TRUE)
  nrmse <- mean(pmin(nrmse_vec, 10.0), na.rm = TRUE)
  log10_rmse <- log10(max(mean(rmse_vec, na.rm = TRUE), 1e-12))

  # 95% Credible / Prediction Interval Coverage and Winkler Score
  coverage_95 <- NA_real_
  winkler_95 <- NA_real_
  if (!is.null(lower_95) && !is.null(upper_95)) {
    L <- as.matrix(lower_95)
    U <- as.matrix(upper_95)
    if ( all(dim(L) == dim(Y_true)) && all(dim(U) == dim(Y_true)) ) {
      alpha <- 0.05
      inside <- (Y_true >= L) & (Y_true <= U)
      coverage_95 <- mean(inside, na.rm = TRUE)
      width <- pmax(0.0, U - L)
      penalty_low <- (2.0 / alpha) * pmax(0.0, L - Y_true)
      penalty_high <- (2.0 / alpha) * pmax(0.0, Y_true - U)
      sd_scale <- mean(apply(Y_true, 2, sd), na.rm = TRUE)
      if (!is.finite(sd_scale) || sd_scale < 1e-8) sd_scale <- 1.0
      winkler_95 <- mean(width + penalty_low + penalty_high, na.rm = TRUE) / sd_scale
    }
  }

  list(
    R2_raw = r2_raw,
    R2_clipped = r2_clipped,
    NRMSE = nrmse,
    log10_RMSE = log10_rmse,
    Coverage_95 = coverage_95,
    Winkler_95 = winkler_95
  )
}

#' Forward ODE Trajectory Rollout Evaluator (R2_traj, NRMSE_traj, and Valid Prediction Time T_valid)
#' @param predict_rhs_fn Function taking state matrix X (n x m) and returning derivative matrix dX (n x m)
#' @param rollout_spec List with `times`, `X_true`, `x0`, and optional `X_inputs`
evaluate_trajectory_rollout <- function(predict_rhs_fn, rollout_spec, tol_valid = 0.20) {
  times <- rollout_spec$times
  X_true <- as.matrix(rollout_spec$X_true)
  x0 <- as.numeric(rollout_spec$x0)
  m <- ncol(X_true)
  names(x0) <- colnames(X_true)
  X_inputs <- rollout_spec$X_inputs

  bound_max <- 50.0 * max(abs(X_true), 1.0)

  ode_func <- function(t, state, parms) {
    if (any(!is.finite(state)) || max(abs(state)) > bound_max) {
      return(list(rep(0.0, m)))
    }
    state_mat <- matrix(state, nrow = 1L, ncol = m)
    colnames(state_mat) <- colnames(X_true)
    if (!is.null(X_inputs)) {
      # Continuous linear interpolation of exogenous inputs at time t
      inp_vals <- vapply(seq_len(ncol(X_inputs)), function(k_inp) {
        stats::approx(times, X_inputs[, k_inp], xout = t, rule = 2)$y
      }, numeric(1))
      inp_mat <- matrix(inp_vals, nrow = 1L)
      colnames(inp_mat) <- colnames(X_inputs)
      state_mat <- cbind(state_mat, inp_mat)
    }
    d_state <- tryCatch(
      as.numeric(predict_rhs_fn(state_mat)),
      error = function(e) rep(0.0, m)
    )
    d_state[!is.finite(d_state)] <- 0.0
    d_state <- pmax(pmin(d_state, bound_max), -bound_max)
    list(d_state)
  }

  sol <- tryCatch(
    suppressWarnings(deSolve::ode(
      y = x0,
      times = times,
      func = ode_func,
      parms = NULL,
      method = "rk4"
    )),
    error = function(e) NULL
  )

  if (is.null(sol) || nrow(sol) != length(times)) {
    return(list(R2_traj = 0.0, NRMSE_traj = 1.0, T_valid = 0.0, X_sim = matrix(NA_real_, nrow(X_true), m)))
  }

  X_sim <- as.matrix(sol[, -1, drop = FALSE])
  pred_m <- compute_prediction_metrics(X_sim, X_true)

  # Valid prediction time T_valid until normalized step error exceeds tol_valid
  scale_sd <- apply(X_true, 2, sd)
  scale_sd[scale_sd < 1e-8] <- 1.0
  rel_err_t <- sqrt(rowMeans(sweep(X_sim - X_true, 2, scale_sd, "/")^2))
  exceed_idx <- which(!is.finite(rel_err_t) | rel_err_t > tol_valid)
  if (length(exceed_idx) == 0L) {
    t_valid <- max(times) - min(times)
  } else {
    t_valid <- times[max(1L, exceed_idx[1] - 1L)] - min(times)
  }
  t_horizon <- max(max(times) - min(times), 1e-8)

  list(
    R2_traj = pred_m$R2_clipped,
    R2_traj_raw = pred_m$R2_raw,
    NRMSE_traj = pred_m$NRMSE,
    T_valid = t_valid,
    T_valid_frac = t_valid / t_horizon,
    X_sim = X_sim
  )
}

#' Pairwise Jaccard Stability across R replications
#' @param selected_sets_list List of length R, each a character vector of selected canonical atoms
compute_jaccard_stability <- function(selected_sets_list) {
  R <- length(selected_sets_list)
  if (R < 2L) return(NA_real_)
  j_vals <- numeric(0)
  for (i in 1:(R - 1L)) {
    Si <- unique(selected_sets_list[[i]])
    for (j in (i + 1L):R) {
      Sj <- unique(selected_sets_list[[j]])
      u_len <- length(union(Si, Sj))
      j_ij <- if (u_len == 0L) 1.0 else length(intersect(Si, Sj)) / u_len
      j_vals <- c(j_vals, j_ij)
    }
  }
  mean(j_vals)
}

#' Nogueira's Stability Estimator (Nogueira et al., JMLR 2018) with 95% Confidence Intervals
#' @param selected_sets_list List of length R, each a character vector of selected canonical atoms
#' @param d_total Optional total universe size of candidate features (inferred from union + padding if NULL)
compute_nogueira_stability <- function(selected_sets_list, d_total = 100L) {
  M <- length(selected_sets_list)
  if (M < 2L) {
    return(list(phi = NA_real_, ci_lower = NA_real_, ci_upper = NA_real_))
  }
  universe <- sort(unique(unlist(selected_sets_list)))
  if (length(universe) == 0L) {
    return(list(phi = 1.0, ci_lower = 1.0, ci_upper = 1.0))
  }
  d <- if (is.null(d_total)) max(length(universe) + 10L, 20L) else max(as.integer(d_total), length(universe))

  # Construct binary selection matrix Z (M x d)
  Z <- matrix(0.0, nrow = M, ncol = d)
  for (m in seq_len(M)) {
    idx <- match(unique(selected_sets_list[[m]]), universe)
    idx <- idx[!is.na(idx)]
    if (length(idx) > 0L) Z[m, idx] <- 1.0
  }

  p_hat <- colMeans(Z)
  s_f2 <- (M / (M - 1.0)) * p_hat * (1.0 - p_hat)
  k_bar <- sum(rowSums(Z)) / M
  denom <- (k_bar / d) * (1.0 - k_bar / d)

  if (denom < 1e-12) {
    return(list(phi = 1.0, ci_lower = 1.0, ci_upper = 1.0))
  }
  phi_hat <- 1.0 - (mean(s_f2) / denom)
  phi_hat <- max(min(phi_hat, 1.0), -1.0)

  # Jackknife / influence-function variance estimate (Nogueira et al. 2018 Theorem 5)
  phi_i <- numeric(M)
  for (i in seq_len(M)) {
    phi_i[i] <- (1.0 / denom) * (mean(Z[i, ] * p_hat) - (k_bar * sum(rowSums(Z)[i])) / (d^2) +
                                   (phi_hat / 2.0) * ((2.0 * k_bar * sum(Z[i, ])) / (d^2) - sum(Z[i, ]) / d - k_bar / d + 1.0))
  }
  var_phi <- (4.0 / (M^2)) * sum((phi_i - mean(phi_i))^2)
  se_phi <- sqrt(max(var_phi, 0.0))

  list(
    phi = phi_hat,
    se = se_phi,
    ci_lower = max(-1.0, phi_hat - 1.96 * se_phi),
    ci_upper = min(1.0, phi_hat + 1.96 * se_phi)
  )
}
