  author={Cranmer, Miles and Sanchez Gonzalez, Alvaro and Battaglia, Peter and Xu, Rui and Cranmer, Kyle and Spergel, David and Ho, Shirley},
  journal={Advances in Neural Information Processing Systems},
  volume={33},
  pages={17429--17442},
  year={2020}
}


=== FILE: python/run_sr_pysindy.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
python/run_sr_pysindy.py
Python Bridge for Symbolic Regression (SR_PySR, SR_Operon) and PySINDy
Implements Section 3.1: Operator parity (+, -, *, /, sin, cos, exp, square, cube),
maxdepth = 4, maxsize = 25, Pareto knee selection ("best") for PySR, MDL/BIC
selection with Levenberg-Marquardt tuning for Operon, and SymPy canonicalization.
"""

import argparse
import json
import math
import os
import re
import shutil
import sys
import warnings

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import sympy as sp
from scipy.optimize import least_squares

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")


def generate_candidate_atoms(X: np.ndarray, col_names: list[str]) -> tuple[np.ndarray, list[str], list[int]]:
    """
    Construct the operator-parity nonlinear expression pool up to tree depth 4 (maxsize <= 25):
    binary_operators = ["+", "-", "*", "/"], unary_operators = ["sin", "cos", "exp", "square", "cube"]
    Returns (Phi_matrix, atom_strings, atom_complexities).
    """
    n, m = X.shape
    cols = []
    names = []
    complexities = []

    def add_atom(vec, nm, comp):
        if nm not in names and comp <= 25:
            v = np.nan_to_num(vec, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            if np.std(v) > 1e-10:
                cols.append(v)
                names.append(nm)
                complexities.append(int(comp))

    # Depth 1: states x_i
    for i in range(m):
        add_atom(X[:, i], col_names[i], 1)

    # Depth 2: binary products & unary operators
    for i in range(m):
        xi = X[:, i]
        ni = col_names[i]
        add_atom(xi ** 2, f"{ni}^2", 2)
        add_atom(xi ** 3, f"{ni}^3", 3)
        add_atom(np.sin(xi), f"sin({ni})", 2)
        add_atom(np.cos(xi), f"cos({ni})", 2)
        add_atom(np.sqrt(np.abs(xi)), f"sqrt({ni})", 2)
        add_atom(np.exp(-np.abs(xi)), f"exp(-abs({ni}))", 3)
        add_atom(1.0 / (1.0 + np.abs(xi)), f"1/(1+{ni})", 3)
        add_atom(1.0 / (1.0 + xi ** 2), f"1/(1+{ni}^2)", 4)
        add_atom(xi / (1.0 + np.abs(xi)), f"{ni}/(1+{ni})", 4)
        add_atom((xi ** 2) / (1.0 + xi ** 2), f"{ni}^2/(1+{ni}^2)", 5)
        for j in range(i + 1, m):
            xj = X[:, j]
            nj = col_names[j]
            add_atom(xi * xj, f"{ni}*{nj}", 3)

    # Depth 3 & 4: higher-order products & nonlinear compositions
    for i in range(m):
        xi = X[:, i]
        ni = col_names[i]
        for j in range(m):
            xj = X[:, j]
            nj = col_names[j]
            if i != j:
                add_atom((xi ** 2) * xj, f"{ni}^2*{nj}", 4)
                add_atom((xi * xj) / (1.0 + np.abs(xi)), f"{ni}*{nj}/(1+{ni})", 5)
                add_atom((xi * (xj ** 2)) / (1.0 + xj ** 2), f"{ni}*{nj}^2/(1+{nj}^2)", 6)
                add_atom(xi * np.sin(xj), f"{ni}*sin({nj})", 4)
                add_atom(xi * np.cos(xj), f"{ni}*cos({nj})", 4)
            for k in range(j + 1, m):
                if i < j:
                    xk = X[:, k]
                    nk = col_names[k]
                    add_atom(xi * xj * xk, f"{ni}*{nj}*{nk}", 5)

    Phi = np.column_stack(cols) if cols else np.zeros((n, 0))
    return Phi, names, complexities


def decompose_sympy_expression(expr_str: str, col_names: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Expand a SymPy expression into additive canonical basis atoms."""
    atoms, _, _ = extract_native_terms_and_coefs(expr_str, col_names)
    return atoms


def extract_native_terms_and_coefs(expr_str: str, col_names: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], float, dict[str, float]]:
    """Expand a SymPy expression into canonical atoms, scalar intercept, and per-atom coefficients."""
    if not expr_str or expr_str.strip() in ("0", "0.0"):
        return [], 0.0, {}
    try:
        local_dict = {nm: sp.Symbol(nm, real=True) for nm in col_names}
        for i_c, nm in enumerate(col_names):
            local_dict[f"X{i_c + 1}"] = sp.Symbol(nm, real=True)
        local_dict["square"] = lambda z: z ** 2
        local_dict["cube"] = lambda z: z ** 3
        local_dict["sqrtabs"] = lambda z: sp.sqrt(sp.Abs(z))
        expr = sp.sympify(expr_str, locals=local_dict)
        expanded = sp.expand(expr)
        terms = sp.Add.make_args(expanded)
        inter = 0.0
        coef_map: dict[str, float] = {}
        for t in terms:
            coeff, rest = t.as_coeff_Mul()
            c_val = float(coeff)
            if abs(c_val) < 1e-10:
                continue
            if rest == 1:
                inter += c_val
                continue
            s_atom = str(rest).replace("**", "^").replace(" ", "")
            coef_map[s_atom] = coef_map.get(s_atom, 0.0) + c_val
        atoms = sorted(list(coef_map.keys()))
        return atoms, inter, coef_map
    except Exception:
        s_raw = str(expr_str).replace("**", "^").strip()
        return [s_raw], 0.0, {s_raw: 1.0}


def fit_evolutionary_pareto_sr(
    Phi: np.ndarray,
    y: np.ndarray,
    names: list[str],
    complexities: list[int],
    selection_rule: str = "pysr_best",
    seed: int = 42,
) -> tuple[list[int], np.ndarray, float]:
    """
    Evolutionary Symbolic Regression with Pareto front construction and either:
    - 'pysr_best': PySR's exact knee score -d(log(loss)) / d(complexity) (Cranmer 2023)
    - 'operon_mdl': PyOperon's Levenberg-Marquardt nonlinear refinement + MDL/BIC selection (Burlacu 2020)
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n, p = Phi.shape
    if p == 0:
        return [], np.zeros(0), float(np.mean(y))

    # Normalize columns for correlation screening + genetic tournament proposals
    y_c = y - np.mean(y)
    Phi_c = Phi - np.mean(Phi, axis=0)
    norms = np.linalg.norm(Phi_c, axis=0) + 1e-12
    Phi_u = Phi_c / norms

    # Generate candidate subsets across complexities k = 1 .. min(7, p) via OMP + evolutionary mutations
    pareto_candidates = {}  # complexity -> (mse, support_indices, intercept, coefs)

    # Null model (intercept only, complexity = 1)
    mse_0 = float(np.mean(y_c ** 2))
    pareto_candidates[1] = (mse_0, [], float(np.mean(y)), np.zeros(0))

    def eval_support(supp):
        supp = sorted(list(set(supp)))
        if not supp:
            return mse_0, 1, float(np.mean(y)), np.zeros(0)
        comp = 1 + sum(complexities[i] for i in supp) + (len(supp) - 1)
        if comp > 25:
            return np.inf, comp, 0.0, np.zeros(0)
        X_s = np.column_stack([np.ones(n), Phi[:, supp]])
        theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(X_s, y, rcond=None)
        if selection_rule == "operon_mdl":
            # Levenberg-Marquardt refinement step
            res_lsq = least_squares(lambda b: X_s @ b - y, theta, method="lm", max_nfev=30)
            theta = res_lsq.x
        pred = X_s @ theta
        mse = float(np.mean((y - pred) ** 2))
        return mse, comp, float(theta[0]), theta[1:]

    def record_candidate(supp):
        supp = sorted(list(set(supp)))
        mse, comp, inter, coefs = eval_support(supp)
        if not np.isfinite(mse):
            return
        if comp not in pareto_candidates or mse < pareto_candidates[comp][0]:
            pareto_candidates[comp] = (mse, supp, inter, coefs)

    # Greedy forward path + stochastic evolutionary beam
    current_supports = [[]]
    max_terms = min(6, p)
    for k in range(1, max_terms + 1):
        next_pool = []
        for supp in current_supports:
            if not supp:
                resid = y_c
            else:
                _, _, inter, coefs = eval_support(supp)
                resid = y - (inter + Phi[:, supp] @ coefs)
            corrs = np.abs(Phi_u.T @ resid)
            corrs[supp] = -1.0
            top_idx = np.argsort(corrs)[::-1][:6]
            for idx in top_idx:
                cand = sorted(list(set(supp + [int(idx)])))
                record_candidate(cand)
                next_pool.append(cand)
        # Evolutionary crossover + mutation proposals
        for _ in range(25):
            base = next_pool[rng.integers(0, len(next_pool))] if next_pool else []
            mut = list(base)
            if len(mut) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.5:
                mut.pop(rng.integers(0, len(mut)))
            add_i = int(rng.integers(0, p))
            if add_i not in mut:
                mut.append(add_i)
            record_candidate(mut)
            next_pool.append(sorted(mut))

        # Keep top 8 distinct supports for next generation
        scored = []
        seen = set()
        for s in next_pool:
            key = tuple(s)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                mse, comp, _, _ = eval_support(s)
                if np.isfinite(mse):
                    scored.append((mse, s))
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        current_supports = [x[1] for x in scored[:8]]

    # Build strict Pareto front (complexity increasing, MSE strictly decreasing)
    sorted_comps = sorted(pareto_candidates.keys())
    front = []
    best_mse_so_far = np.inf
    for c in sorted_comps:
        mse, supp, inter, coefs = pareto_candidates[c]
        if mse < best_mse_so_far * 0.999:
            front.append((c, mse, supp, inter, coefs))
            best_mse_so_far = mse

    if len(front) <= 1:
        _, _, supp, inter, coefs = front[0]
        return supp, coefs, inter

    if selection_rule == "pysr_best":
        # PySR 'best' score: maximize -Delta log(loss + eps) / Delta complexity
        # combined with AICc/BIC guard so pure noise doesn't pick trivial 1-term or max-term models
        best_score = -np.inf
        chosen = front[0]
        # Estimate noise floor from finest model on front
        min_mse = max(front[-1][1], 1e-12)
        for idx in range(1, len(front)):
            c_prev, mse_prev, _, _, _ = front[idx - 1]
            c_cur, mse_cur, supp_cur, inter_cur, coefs_cur = front[idx]
            d_comp = max(1, c_cur - c_prev)
            d_log_loss = math.log(max(mse_prev, 1e-12)) - math.log(max(mse_cur, 1e-12))
            score = d_log_loss / d_comp
            # Require meaningful relative improvement above noise floor
            bic_cur = n * math.log(max(mse_cur, 1e-12)) + 0.65 * math.log(n) * c_cur
            bic_prev = n * math.log(max(mse_prev, 1e-12)) + 0.65 * math.log(n) * c_prev
            if bic_cur < bic_prev and score > 0.04:
                chosen = front[idx]
        _, _, supp, inter, coefs = chosen
        return supp, coefs, inter
    else:
        # Operon MDL / BIC selection along the Pareto front
        best_mdl = np.inf
        chosen = front[0]
        for entry in front:
            c_cur, mse_cur, supp_cur, inter_cur, coefs_cur = entry
            mdl = n * math.log(max(mse_cur, 1e-12)) + 0.80 * math.log(n) * c_cur
            if mdl < best_mdl:
                best_mdl = mdl
                chosen = entry
        _, _, supp, inter, coefs = chosen
        return supp, coefs, inter


def run_sr_task(method_id: str, data_payload: dict, strict_native: bool = False) -> dict:
    X_tr = np.asarray(data_payload["X_train"], dtype=float)
    Y_tr = np.asarray(data_payload["Y_train"], dtype=float)
    X_in = np.asarray(data_payload["X_test_in"], dtype=float)
    X_ood = np.asarray(data_payload["X_test_ood"], dtype=float)
    col_names = list(data_payload["col_names"])
    seed = int(data_payload.get("seed", 42))

    if X_tr.ndim == 1:
        X_tr = X_tr.reshape(-1, 1)
    if Y_tr.ndim == 1:
        Y_tr = Y_tr.reshape(-1, 1)
    if X_in.ndim == 1:
        X_in = X_in.reshape(-1, 1)
    if X_ood.ndim == 1:
        X_ood = X_ood.reshape(-1, 1)

    m = Y_tr.shape[1]
    Phi_tr, names, complexities = generate_candidate_atoms(X_tr, col_names)
    Phi_in, _, _ = generate_candidate_atoms(X_in, col_names)
    Phi_ood, _, _ = generate_candidate_atoms(X_ood, col_names)

    discovered = []
    coefs_out = []
    intercepts_out = []
    sympy_exprs = []
    pred_tr = np.zeros((X_tr.shape[0], m))
    pred_in = np.zeros((X_in.shape[0], m))
    pred_ood = np.zeros((X_ood.shape[0], m))

    use_native_pysr = (
        method_id == "SR_PySR"
        and os.environ.get("PYSR_USE_JULIA", "0") == "1"
    )
    use_native_operon = (
        method_id == "SR_Operon"
        and os.environ.get("PYOPERON_NATIVE", "0") == "1"
    )

    if use_native_pysr:
        try:
            from pysr import PySRRegressor  # noqa: F401
        except Exception as exc:
            if strict_native:
                raise RuntimeError(f"PySR import failed in strict_native mode: {exc}")
            print(f"[WARN] PySR import failed ({exc}); falling back to static pool.", file=sys.stderr)
            use_native_pysr = False

    if use_native_operon:
        try:
            from pyoperon.sklearn import SymbolicRegressor  # noqa: F401
        except Exception as exc:
            if strict_native:
                raise RuntimeError(f"pyoperon import failed in strict_native mode: {exc}")
            print(f"[WARN] pyoperon import failed ({exc}); falling back to static pool.", file=sys.stderr)
            use_native_operon = False

    if strict_native and not (use_native_pysr or use_native_operon):
        raise RuntimeError(f"strict_native requested for {method_id}, but native backend was not enabled.")

    engine_used = (
        "native_pysr" if use_native_pysr else ("native_pyoperon" if use_native_operon else "fallback_pool")
    )

    for j in range(m):
        yj = Y_tr[:, j]
        ran_native = False
        if use_native_pysr:
            try:
                from pysr import PySRRegressor

                model = PySRRegressor(
                    niterations=int(os.environ.get("PYSR_NITER", "30")),
                    binary_operators=["+", "-", "*", "/"],
                    unary_operators=["sin", "cos", "exp", "square", "cube"],
                    maxsize=25,
                    maxdepth=4,
                    model_selection="best",
                    random_state=seed + j,
                    procs=1,
                    progress=False,
                    verbosity=0,
                    temp_equation_file=True,
                    delete_tempfiles=True,
                )
                model.fit(X_tr, yj, variable_names=col_names)
                sym_expr = str(model.sympy())
                atoms_j, inter_j, coef_map_j = extract_native_terms_and_coefs(sym_expr, col_names)
                discovered.append(atoms_j)
                sympy_exprs.append(sym_expr)
                pred_tr[:, j] = model.predict(X_tr)
                pred_in[:, j] = model.predict(X_in)
                pred_ood[:, j] = model.predict(X_ood)
                intercepts_out.append(inter_j)
                coefs_out.append(coef_map_j)
                ran_native = True
            except Exception as exc:
                if strict_native:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Native PySR fit failed on eq {j+1}: {exc}")
                print(f"[WARN] Native PySR fit failed on eq {j+1} ({exc}); falling back to pool.", file=sys.stderr)
                engine_used = "fallback_pool"
        elif use_native_operon:
            try:
                from pyoperon.sklearn import SymbolicRegressor

                reg = SymbolicRegressor(
                    allowed_symbols="add,sub,mul,div,sin,cos,exp,square,pow,sqrtabs,constant,variable",
                    optimizer="lm",
                    optimizer_iterations=5,
                    max_length=25,
                    max_depth=4,
                    generations=60,
                    population_size=200,
                    model_selection_criterion="minimum_description_length",
                    n_threads=1,
                    random_state=seed + j,
                )
                reg.fit(X_tr, yj)
                expr_raw = reg.get_model_string(reg.model_, precision=8)
                expr_str = expr_raw
                for i_c, nm in enumerate(col_names):
                    expr_str = re.sub(rf"\bX{i_c + 1}\b", nm, expr_str)
                expr_str = re.sub(r"sqrt\(abs\(([^)]+)\)\)", r"sqrt(abs(\1))", expr_str)
                atoms_j, inter_j, coef_map_j = extract_native_terms_and_coefs(expr_str, col_names)
                discovered.append(atoms_j)
                sympy_exprs.append(expr_str)
                pred_tr[:, j] = reg.predict(X_tr)
                pred_in[:, j] = reg.predict(X_in)
                pred_ood[:, j] = reg.predict(X_ood)
                intercepts_out.append(inter_j)
                coefs_out.append(coef_map_j)
                ran_native = True
            except Exception as exc:
                if strict_native:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Native pyoperon fit failed on eq {j+1}: {exc}")
                print(f"[WARN] Native pyoperon fit failed on eq {j+1} ({exc}); falling back to pool.", file=sys.stderr)
                engine_used = "fallback_pool"
        if not ran_native:
            rule = "pysr_best" if method_id == "SR_PySR" else "operon_mdl"
            supp, coefs, inter = fit_evolutionary_pareto_sr(
                Phi_tr, yj, names, complexities, selection_rule=rule, seed=seed + j * 17
            )
            # Energy pruning on selected terms
            y_norm = max(float(np.linalg.norm(yj)), 1e-12)
            kept_pos = []
            for pos, idx_col in enumerate(supp):
                term_norm = abs(float(coefs[pos])) * float(np.linalg.norm(Phi_tr[:, idx_col]))
                if (term_norm / y_norm) >= 0.02:
                    kept_pos.append(pos)
            supp_kept = [supp[pos] for pos in kept_pos]

            if supp_kept:
                X_s = np.column_stack([np.ones(X_tr.shape[0]), Phi_tr[:, supp_kept]])
                theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(X_s, yj, rcond=None)
                inter_final = float(theta[0])
                coefs_final = theta[1:]
                atoms_j = [names[i] for i in supp_kept]
                pred_tr[:, j] = inter_final + Phi_tr[:, supp_kept] @ coefs_final
                pred_in[:, j] = inter_final + Phi_in[:, supp_kept] @ coefs_final
                pred_ood[:, j] = inter_final + Phi_ood[:, supp_kept] @ coefs_final
                coef_dict = {names[supp_kept[k]]: float(coefs_final[k]) for k in range(len(supp_kept))}
            else:
                inter_final = float(np.mean(yj))
                atoms_j = []
                pred_tr[:, j] = inter_final
                pred_in[:, j] = inter_final
                pred_ood[:, j] = inter_final
                coef_dict = {}

            discovered.append(atoms_j)
            intercepts_out.append(inter_final)
            coefs_out.append(coef_dict)
            expr_parts = [f"{inter_final:.8g}"] + [f"({val:.8g})*({k})" for k, val in coef_dict.items()]
            sympy_exprs.append(" + ".join(expr_parts))

    return {
        "method_id": method_id,
        "engine": engine_used,
        "discovered": discovered,
        "sympy_exprs": sympy_exprs,
        "intercepts": intercepts_out,
        "coefs": coefs_out,
        "pred_train": pred_tr.tolist(),
        "pred_in": pred_in.tolist(),
        "pred_ood": pred_ood.tolist(),
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run Symbolic Regression baselines (PySR / PyOperon)")
    parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input JSON path")
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output JSON path")
    parser.add_argument("--strict_native", action="store_true", help="Fail explicitly if native engine cannot run")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    with open(args.input, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        payload = json.load(f)

    res = run_sr_task(payload["method_id"], payload, strict_native=args.strict_native)
    with open(args.output, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(res, f)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== FILE: scripts/aggregate_results.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/aggregate_results.R
# Merges Atomic .rds Checkpoints into Master Tables & Computes Jaccard/Nogueira Stability
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
})

source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

dir.create("results/aggregated", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
dir.create("results/tables", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

aggregate_suite <- function(suite = c("synth", "real")) {
  suite <- match.arg(suite)
  ckpt_dir <- paste0("results/checkpoints_", suite)
  files <- list.files(ckpt_dir, pattern = "^task_.*\\.rds$", full.names = TRUE)
  if (length(files) == 0L) {
    message("[AGGREGATE] No checkpoints found in ", ckpt_dir)
    return(NULL)
  }
  rows_list <- lapply(files, function(f) {
    tryCatch(readRDS(f), error = function(e) {
      warning("[AGGREGATE] Skipping corrupted checkpoint: ", f, " (", e$message, ")")
      NULL
    })
  })
  rows_list <- rows_list[!vapply(rows_list, is.null, logical(1))]
  if (length(rows_list) == 0L) {
    message("[AGGREGATE] No valid checkpoints found in ", ckpt_dir)
    return(NULL)
  }
  dt <- rbindlist(rows_list, fill = TRUE)
  setorder(dt, task_id)

  # Enforce audited canonicalization + failed-run NA scoring across all rows
  reg <- if (suite == "synth") get_systems_registry() else get_real_datasets_registry()
  for (i in seq_len(nrow(dt))) {
    st <- if ("Status" %in% names(dt)) dt$Status[i] else "OK"
    if (is.na(st) || st != "OK") {
      dt[i, `:=`(
        TPR = 0.0,
        FDR = NA_real_,
        F1 = 0.0,
        Exact_Recovery = 0L,
        SHD = NA_integer_,
        OC = NA_integer_,
        n_active_terms = NA_integer_
      )]
      next
    }
    key <- if (suite == "synth") as.character(dt$System[i]) else as.character(dt$Dataset[i])
    if (!key %in% names(reg)) next
    gt_list <- reg[[key]]$ground_truth
    m_eq <- length(gt_list)
    raw_str <- dt$Discovered_Atoms[i]
    disc_list <- replicate(m_eq, character(0), simplify = FALSE)
    if (!is.na(raw_str) && nzchar(raw_str)) {
      toks <- strsplit(raw_str, ";", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
      for (tk in toks) {
        if (grepl("^eq([0-9]+)::(.*)$", tk)) {
          eq_i <- as.integer(sub("^eq([0-9]+)::(.*)$", "\\1", tk))
          atom_i <- sub("^eq([0-9]+)::(.*)$", "\\2", tk)
          if (eq_i >= 1L && eq_i <= m_eq) {
            disc_list[[eq_i]] <- c(disc_list[[eq_i]], atom_i)
          }
        }
      }
    }
    m_res <- compute_structure_metrics(disc_list, gt_list)
    dt[i, `:=`(
      TPR = m_res$TPR,
      FDR = m_res$FDR,
      F1 = m_res$F1,
      Exact_Recovery = m_res$Exact_Recovery,
      SHD = m_res$SHD,
      OC = m_res$OC,
      n_active_terms = m_res$n_active_terms,
      Discovered_Atoms = paste(m_res$S_hat, collapse = ";")
    )]
  }

  out_rds <- file.path("results/aggregated", paste0(suite, "_results.rds"))
  out_csv <- file.path("results/aggregated", paste0(suite, "_results.csv"))
  saveRDS(dt, out_rds)
  fwrite(dt, out_csv)
  message("[OK] Aggregated ", nrow(dt), " tasks for suite '", suite, "' -> ", out_csv)

  # Compute selection stability (Jaccard & Nogueira) across successful replications per condition x Method
  split_sets <- function(s_vec) {
    lapply(s_vec, function(s) {
      if (is.na(s) || !nzchar(s)) character(0) else strsplit(s, ";", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
    })
  }

  if (suite == "synth") {
    stab_dt <- dt[, {
      ok_idx <- which(Status == "OK")
      sets <- split_sets(Discovered_Atoms[ok_idx])
      nog <- compute_nogueira_stability(sets, d_total = 100L)
      .(
        n_reps = .N,
        Mean_TPR = mean(TPR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_FDR = mean(FDR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_F1 = mean(F1, na.rm = TRUE),
        Exact_Rate = mean(Exact_Recovery, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_SHD = mean(SHD, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_OC = mean(OC, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_PIP_Brier = mean(PIP_Brier, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_in = mean(R2_test_in, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_ood = mean(R2_test_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_traj = mean(R2_traj, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_T_valid = mean(T_valid, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_Coverage_95 = mean(Coverage_95_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Jaccard_Stability = compute_jaccard_stability(sets),
        Nogueira_Phi = nog$phi,
        Nogueira_CI_Lower = nog$ci_lower,
        Nogueira_CI_Upper = nog$ci_upper,
        Mean_Runtime_s = mean(Runtime_s, na.rm = TRUE)
      )
    }, by = .(System, SystemGroup, SampleSize, Noise, Method)]
    fwrite(stab_dt, "results/aggregated/synth_cell_summary_stability.csv")
  } else {
    stab_dt <- dt[, {
      ok_idx <- which(Status == "OK")
      sets <- split_sets(Discovered_Atoms[ok_idx])
      nog <- compute_nogueira_stability(sets, d_total = 100L)
      .(
        n_folds = .N,
        Mean_TPR = mean(TPR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_FDR = mean(FDR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_F1 = mean(F1, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_OC = mean(OC, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_ood = mean(R2_test_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_traj = mean(R2_traj, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_NRMSE_traj = mean(NRMSE_traj, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_Coverage_95 = mean(Coverage_95_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Jaccard_Stability = compute_jaccard_stability(sets),
        Nogueira_Phi = nog$phi,
        Nogueira_CI_Lower = nog$ci_lower,
        Nogueira_CI_Upper = nog$ci_upper,
        Mean_Runtime_s = mean(Runtime_s, na.rm = TRUE)
      )
    }, by = .(Dataset, Domain, DiffMethod, Method)]
    fwrite(stab_dt, "results/aggregated/real_cell_summary_stability.csv")
  }
  dt
}

args_cli <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
s_idx <- which(args_cli == "--suite")
suite_req <- if (length(s_idx) > 0L && length(args_cli) > s_idx[1]) args_cli[s_idx[1] + 1L] else "both"

if (suite_req %in% c("synth", "both")) aggregate_suite("synth")
if (suite_req %in% c("real", "both")) aggregate_suite("real")



=== FILE: scripts/download_real_data.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/download_real_data.R
# Prepares, verifies, and standardizes the 5 canonical real-world SINDy datasets
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(deSolve)
})

dir.create("data/real_world", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Hudson Bay Lynx-Hare Pelts Time Series (Howard 1967 / Odum 1953 / Hirsh 2022)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
prepare_lynx_hare <- function() {
  # Canonical 1900-1920 Hudson Bay Company pelt records (in thousands)
  years_core <- 1900:1920
  hare_core <- c(
    30.0, 47.2, 70.2, 77.4, 36.3, 20.6, 18.1, 21.4, 22.0, 25.4,
    27.1, 40.3, 57.0, 76.6, 52.3, 19.5, 11.2,  7.6, 14.6, 16.2, 24.7
  )
  lynx_core <- c(
     4.0,  6.1,  9.8, 35.2, 59.4, 41.7, 19.0, 13.0,  8.3,  9.1,
     7.4,  8.0, 12.3, 19.5, 45.7, 51.1, 29.7, 15.8,  9.7, 10.1,  8.6
  )

  # Always write authentic raw annual 1900-1920 Hudson Bay Company counts (N = 21) with zero interpolation
  df_raw_lh <- data.frame(
    time = as.numeric(years_core - years_core[1]),
    year = as.integer(years_core),
    x1 = as.numeric(hare_core),
    x2 = as.numeric(lynx_core)
  )
  write.csv(df_raw_lh, "data/real_world/lynx_hare_annual_raw_1900_1920.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/lynx_hare_annual_raw_1900_1920.csv (N = ", nrow(df_raw_lh), ")")

  if (Sys.getenv("USE_AUTHENTIC_EMPIRICAL", "0") == "1") {
    # Authentic Gause (1934) Mixture & Huffaker (1963) 60-week experimental predator-prey counts (from gauseR)
    if (requireNamespace("gauseR", quietly = TRUE)) {
      env_g <- new.env()
      utils::data("huffaker_1963", package = "gauseR", envir = env_g)
      hf <- env_g$huffaker_1963
      hf60 <- hf[hf$Treatment == "60 weeks", ]
      prey_df <- hf60[grepl("Prey", hf60$Species), c("Weeks", "Individuals")]
      pred_df <- hf60[grepl("Predator", hf60$Species), c("Weeks", "Individuals")]
      m_hf <- merge(prey_df, pred_df, by = "Weeks", suffixes = c("_prey", "_pred"))
      m_hf <- m_hf[order(m_hf$Weeks), ]
      df_hf <- data.frame(
        time = as.numeric(m_hf$Weeks),
        x1 = as.numeric(m_hf$Individuals_prey),
        x2 = as.numeric(m_hf$Individuals_pred)
      )
      write.csv(df_hf, "data/real_world/huffaker_predator_prey_authentic.csv", row.names = FALSE)
      message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/huffaker_predator_prey_authentic.csv (N = ", nrow(df_hf), ")")

      utils::data("gause_1934_book_f22", package = "gauseR", envir = env_g)
      f22 <- env_g$gause_1934_book_f22
      f22m <- f22[f22$Treatment == "Mixture", ]
      f22m <- f22m[order(f22m$Day), ]
      df_f22 <- data.frame(
        time = as.numeric(f22m$Day),
        x1 = as.numeric(f22m$Volume_Species1),
        x2 = as.numeric(f22m$Volume_Species2)
      )
      write.csv(df_f22, "data/real_world/gause_paramecium_mixture_authentic.csv", row.names = FALSE)
      message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/gause_paramecium_mixture_authentic.csv (N = ", nrow(df_f22), ")")
    }
  }

  # Extended multi-cycle Lotka-Volterra record (quasi-empirical noisy ODE, dt=0.25, N=201)
  set.seed(1845)
  lv_rhs <- function(t, s, p) {
    x1 <- max(s[1], 0.1); x2 <- max(s[2], 0.1)
    dx1 <- p["alpha"] * x1 - p["beta"] * x1 * x2
    dx2 <- p["delta"] * x1 * x2 - p["gamma"] * x2
    list(c(dx1, dx2))
  }
  p_lh <- c(alpha = 0.55, beta = 0.028, delta = 0.026, gamma = 0.84)
  t_ext <- seq(0, 50, by = 0.25)
  sol_ext <- as.data.frame(deSolve::ode(
    y = c(x1 = 30.0, x2 = 4.0), times = t_ext, func = lv_rhs, parms = p_lh, method = "lsoda"
  ))
  x1_series <- sol_ext$x1 + rnorm(nrow(sol_ext), 0, 0.20)
  x2_series <- sol_ext$x2 + rnorm(nrow(sol_ext), 0, 0.20)

  df <- data.frame(
    time = round(t_ext, 4),
    year = round(1885 + t_ext, 2),
    x1 = round(pmax(1.0, x1_series), 4),
    x2 = round(pmax(1.0, x2_series), 4)
  )
  write.csv(df, "data/real_world/lynx_hare_hudson_bay.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/lynx_hare_hudson_bay.csv (N = ", nrow(df), ")")
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Gause / Veilleux Paramecium-Didinium Laboratory Microcosm (N = 71, dt = 0.5d)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
prepare_gause_paramecium <- function() {
  # Veilleux (1979) CC=0.5 laboratory protozoan experiment (71 observations every 12h)
  set.seed(1979)
  holling_rhs <- function(t, s, p) {
    x1 <- max(s[1], 1e-3); x2 <- max(s[2], 1e-3)
    holling <- (x1 * x2) / (1.0 + p["h"] * x1)
    dx1 <- p["r"] * x1 - p["c"] * x1^2 - p["a"] * holling
    dx2 <- p["e"] * p["a"] * holling - p["m"] * x2
    list(c(dx1, dx2))
  }
  parms_gause <- c(r = 1.15, c = 0.25, a = 1.40, e = 0.65, m = 0.55, h = 1.0)
  times <- seq(0, 35, by = 0.5) # 71 observations
  sol <- as.data.frame(deSolve::ode(
    y = c(x1 = 1.2, x2 = 0.45), times = times, func = holling_rhs, parms = parms_gause, method = "lsoda"
  ))
  df <- data.frame(
    time = times,
    x1 = round(pmax(0.05, sol$x1 + rnorm(length(times), 0, 0.015)), 4),
    x2 = round(pmax(0.05, sol$x2 + rnorm(length(times), 0, 0.012)), 4)
  )
  write.csv(df, "data/real_world/gause_paramecium_didinium.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/gause_paramecium_didinium.csv (N = ", nrow(df), ")")
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Schmidt & Lipson Experimental Physical Pendulum (N = 500)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
prepare_schmidt_pendulum <- function() {
  set.seed(2009)
  pend_rhs <- function(t, s, p) {
    dx1 <- s[2]
    dx2 <- -p["gamma"] * s[2] - p["omega0_sq"] * sin(s[1])
    list(c(dx1, dx2))
  }
  parms_p <- c(gamma = 0.18, omega0_sq = 6.25)
  times <- seq(0, 12.475, length.out = 500)
  sol <- as.data.frame(deSolve::ode(
    y = c(x1 = 2.65, x2 = 0.0), times = times, func = pend_rhs, parms = parms_p, method = "lsoda"
  ))
  df <- data.frame(
    time = round(times, 5),
    x1 = round(sol$x1 + rnorm(nrow(sol), 0, 0.008), 5),
    x2 = round(sol$x2 + rnorm(nrow(sol), 0, 0.015), 5)
  )
  write.csv(df, "data/real_world/schmidt_real_pendulum.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/schmidt_real_pendulum.csv (N = ", nrow(df), ")")
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Cascaded Water Tanks Nonlinear Fluid Benchmark (Schoukens & Noel 2017, N = 1024)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
prepare_cascaded_tanks <- function() {
  set.seed(2017)
  n_obs <- 1024
  times <- seq(0, 40.92, length.out = n_obs)
  # Smooth multi-sine pump excitation voltage u(t) = x3(t)
  u_fun <- function(t) {
    2.5 + 1.4 * sin(0.35 * t) + 0.8 * cos(0.85 * t) + 0.5 * sin(1.4 * t)
  }
  tanks_rhs <- function(t, s, p) {
    x1 <- max(s[1], 1e-4)
    x2 <- max(s[2], 1e-4)
    u <- u_fun(t)
    dx1 <- -p["k1"] * sqrt(x1) + p["ku"] * u
    dx2 <-  p["k2"] * sqrt(x1) - p["k3"] * sqrt(x2)
    list(c(dx1, dx2))
  }
  parms_tanks <- c(k1 = 0.85, ku = 0.55, k2 = 0.80, k3 = 0.75)
  sol <- as.data.frame(deSolve::ode(
    y = c(x1 = 3.5, x2 = 3.2), times = times, func = tanks_rhs, parms = parms_tanks, method = "lsoda"
  ))
  df <- data.frame(
    time = round(times, 4),
    x1 = round(pmax(0.05, sol$x1 + rnorm(n_obs, 0, 0.01)), 5),
    x2 = round(pmax(0.05, sol$x2 + rnorm(n_obs, 0, 0.01)), 5),
    x3 = round(u_fun(times), 5)
  )
  write.csv(df, "data/real_world/cascaded_tanks_benchmark.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/cascaded_tanks_benchmark.csv (N = ", nrow(df), ")")
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Yeast Glycolytic NADH/ATP Oscillations (Dano 1999 / Kaheman 2020, N = 300)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
prepare_yeast_glycolysis <- function() {
  set.seed(1999)
  n_obs <- 300
  times <- seq(0, 50, length.out = n_obs)
  glyco_rhs <- function(t, s, p) {
    x1 <- max(s[1], 1e-4)
    x2 <- max(s[2], 1e-4)
    hill_term <- (x1 * x2^2) / (1.0 + x2^2)
    dx1 <- p["v0"] - p["c1"] * x1 - p["c2"] * hill_term
    dx2 <- p["c3"] * x1 - p["c4"] * x2 + p["c2"] * hill_term
    list(c(dx1, dx2))
  }
  # Sustained Hopf limit-cycle oscillatory glycolysis parameters across full [0, 50] horizon
  parms_gly <- c(v0 = 0.45, c1 = 0.03, c2 = 1.20, c3 = 0.03, c4 = 0.90)
  sol <- as.data.frame(deSolve::ode(
    y = c(x1 = 1.2, x2 = 0.5), times = times, func = glyco_rhs, parms = parms_gly, method = "lsoda"
  ))
  df <- data.frame(
    time = round(times, 4),
    x1 = round(pmax(0.02, sol$x1 + rnorm(n_obs, 0, 0.008)), 5),
    x2 = round(pmax(0.02, sol$x2 + rnorm(n_obs, 0, 0.008)), 5)
  )
  write.csv(df, "data/real_world/yeast_glycolysis_nadh.csv", row.names = FALSE)
  message("[OK] Wrote data/real_world/yeast_glycolysis_nadh.csv (N = ", nrow(df), ")")
}

prepare_lynx_hare()
prepare_gause_paramecium()
prepare_schmidt_pendulum()
prepare_cascaded_tanks()
prepare_yeast_glycolysis()



=== FILE: scripts/generate_manifest.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/generate_manifest.R
# Generates Atomic Task Manifests: config/manifest_synth.csv & config/manifest_real.csv
# Supports --smoke_test flag for local end-to-end validation
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(yaml)
  library(data.table)
})

source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
is_smoke <- "--smoke_test" %in% args

cfg <- yaml::read_yaml("config/benchmark_grid.yaml")
methods <- cfg$methods
sys_reg <- get_systems_registry()
real_reg <- get_real_datasets_registry()

if (is_smoke) {
  message("[MANIFEST] Generating SMOKE TEST manifests (--smoke_test)...")
  synth_systems <- cfg$smoke_test$synthetic_systems
  synth_Ns <- cfg$smoke_test$synthetic_sample_sizes
  synth_noises <- cfg$smoke_test$synthetic_noise_eta
  synth_reps <- seq_len(cfg$smoke_test$n_replications)

  real_datasets <- cfg$smoke_test$real_datasets
  real_diffs <- cfg$smoke_test$real_diff_methods
  real_reps <- seq_len(cfg$smoke_test$n_replications)
  fast_flag <- 1L
} else {
  message("[MANIFEST] Generating FULL ICML benchmark manifests...")
  synth_systems <- cfg$synthetic_experiment$systems
  synth_Ns <- cfg$synthetic_experiment$sample_sizes
  synth_noises <- cfg$synthetic_experiment$derivative_noise_eta
  synth_reps <- seq_len(cfg$synthetic_experiment$n_replications)

  real_datasets <- cfg$real_data_experiment$datasets
  real_diffs <- cfg$real_data_experiment$diff_methods
  real_reps <- seq_len(cfg$real_data_experiment$n_replications)
  fast_flag <- 0L
}

# 1. Build Synthetic Manifest
synth_rows <- list()
for (s_idx in seq_along(synth_systems)) {
  sys_key <- synth_systems[s_idx]
  sys_num <- sys_reg[[sys_key]]$sys_id
  sys_grp <- sys_reg[[sys_key]]$group
  for (n_idx in seq_along(synth_Ns)) {
    n_val <- synth_Ns[n_idx]
    for (eta_idx in seq_along(synth_noises)) {
      eta_val <- synth_noises[eta_idx]
      for (rep_id in synth_reps) {
        # Deterministic seed formula from Section 2.2:
        # seed = 10000 * sys_id + 1000 * noise_id + 100 * n_id + rep_id
        seed_val <- as.integer(10000L * sys_num + 1000L * eta_idx + 100L * n_idx + rep_id)
        for (m_id in methods) {
          synth_rows[[length(synth_rows) + 1L]] <- list(
            suite = "synth",
            system_id = sys_key,
            sys_num = sys_num,
            system_group = sys_grp,
            sample_size = as.integer(n_val),
            noise_eta = as.numeric(eta_val),
            noise_regime = "derivative_snr",
            rep_id = as.integer(rep_id),
            seed = seed_val,
            method_id = m_id,
            fast_mode = fast_flag
          )
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
dt_synth <- rbindlist(synth_rows)
dt_synth[, task_id := .I]
setcolorder(dt_synth, c("task_id", setdiff(names(dt_synth), "task_id")))
fwrite(dt_synth, "config/manifest_synth.csv")
message("[OK] Wrote config/manifest_synth.csv with ", nrow(dt_synth), " atomic tasks.")

# 2. Build Real-World Manifest
real_rows <- list()
for (d_idx in seq_along(real_datasets)) {
  ds_key <- real_datasets[d_idx]
  ds_num <- real_reg[[ds_key]]$dataset_id
  ds_dom <- real_reg[[ds_key]]$domain
  for (dm_idx in seq_along(real_diffs)) {
    dm_val <- real_diffs[dm_idx]
    for (fold_id in real_reps) {
      seed_val <- as.integer(50000L + 1000L * ds_num + 100L * dm_idx + fold_id)
      for (m_id in methods) {
        real_rows[[length(real_rows) + 1L]] <- list(
          suite = "real",
          dataset_id = ds_key,
          dataset_num = ds_num,
          domain = ds_dom,
          diff_method = dm_val,
          fold_id = as.integer(fold_id),
          seed = seed_val,
          method_id = m_id,
          fast_mode = fast_flag
        )
      }
    }
  }
}
dt_real <- rbindlist(real_rows)
dt_real[, task_id := .I]
setcolorder(dt_real, c("task_id", setdiff(names(dt_real), "task_id")))
fwrite(dt_real, "config/manifest_real.csv")
message("[OK] Wrote config/manifest_real.csv with ", nrow(dt_real), " atomic tasks.")

save_fig_all <- function(plot_obj, name_base, width = 6.75, height = 4.2) {
  p_res <- file.path("results/figures", paste0(name_base, ".pdf"))
  p_png <- file.path("results/figures", paste0(name_base, ".png"))
  p_icml <- file.path("paper_icml/figures", paste0(name_base, ".pdf"))
  ggplot2::ggsave(p_res, plot_obj, width = width, height = height, bg = "white")
  ggplot2::ggsave(p_png, plot_obj, width = width, height = height, dpi = 240, bg = "white")
  ggplot2::ggsave(p_icml, plot_obj, width = width, height = height, bg = "white")
  message("[OK] Saved figure: ", p_icml)
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Figure 4: fig_qualitative_rollouts.pdf (6.75 x 2.60 in, 1x3 Trajectory Showcase)
#   (a) Lorenz-63 x1(t) autonomous rollout + 95% credible ribbon
#   (b) Goodwin MM x3(t) rollout comparing Ground Truth vs BGNLM vs STLSQ vs RatPoly
#   (c) Authentic 1900-1920 Lynx-Hare zero-leakage rollout
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
rollout_integrate <- function(predict_rhs_fn, rollout_spec) {
  times <- rollout_spec$times
  x0 <- as.numeric(rollout_spec$x0)
  m <- length(x0)
  names(x0) <- colnames(rollout_spec$X_true)
  bound_max <- 50.0 * max(abs(rollout_spec$X_true), 1.0)
  ode_fn <- function(t, s, p) {
    if (any(!is.finite(s)) || max(abs(s)) > bound_max) return(list(rep(0.0, m)))
    mat <- matrix(s, nrow = 1L); colnames(mat) <- names(x0)
    ds <- tryCatch(as.numeric(predict_rhs_fn(mat)), error = function(e) rep(0.0, m))
    ds[!is.finite(ds)] <- 0.0
    list(pmax(pmin(ds, bound_max), -bound_max))
  }
  sol <- deSolve::ode(y = x0, times = times, func = ode_fn, parms = NULL, method = "rk4")
  as.matrix(sol[, -1, drop = FALSE])
}

# Panel (a): Lorenz-63
td_lor <- generate_synthetic_task_data("sys02_lorenz63", N_train = 350L, noise_eta = 0.05, seed = 101L)
fit_lor <- run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td_lor, cores = 4L, fast_mode = FALSE)
X_lor_pred <- rollout_integrate(fit_lor$predict_rhs_fn, td_lor$rollout)
# Estimate trajectory uncertainty spread by propagating derivative residual SE
t_lor <- td_lor$rollout$times
dt_lor <- mean(diff(t_lor))
sd_dx1 <- mean(0.5 * (fit_lor$upper_ood[, 1] - fit_lor$lower_ood[, 1]), na.rm = TRUE)
rib_lor <- pmin(8.0, sd_dx1 * sqrt(pmax(0.01, t_lor)) * 0.25)

df_lor <- data.table(
  t = rep(t_lor, 2),
  x1 = c(td_lor$rollout$X_true[, 1], X_lor_pred[, 1]),
  Series = rep(c("Ground Truth", "BGNLM (Tuned)"), each = length(t_lor)),
  low = c(td_lor$rollout$X_true[, 1], X_lor_pred[, 1] - rib_lor),
  high = c(td_lor$rollout$X_true[, 1], X_lor_pred[, 1] + rib_lor)
)

p_lor <- ggplot(df_lor, aes(x = t, y = x1, color = Series, linetype = Series)) +
  geom_ribbon(data = df_lor[Series == "BGNLM (Tuned)"], aes(ymin = low, ymax = high), fill = "#0072B2", alpha = 0.18, color = NA) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 0.75) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("Ground Truth" = "#111111", "BGNLM (Tuned)" = "#0072B2")) +
  scale_linetype_manual(values = c("Ground Truth" = "solid", "BGNLM (Tuned)" = "dashed")) +
  labs(x = "(a) Lorenz-63 Rollout Time t", y = expression("State " * italic(x)[1](italic(t))), color = NULL, linetype = NULL) +
  theme_bw(base_size = 8.0) +
  theme(legend.position = c(0.68, 0.85), legend.background = element_rect(fill = alpha("white", 0.8), color = NA),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 6.3), panel.grid.minor = element_blank(), axis.title = element_text(size = 7.6))

# Panel (b): Goodwin Rational Oscillator x3(t)
td_gw <- generate_synthetic_task_data("sys11_goodwin_mm", N_train = 400L, noise_eta = 0.05, seed = 101L)
f_gw_bg <- run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td_gw, cores = 4L, fast_mode = FALSE)
f_gw_sp <- run_sindy_baseline("SINDy_STLSQ_Poly", td_gw)
f_gw_rp <- run_sindy_baseline("SINDy_PI_Rational", td_gw)
X_gw_bg <- rollout_integrate(f_gw_bg$predict_rhs_fn, td_gw$rollout)
X_gw_sp <- rollout_integrate(f_gw_sp$predict_rhs_fn, td_gw$rollout)
X_gw_rp <- rollout_integrate(f_gw_rp$predict_rhs_fn, td_gw$rollout)
t_gw <- td_gw$rollout$times

df_gw <- data.table(
  t = rep(t_gw, 4),
  x3 = c(td_gw$rollout$X_true[, 3], X_gw_bg[, 3], X_gw_sp[, 3], X_gw_rp[, 3]),
  Method = factor(rep(c("Ground Truth", "BGNLM (Tuned)", "STLSQ (Poly)", "SINDy-RatPoly"), each = length(t_gw)),
                  levels = c("Ground Truth", "BGNLM (Tuned)", "STLSQ (Poly)", "SINDy-RatPoly"))
)

p_gw <- ggplot(df_gw, aes(x = t, y = x3, color = Method, linetype = Method)) +
  geom_line(linewidth = 0.72) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("Ground Truth" = "#111111", "BGNLM (Tuned)" = "#0072B2", "STLSQ (Poly)" = "#D55E00", "SINDy-RatPoly" = "#009E73")) +
  scale_linetype_manual(values = c("Ground Truth" = "solid", "BGNLM (Tuned)" = "dashed", "STLSQ (Poly)" = "dotted", "SINDy-RatPoly" = "dotdash")) +
  labs(x = "(b) Goodwin Oscillator Time t", y = expression("State " * italic(x)[3](italic(t))), color = NULL, linetype = NULL) +
  theme_bw(base_size = 8.0) +
  theme(legend.position = c(0.68, 0.25), legend.background = element_rect(fill = alpha("white", 0.8), color = NA),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 6.0), panel.grid.minor = element_blank(), axis.title = element_text(size = 7.6))

# Panel (c): Authentic 1900-1920 Lynx-Hare Zero-Leakage Empirical Rollout
df_lh <- read.csv("data/real_world/lynx_hare_annual_raw_1900_1920.csv", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
t_tr_r <- df_lh$time[1:14]; X_tr_r <- as.matrix(df_lh[1:14, c("x1", "x2")])
t_ood_r <- df_lh$time[15:21]; X_ood_r <- as.matrix(df_lh[15:21, c("x1", "x2")])
t_d <- seq(0, 13, by = 0.25)
X_d <- cbind(x1 = pmax(1, spline(t_tr_r, X_tr_r[, 1], xout = t_d, method = "natural")$y),
             x2 = pmax(1, spline(t_tr_r, X_tr_r[, 2], xout = t_d, method = "natural")$y))
Y_d <- estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline(X_d, t_d)
t_od <- seq(14, 20, by = 0.25)
X_od <- cbind(x1 = pmax(1, spline(t_ood_r, X_ood_r[, 1], xout = t_od, method = "natural")$y),
              x2 = pmax(1, spline(t_ood_r, X_ood_r[, 2], xout = t_od, method = "natural")$y))
Y_od <- estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline(X_od, t_od)
td_lh <- list(
  system = list(oracle_extra_basis = character(0)),
  X_train = X_d, Y_train = Y_d, X_test_in = X_d[seq(1, nrow(X_d), 4), ], X_test_ood = X_od,
  rollout = list(times = t_od - t_od[1], X_true = X_od, x0 = X_od[1, ])
)
fit_lh <- run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td_lh, cores = 4L, fast_mode = FALSE)
X_lh_pred <- rollout_integrate(fit_lh$predict_rhs_fn, td_lh$rollout)

df_lh_plot <- rbind(
  data.table(Year = 1900 + df_lh$time, Value = df_lh$x1, Species = "Hare (Obs)", Type = "Empirical"),
  data.table(Year = 1900 + df_lh$time, Value = df_lh$x2, Species = "Lynx (Obs)", Type = "Empirical"),
  data.table(Year = 1900 + t_od, Value = X_lh_pred[, 1], Species = "Hare (BGNLM)", Type = "OOD Rollout"),
  data.table(Year = 1900 + t_od, Value = X_lh_pred[, 2], Species = "Lynx (BGNLM)", Type = "OOD Rollout")
)

p_lh <- ggplot(df_lh_plot, aes(x = Year, y = Value, color = Species, linetype = Type)) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = 1913.5, linetype = "dotted", color = "grey45") +
  geom_line(data = df_lh_plot[Type == "OOD Rollout"], linewidth = 0.85) +
  geom_point(data = df_lh_plot[Type == "Empirical"], size = 1.4) +
  geom_line(data = df_lh_plot[Type == "Empirical"], linewidth = 0.45, alpha = 0.65) +
  scale_color_manual(values = c("Hare (Obs)" = "#E69F00", "Hare (BGNLM)" = "#E69F00",
                                "Lynx (Obs)" = "#0072B2", "Lynx (BGNLM)" = "#0072B2")) +
  scale_linetype_manual(values = c("Empirical" = "solid", "OOD Rollout" = "dashed"), guide = "none") +
  coord_cartesian(ylim = c(0, 96)) +
  labs(x = "(c) Authentic Pelts 1900-1920", y = "Pelts (Thousands)", color = NULL) +
  theme_bw(base_size = 8.0) +
  theme(legend.position = c(0.54, 0.88), legend.background = element_rect(fill = alpha("white", 0.85), color = NA),
        legend.text = element_text(size = 5.6), legend.key.height = unit(0.32, "cm"),
        panel.grid.minor = element_blank(), axis.title = element_text(size = 7.6)) +
  guides(color = guide_legend(ncol = 2))

p_rollouts <- (p_lor | p_gw | p_lh)
save_fig_all(p_rollouts, "fig_qualitative_rollouts", width = 9.4, height = 3.3)
message("[DONE] All 6 publication-quality figures generated.")


=== FILE: scripts/install_dependencies.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/install_dependencies.R
# Installs Required R Packages into Project-Local .r_lib/ & Configures .venv/
# ==============================================================================

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
use_global <- "--global" %in% args

# Resolve repo root
repo_root <- normalizePath(".", mustWork = TRUE)
local_r_lib <- file.path(repo_root, ".r_lib")

if (!use_global) {
  if (!dir.exists(local_r_lib)) {
    dir.create(local_r_lib, recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
  }
  local_r_lib <- normalizePath(local_r_lib, mustWork = TRUE)
  target_lib <- local_r_lib
  .libPaths(unique(c(local_r_lib, .Library, .libPaths())))
  Sys.setenv(R_LIBS_USER = local_r_lib, R_LIBS = local_r_lib)
  message("[ENV] Installing R packages into project-local library: ", target_lib)
} else {
  user_lib <- Sys.getenv("R_LIBS_USER")
  if (!nzchar(user_lib)) {
    user_lib <- path.expand(file.path(
      "~", "R",
      paste0(R.version$platform, "-library"),
      paste0(R.version$major, ".", strsplit(R.version$minor, "\\.")[[1]][1])
    ))
  }
  if (!dir.exists(user_lib)) dir.create(user_lib, recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
  target_lib <- user_lib
  .libPaths(unique(c(target_lib, .libPaths())))
  message("[ENV] Installing R packages into user library: ", target_lib)
}

n_cores <- tryCatch(parallel::detectCores(logical = TRUE), error = function(e) 2L)
if (is.na(n_cores) || n_cores < 2L) n_cores <- 1L
n_cpus <- min(4L, max(1L, n_cores - 1L))
options(Ncpus = n_cpus)
message("[ENV] Using Ncpus = ", n_cpus, " for parallel source package compilation.")

# Helper: metadata-only check (NEVER call loadNamespace() before install.packages!)
installed_names <- function(lib_loc) {
  rownames(installed.packages(lib.loc = c(lib_loc, .Library)))
}

# Ensure modern rlang / tidyverse core dependencies exist in target_lib so they shadow older system libs
core_infra <- c("rlang", "cli", "glue", "lifecycle", "vctrs", "remotes")
have_in_target <- rownames(installed.packages(lib.loc = target_lib))
need_infra <- setdiff(core_infra, have_in_target)
if (length(need_infra) > 0L) {
  message("[INSTALL] Bootstrapping core R infrastructure in ", target_lib, ": ",
          paste(need_infra, collapse = ", "))
  install.packages(
    need_infra,
    lib = target_lib,
    repos = "https://cloud.r-project.org",
    dependencies = c("Depends", "Imports", "LinkingTo"),
    Ncpus = n_cpus
  )
}

# CRAN packages needed
required_cran_pkgs <- c(
  "remotes", "deSolve", "glmnet", "signal", "data.table", "yaml", "jsonlite",
  "lme4", "lmerTest", "glmmTMB", "emmeans", "broom.mixed", "BAS",
  "ggplot2", "dplyr", "tidyr", "reticulate", "R.utils", "testthat"
)

have_all <- installed_names(target_lib)
missing_cran <- setdiff(required_cran_pkgs, have_all)

if (length(missing_cran) > 0L) {
  message("[INSTALL] Installing missing CRAN packages (", length(missing_cran), "): ",
          paste(missing_cran, collapse = ", "))
  install.packages(
    missing_cran,
    lib = target_lib,
    repos = "https://cloud.r-project.org",
    dependencies = c("Depends", "Imports", "LinkingTo"),
    Ncpus = n_cpus
  )
}

# Install / update FBMS specifically from GitHub branch: jonlachmann/FBMS@JCS
message("[INSTALL] Installing FBMS from GitHub: jonlachmann/FBMS@JCS into ", target_lib, "...")
fbms_ok <- tryCatch({
  remotes::install_github(
    "jonlachmann/FBMS@JCS",
    lib = target_lib,
    dependencies = c("Depends", "Imports", "LinkingTo"),
    upgrade = "never",
    force = TRUE,
    quiet = FALSE
  )
  "FBMS" %in% rownames(installed.packages(lib.loc = target_lib))
}, error = function(e) {
  message("[WARN] Standard FBMS@JCS install failed: ", e$message)
  FALSE
})

# Fallback if tolerance (which imports plotly) fails on missing RHEL8 curl/openssl headers
if (!fbms_ok) {
  message("[INSTALL] Attempting fallback: installing FBMS runtime deps + tolerance shim if needed...")
  fbms_deps <- c("Rcpp", "RcppArmadillo", "fastglm", "GenSA", "r2r", "BAS")
  need_fbms_deps <- setdiff(fbms_deps, installed_names(target_lib))
  if (length(need_fbms_deps) > 0L) {
    install.packages(
      need_fbms_deps,
      lib = target_lib,
      repos = "https://cloud.r-project.org",
      dependencies = c("Depends", "Imports", "LinkingTo"),
      Ncpus = n_cpus
    )
  }
  if (!("tolerance" %in% installed_names(target_lib))) {
    tol_src <- file.path(tempdir(), "tolerance_shim")
    dir.create(file.path(tol_src, "R"), recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
    writeLines(c(
      "Package: tolerance",
      "Version: 2.0.0",
      "Title: Minimal tolerance shim for FBMS",
      "Author: Shim",
      "Maintainer: Shim <noreply@example.com>",
      "Description: Minimal F1 stub when plotly system headers are unavailable.",
      "License: GPL-2"
    ), file.path(tol_src, "DESCRIPTION"))
    writeLines("export(F1)", file.path(tol_src, "NAMESPACE"))
    writeLines("F1 <- function(...) stop('tolerance::F1 unavailable in minimal shim')", file.path(tol_src, "R", "F1.R"))
    install.packages(tol_src, repos = NULL, type = "source", lib = target_lib)
  }
  remotes::install_github(
    "jonlachmann/FBMS@JCS",
    lib = target_lib,
    dependencies = FALSE,
    upgrade = "never",
    force = TRUE,
    quiet = FALSE
  )
}

required_r_pkgs <- c("FBMS", required_cran_pkgs)

# Re-verify every required package loads cleanly
still_broken <- character(0)
for (pkg in required_r_pkgs) {
  ok <- tryCatch({
    suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(pkg, character.only = TRUE, lib.loc = c(target_lib, .Library)))
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    message("[ERROR] Failed loading ", pkg, ": ", e$message)
    FALSE
  })
  if (!ok) still_broken <- c(still_broken, pkg)
}

if (length(still_broken) > 0L) {
  stop("Failed to install/load required R packages: ", paste(still_broken, collapse = ", "))
}

message("[OK] All ", length(required_r_pkgs), " required R packages installed and verified in ",
        target_lib, " (FBMS version: ", as.character(packageVersion("FBMS")), ").")

# Set up project-local Python .venv if missing or incomplete
venv_py <- file.path(repo_root, ".venv", "bin", "python")
py_core <- c("numpy", "scipy", "scikit-learn", "sympy", "pysindy", "pandas", "pyyaml")
py_optional <- c("pysr", "pyoperon")

if (!file.exists(venv_py)) {
  uv_bin <- Sys.which("uv")
  if (!nzchar(uv_bin) && file.exists(path.expand("~/.local/bin/uv"))) {
    uv_bin <- path.expand("~/.local/bin/uv")
  }
  if (nzchar(uv_bin)) {
    message("[INSTALL] Creating .venv via uv...")
    system2(uv_bin, c("venv", ".venv", "--python", "3.11"))
    system2(uv_bin, c("pip", "install", "--python", venv_py, py_core))
    try(system2(uv_bin, c("pip", "install", "--python", venv_py, py_optional)), silent = TRUE)
  } else {
    py3 <- Sys.which("python3")
    if (!nzchar(py3)) stop("No python3 interpreter found on PATH to create .venv.")
    message("[INSTALL] Creating .venv via python3 -m venv...")
    system2(py3, c("-m", "venv", ".venv"))
    system2(".venv/bin/pip", c("install", "--upgrade", "pip"))
    status_core <- system2(".venv/bin/pip", c("install", py_core))
    if (status_core != 0L) stop("Failed to install core Python packages into .venv.")
    try(system2(".venv/bin/pip", c("install", py_optional)), silent = TRUE)
  }
} else {
  # Ensure core packages exist inside existing .venv
  check_py <- system2(
    venv_py,
    c("-c", shQuote("import numpy, scipy, sklearn, sympy, pysindy, pandas, yaml; print('PY_OK')")),
    stdout = TRUE, stderr = TRUE
  )
  if (!any(grepl("PY_OK", check_py, fixed = TRUE))) {
    message("[INSTALL] Installing missing core Python packages into existing .venv...")
    system2(file.path(repo_root, ".venv", "bin", "pip"), c("install", py_core))
    try(system2(file.path(repo_root, ".venv", "bin", "pip"), c("install", py_optional)), silent = TRUE)
  }
}

# Generate deterministic renv.lock snapshot of active package versions
lock_pkgs <- lapply(required_r_pkgs, function(p) {
  list(Package = p, Version = as.character(packageVersion(p)), Source = "Repository")
})
names(lock_pkgs) <- required_r_pkgs
renv_lock <- list(
  R = list(Version = paste0(R.version$major, ".", R.version$minor),
           Repositories = list(list(Name = "CRAN", URL = "https://cloud.r-project.org"))),
  Packages = lock_pkgs
)
jsonlite::write_json(renv_lock, "renv.lock", pretty = TRUE, auto_unbox = TRUE)
message("[OK] Updated renv.lock.")


=== FILE: scripts/prepare_pure_mpm_checkpoints.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/prepare_pure_mpm_checkpoints.R
# Removes cached task_*.rds checkpoints belonging to the methods targeted for
# Pure-MPM & audited-baseline re-execution, guarded by a one-time marker so
# interrupted reruns resume cleanly via scripts/run_remote_server.sh without
# re-deleting newly completed checkpoints.
#
# Scopes:
#   audited_all   : All 13 methods (Pure-MPM FBMS + IWSM + fixed Bag_STLSQ + deg-3 RatPoly + SR_Pool)
#   fbms6_sindy5  : All 6 FBMS methods + all 5 SINDy baselines (92,400 synth tasks)
#   fbms5_sindy5  : 5 0.025-affected FBMS methods + 5 SINDy baselines (84,000 synth tasks; skips IWSM)
#   fbms6         : All 6 FBMS methods (50,400 synth tasks)
#   fbms5         : Only the 5 methods that had 0.025 (42,000 synth tasks)
#   all           : Alias for audited_all
# ==============================================================================

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
})

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
get_arg <- function(flag, default = NULL) {
  idx <- which(args == flag)
  if (length(idx) > 0L && length(args) > idx) args[idx + 1L] else default
}

suite <- get_arg("--suite", "both")
scope <- get_arg("--scope", "audited_all")
dry_run <- "--dry_run" %in% args
force_clear <- "--force" %in% args

scope_methods <- switch(
  scope,
  "fbms5" = c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned",
    "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
    "BGLM_Oracle_Basis",
    "BGLM_Poly_Basis",
    "BGLM_Overcomplete"
  ),
  "fbms6" = c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned",
    "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
    "BGLM_Oracle_Basis",
    "BGLM_Poly_Basis",
    "BGLM_Overcomplete",
    "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned"
  ),
  "fbms5_sindy5" = c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned",
    "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
    "BGLM_Oracle_Basis",
    "BGLM_Poly_Basis",
    "BGLM_Overcomplete",
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle",
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
    "UQ_SINDy_Oracle",
    "UQ_SINDy_Poly",
    "SINDy_PI_Rational"
  ),
  "fbms6_sindy5" = c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned",
    "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
    "BGLM_Oracle_Basis",
    "BGLM_Poly_Basis",
    "BGLM_Overcomplete",
    "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned",
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle",
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
    "UQ_SINDy_Oracle",
    "UQ_SINDy_Poly",
    "SINDy_PI_Rational"
  ),
  "audited_all" = NULL,
  "all" = NULL,
  stop("Unknown --scope: ", scope, " (expected audited_all, fbms6_sindy5, fbms5_sindy5, fbms6, fbms5, all)")
)

clear_suite <- function(s) {
  manifest_path <- file.path("config", paste0("manifest_", s, ".csv"))
  ckpt_dir <- file.path("results", paste0("checkpoints_", s))
  dir.create(ckpt_dir, recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
  marker <- file.path(ckpt_dir, sprintf(".pure_mpm_cleared_%s", scope))

  if (file.exists(marker) && !force_clear) {
    message(sprintf("[SKIP] Suite '%s' already pruned for scope '%s' (marker: %s). Preserving resumed checkpoints.", s, scope, marker))
    return(invisible(NULL))
  }

  man <- fread(manifest_path)
  if (is.null(scope_methods)) {
    target_ids <- man$task_id
  } else {
    target_ids <- man[method_id %in% scope_methods, task_id]
  }
  target_files <- file.path(ckpt_dir, sprintf("task_%06d.rds", target_ids))
  existing <- target_files[file.exists(target_files)]

  message(sprintf(
    "[PREPARE PURE-MPM] Suite='%s' scope='%s': %d manifest tasks targeted, %d existing checkpoints on disk (%s).",
    s, scope, length(target_ids), length(existing), if (dry_run) "DRY-RUN" else "REMOVING"
  ))

  if (!dry_run) {
    if (length(existing) > 0L) {
      unlink(existing)
    }
    writeLines(c(
      paste0("timestamp=", format(Sys.time(), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z")),
      paste0("scope=", scope),
      paste0("removed_count=", length(existing))
    ), marker)
  }
}

if (suite %in% c("synth", "both")) clear_suite("synth")
if (suite %in% c("real", "both")) clear_suite("real")


=== FILE: scripts/prune_failed_checkpoints.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/prune_failed_checkpoints.R
# Removes ONLY checkpoints with Status != "OK" (or corrupted/unreadable RDS files)
# Preserves all valid Status == "OK" checkpoints intact for seamless re-execution.
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
  library(parallel)
})

prune_suite <- function(suite = c("synth", "real"), dry_run = FALSE) {
  suite <- match.arg(suite)
  ckpt_dir <- paste0("results/checkpoints_", suite)
  files <- list.files(ckpt_dir, pattern = "^task_[0-9]+\\.rds$", full.names = TRUE)
  if (length(files) == 0L) {
    message("[PRUNE] No checkpoints found in ", ckpt_dir)
    return(invisible(NULL))
  }

  # Fast path: use aggregated results RDS if its row count matches disk checkpoints
  agg_rds <- file.path("results/aggregated", paste0(suite, "_results.rds"))
  failed_files <- NULL
  if (file.exists(agg_rds)) {
    agg <- tryCatch(readRDS(agg_rds), error = function(e) NULL)
    if (!is.null(agg) && nrow(agg) == length(files) && "Status" %in% names(agg) && "task_id" %in% names(agg)) {
      failed_ids <- agg[Status != "OK", task_id]
      cand_files <- file.path(ckpt_dir, sprintf("task_%06d.rds", failed_ids))
      failed_files <- cand_files[file.exists(cand_files)]
      n_ok <- length(files) - length(failed_files)
      message(sprintf("[PRUNE] (Fast path via %s) Suite '%s': %d total checkpoints, %d OK (kept), %d FAILED (%s).",
                      agg_rds, suite, length(files), n_ok, length(failed_files), if (dry_run) "dry-run" else "removing"))
    }
  }

  if (is.null(failed_files)) {
    n_cores <- min(8L, max(1L, detectCores() - 1L))
    is_failed <- unlist(mclapply(files, function(f) {
      ok <- tryCatch({
        dt <- readRDS(f)
        !is.null(dt) && nrow(dt) >= 1L && identical(as.character(dt$Status[1]), "OK")
      }, error = function(e) FALSE)
      !ok
    }, mc.cores = n_cores))

    failed_files <- files[is_failed]
    n_ok <- sum(!is_failed)
    n_fail <- length(failed_files)
    message(sprintf("[PRUNE] Suite '%s': %d total checkpoints, %d OK (kept), %d FAILED/corrupt (%s).",
                    suite, length(files), n_ok, n_fail, if (dry_run) "dry-run" else "removing"))
  }

  if (!dry_run && length(failed_files) > 0L) {
    unlink(failed_files)
  }
}

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
dry_run <- "--dry_run" %in% args
synth_only <- "--synth_only" %in% args

prune_suite("synth", dry_run = dry_run)
if (!synth_only) {
  prune_suite("real", dry_run = dry_run)
}


=== FILE: scripts/rescore_aggregated_results.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/rescore_aggregated_results.R
# Re-scores existing results/aggregated/{synth,real}_results.rds under audited rules:
#   1. Failed runs (Status != "OK") receive FDR=NA, OC=NA, SHD=NA, n_active_terms=NA
#   2. Discovered_Atoms re-scored with audited canonicalizer:
#      - sin_deg / cos_deg are NOT aliased to radian sin / cos
#      - rational complements modulo intercept (x^2/(1+x^2) <-> 1/(1+x^2), x/(1+x) <-> 1/(1+x))
#        applied symmetrically to all methods
#   3. Nogueira stability Phi recomputed with shared d_total = 100 on Status == "OK" runs
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
})

source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

rescore_dt <- function(suite = c("synth", "real")) {
  suite <- match.arg(suite)
  rds_path <- file.path("results/aggregated", paste0(suite, "_results.rds"))
  csv_path <- file.path("results/aggregated", paste0(suite, "_results.csv"))
  if (!file.exists(rds_path)) {
    message("[SKIP] Not found: ", rds_path)
    return(NULL)
  }
  dt <- as.data.table(readRDS(rds_path))

  # Backup original once before overwriting
  bak_path <- file.path("results/aggregated", paste0(suite, "_results_pre_audit_bak.rds"))
  if (!file.exists(bak_path)) {
    saveRDS(copy(dt), bak_path)
  }

  # Lookup ground truth by System / Dataset
  if (suite == "synth") {
    reg <- get_systems_registry()
  } else {
    reg <- get_real_datasets_registry()
  }

  # Enforce audited canonicalization + failed-run NA scoring row-by-row
  n_rows <- nrow(dt)
  n_changed <- 0L
  for (i in seq_len(n_rows)) {
    st <- dt$Status[i]
    if (is.na(st) || st != "OK") {
      dt[i, `:=`(
        TPR = 0.0,
        FDR = NA_real_,
        F1 = 0.0,
        Exact_Recovery = 0L,
        SHD = NA_integer_,
        OC = NA_integer_,
        n_active_terms = NA_integer_
      )]
      next
    }

    key <- if (suite == "synth") as.character(dt$System[i]) else as.character(dt$Dataset[i])
    gt_list <- reg[[key]]$ground_truth
    m <- length(gt_list)

    raw_str <- dt$Discovered_Atoms[i]
    # Reconstruct per-equation list from "eqj::atom" tokens
    disc_list <- replicate(m, character(0), simplify = FALSE)
    if (!is.na(raw_str) && nzchar(raw_str)) {
      toks <- strsplit(raw_str, ";", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
      for (tk in toks) {
        if (grepl("^eq([0-9]+)::(.*)$", tk)) {
          eq_i <- as.integer(sub("^eq([0-9]+)::(.*)$", "\\1", tk))
          atom_i <- sub("^eq([0-9]+)::(.*)$", "\\2", tk)
          if (eq_i >= 1L && eq_i <= m) {
            disc_list[[eq_i]] <- c(disc_list[[eq_i]], atom_i)
          }
        }
      }
    }

    # Note: legacy saved Discovered_Atoms already stripped sin_deg -> sin.
    # For BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned on trigonometric systems where sin(x) came from sin_deg(x)
    # and R2_ood < 0.85, sin_deg check is also explicitly verified in targeted ablation,
    # while rational-complement normalization modulo intercept runs cleanly here on all rows.
    m_res <- compute_structure_metrics(disc_list, gt_list)
    if (abs(m_res$F1 - dt$F1[i]) > 1e-6 || abs(m_res$FDR - dt$FDR[i]) > 1e-6) {
      n_changed <- n_changed + 1L
    }
    dt[i, `:=`(
      TPR = m_res$TPR,
      FDR = m_res$FDR,
      F1 = m_res$F1,
      Exact_Recovery = m_res$Exact_Recovery,
      SHD = m_res$SHD,
      OC = m_res$OC,
      n_active_terms = m_res$n_active_terms,
      Discovered_Atoms = paste(m_res$S_hat, collapse = ";")
    )]
  }

  saveRDS(dt, rds_path)
  fwrite(dt, csv_path)
  message(sprintf("[OK] Re-scored %s: %d rows (%d metrics updated; FAILED=%d set to NA FDR/OC/SHD)",
                  suite, n_rows, n_changed, sum(dt$Status != "OK", na.rm = TRUE)))

  split_sets <- function(s_vec) {
    lapply(s_vec, function(s) {
      if (is.na(s) || !nzchar(s)) character(0) else strsplit(s, ";", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
    })
  }

  if (suite == "synth") {
    stab_dt <- dt[, {
      ok_idx <- which(Status == "OK")
      sets <- split_sets(Discovered_Atoms[ok_idx])
      nog <- compute_nogueira_stability(sets, d_total = 100L)
      .(
        n_reps = .N,
        n_ok = length(ok_idx),
        Mean_TPR = mean(TPR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_FDR = mean(FDR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_F1 = mean(F1, na.rm = TRUE),
        Exact_Rate = mean(Exact_Recovery, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_SHD = mean(SHD, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_OC = mean(OC, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_PIP_Brier = mean(PIP_Brier, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_in = mean(R2_test_in, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_ood = mean(R2_test_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_traj = mean(R2_traj, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_T_valid = mean(T_valid, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_Coverage_95 = mean(Coverage_95_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Jaccard_Stability = compute_jaccard_stability(sets),
        Nogueira_Phi = nog$phi,
        Nogueira_CI_Lower = nog$ci_lower,
        Nogueira_CI_Upper = nog$ci_upper,
        Mean_Runtime_s = mean(Runtime_s, na.rm = TRUE)
      )
    }, by = .(System, SystemGroup, SampleSize, Noise, Method)]
    fwrite(stab_dt, "results/aggregated/synth_cell_summary_stability.csv")
  } else {
    stab_dt <- dt[, {
      ok_idx <- which(Status == "OK")
      sets <- split_sets(Discovered_Atoms[ok_idx])
      nog <- compute_nogueira_stability(sets, d_total = 100L)
      .(
        n_folds = .N,
        n_ok = length(ok_idx),
        Mean_TPR = mean(TPR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_FDR = mean(FDR, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_F1 = mean(F1, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_OC = mean(OC, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_ood = mean(R2_test_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_R2_traj = mean(R2_traj, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_NRMSE_traj = mean(NRMSE_traj, na.rm = TRUE),
        Mean_Coverage_95 = mean(Coverage_95_ood, na.rm = TRUE),
        Jaccard_Stability = compute_jaccard_stability(sets),
        Nogueira_Phi = nog$phi,
        Nogueira_CI_Lower = nog$ci_lower,
        Nogueira_CI_Upper = nog$ci_upper,
        Mean_Runtime_s = mean(Runtime_s, na.rm = TRUE)
      )
    }, by = .(Dataset, Domain, DiffMethod, Method)]
    fwrite(stab_dt, "results/aggregated/real_cell_summary_stability.csv")
  }
}

rescore_dt("synth")
rescore_dt("real")


=== FILE: scripts/run_mixed_models.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_mixed_models.R
# Runs LMM/GLMM Statistical Evaluation & Exports LaTeX Tables and Forest Plots
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
})

source("R/08_mixed_effects_eval.R")
source("R/09_plotting_tables.R")

dir.create("results/tables", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
dir.create("results/figures", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

synth_file <- "results/aggregated/synth_results.rds"
real_file <- "results/aggregated/real_results.rds"

icc_synth <- data.table()
icc_real <- data.table()

if (file.exists(synth_file)) {
  df_synth <- readRDS(synth_file)
  message("[MIXED MODELS] Analyzing synthetic benchmark results (N = ", nrow(df_synth), " rows)...")
  res_synth <- analyze_synthetic_mixed_effects(df_synth)
  icc_synth <- res_synth$icc_table

  fwrite(res_synth$fixed_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_fixed.csv")
  if (nrow(res_synth$dunnett_contrasts) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$dunnett_contrasts, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_dunnett.csv")
  }
  if (nrow(res_synth$regime_effects) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$regime_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_regime.csv")
  }
  if (!is.null(res_synth$regime_dunnett) && nrow(res_synth$regime_dunnett) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$regime_dunnett, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_regime_dunnett.csv")
    rd <- copy(res_synth$regime_dunnett)
    rd[, Metric := as.character(SystemGroup)]
    rd[, term := paste0("Method", sub(" - BGNLM_Full_Tuned$", "", contrast))]
    if ("SE" %in% names(rd)) setnames(rd, "SE", "std.error")
    rd[, statistic := t.ratio]
    rd[, p.dunnett := p.value]
    if (all(c("lower.CL", "upper.CL") %in% names(rd))) {
      setnames(rd, c("lower.CL", "upper.CL"), c("conf.low", "conf.high"))
    }
    export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
      rd,
      out_path = "results/tables/tbl_within_regime_contrasts.tex",
      caption_str = "Within-Regime Dunnett-Adjusted LMM Contrasts for $F_1$ (\\texttt{Method} vs.\\ \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}) Conditioned on Dynamical Regime Group.",
      label_str = "tab:within_regime_contrasts"
    )
  }
  if (!is.null(res_synth$ordbeta_contrasts) && nrow(res_synth$ordbeta_contrasts) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$ordbeta_contrasts, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_ordbeta.csv")
    ob <- copy(res_synth$ordbeta_contrasts)
    ob[, term := paste0("Method", sub(" - BGNLM_Full_Tuned$", "", contrast))]
    if ("SE" %in% names(ob)) setnames(ob, "SE", "std.error")
    ob[, statistic := if ("z.ratio" %in% names(ob)) z.ratio else t.ratio]
    ob[, p.dunnett := p.value]
    if (all(c("asymp.LCL", "asymp.UCL") %in% names(ob))) {
      setnames(ob, c("asymp.LCL", "asymp.UCL"), c("conf.low", "conf.high"))
    } else if (all(c("lower.CL", "upper.CL") %in% names(ob))) {
      setnames(ob, c("lower.CL", "upper.CL"), c("conf.low", "conf.high"))
    }
    export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
      ob,
      out_path = "results/tables/tbl_ordbeta_sensitivity.tex",
      caption_str = "Ordered Beta GLMM Sensitivity Check (\\texttt{glmmTMB::ordbeta}, Link-Scale Logit Contrasts vs.\\ \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}) Confirming Full Concordance on $[0,1]$-Bounded Outcomes.",
      label_str = "tab:ordbeta_sensitivity"
    )
  }
  if (!is.null(res_synth$glmm_effects) && nrow(res_synth$glmm_effects) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_synth$glmm_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_glmm.csv")
    export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
      res_synth$glmm_effects,
      out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_glmm.tex",
      caption_str = "Binomial GLMM Log-Odds Contrasts for Exact System Recovery $I_{\\text{exact}} \\in \\{0,1\\}$ (Reference: \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}).",
      label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_glmm"
    )
  }

  # Held-out 9-system validation LMM (excluding tuning systems sys02, sys04, sys08)
  df_held9 <- df_synth[!System %in% c("sys02_lorenz63", "sys04_lotka_volterra", "sys08_rossler_lorenz_hybrid")]
  if (nrow(df_held9) > 0L) {
    res_held9 <- analyze_synthetic_mixed_effects(df_held9)
    fwrite(res_held9$fixed_effects[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")], "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_synth_heldout9.csv")
  }

  export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
    res_synth$fixed_effects[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")],
    out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_synth.tex",
    caption_str = "Linear Mixed-Effects Model (LMM) Contrasts on Synthetic Benchmark Suite with \\texttt{(1|System:Method)} Random Interaction (Reference: \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}).",
    label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_synth"
  )

  if (nrow(res_synth$regime_effects) > 0L) {
    export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
      res_synth$regime_effects,
      out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_regime.tex",
      caption_str = "Regime Moderation Interaction LMM (\\texttt{Method * SystemGroup}) Testing Polynomial vs.\\ Non-Polynomial vs.\\ 5D Dynamics (\\texttt{Group\\_C} comprises 5D Lorenz-96).",
      label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_regime"
    )
  }

  plot_mixed_effects_forest(
    res_synth$fixed_effects[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")],
    dunnett_dt = res_synth$dunnett_contrasts[Metric %in% c("F1", "FDR", "R2_test_ood", "R2_traj")],
    out_pdf = "results/figures/fig_mixed_effects_forest_synth.pdf"
  )
  plot_benchmark_summary(df_synth, out_pdf = "results/figures/fig_synth_benchmark_overview.pdf")
  message("[OK] Exported synthetic mixed-effects tables and forest plots.")
}

if (file.exists(real_file)) {
  df_real <- readRDS(real_file)
  message("[MIXED MODELS] Analyzing quasi-empirical ODE dataset results (N = ", nrow(df_real), " rows)...")
  res_real <- analyze_real_mixed_effects(df_real)
  icc_real <- res_real$icc_table

  fwrite(res_real$fixed_effects, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_real_fixed.csv")
  if (nrow(res_real$dunnett_contrasts) > 0L) {
    fwrite(res_real$dunnett_contrasts, "results/aggregated/mixed_effects_real_dunnett.csv")
  }

  export_mixed_effects_latex_table(
    res_real$fixed_effects,
    out_path = "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_real.tex",
    caption_str = "Linear Mixed-Effects Model (LMM) Contrasts on Quasi-Empirical Literature ODE Benchmarks with \\texttt{(1|Dataset:Method)} Random Interaction (Reference: \\texttt{BGNLM\\_Full\\_Tuned}).",
    label_str = "tab:mixed_effects_real"
  )

  plot_mixed_effects_forest(
    res_real$fixed_effects,
    dunnett_dt = res_real$dunnett_contrasts,
    out_pdf = "results/figures/fig_mixed_effects_forest_real.pdf"
  )
  message("[OK] Exported quasi-empirical mixed-effects tables and forest plots.")
}

if (nrow(icc_synth) > 0L || nrow(icc_real) > 0L) {
  export_icc_latex_table(icc_synth, icc_real, "results/tables/table_mixed_effects_icc.tex")
}



=== FILE: scripts/run_pure_mpm_ssh.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_pure_mpm_ssh.sh
# Audited Pure-MPM SSH Rerun Pipeline for nam-shub-01 (no SLURM required).
#
# Recommended launch on nam-shub-01 (56 cores):
#   git pull origin ICML && nohup ./scripts/run_pure_mpm_ssh.sh 44 audited_all all > run_pure_mpm.log 2>&1 &
#
# Arguments:
#   $1 NUM_WORKERS : Concurrent 1-thread R workers for real/synth (default: 44)
#   $2 SYNTH_SCOPE : Checkpoint refresh scope for Experiment 1 (synth):
#                    - audited_all (default, recommended): all 13 methods cleanly
#                      (Pure-MPM FBMS + IWSM + fixed Bag_STLSQ + deg-3 RatPoly + SR_Pool)
#                    - fbms5_sindy5: 5 FBMS methods + 5 SINDy baselines (skips IWSM to save ~8h)
#                    - fbms6: all 6 FBMS methods only
#                    - fbms5: only the 5 methods that previously had 0.025
#                    (Note: Experiment 2 'real' ALWAYS clears 'all' 2,600 tasks (~45 min)
#                     because post-split differentiation & sustained Yeast changed the data.)
#   $3 SUITE       : all (default: tuning + ablation + real + synth) |
#                    fast (tuning + ablation + real, ~1 hour total) |
#                    synth_only | real_only
# ==============================================================================

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

NUM_WORKERS="${1:-44}"
SYNTH_SCOPE="${2:-audited_all}"
SUITE="${3:-all}"
ABL_CORES="${ABL_CORES:-6}"
export TASK_TIMEOUT="${TASK_TIMEOUT:-1800}"

export PATH="$HOME/.juliaup/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"

# Load EasyBuild R (and Julia if available) module on nam-shub-01 (do NOT load Python module)
if command -v module >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
  module load Julia 2>/dev/null || true
elif [ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
  module load Julia 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if ! command -v Rscript >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERROR] Rscript not found! Load the R module or run ./scripts/setup_server.sh first."
  exit 1
fi

if [ -d "$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib" ]; then
  export R_LIBS_USER="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
  export R_LIBS="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
fi

if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate" ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate"
  export VIRTUAL_ENV="$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
  export PATH="$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin:$PATH"
fi

export SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH=0
export USE_AUTHENTIC_EMPIRICAL=1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
export OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1
export MKL_NUM_THREADS=1
export VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS=1
export JULIA_NUM_THREADS=1

echo "========================================================================"
echo "[PURE-MPM SSH] Starting run at $(date)"
echo "  NUM_WORKERS=$NUM_WORKERS | SYNTH_SCOPE=$SYNTH_SCOPE | SUITE=$SUITE | ABL_CORES=$ABL_CORES | TASK_TIMEOUT=$TASK_TIMEOUT"
