echo "========================================================================"

echo "[STEP 0/4] Bootstrapping & verifying server R (.r_lib) + Python (.venv) + Julia dependencies..."
Rscript -e '
  lib_t <- Sys.getenv("R_LIBS_USER")
  if (!nzchar(lib_t) || !dir.exists(lib_t)) lib_t <- .libPaths()[1]
  for (pkg in c("BAS", "gauseR", "glmmTMB")) {
    if (!requireNamespace(pkg, quietly = TRUE)) {
      message("[SETUP] Installing missing R package into ", lib_t, ": ", pkg)
      try(install.packages(pkg, lib = lib_t, repos = "https://cloud.r-project.org"), silent = TRUE)
    }
  }
'
Rscript scripts/verify_env.R

if [[ "$SUITE" == "all" || "$SUITE" == "fast" ]]; then
  echo "[STEP 1/4] Preparing real-world datasets (zero-leakage & sustained Yeast)..."
  Rscript scripts/download_real_data.R

  echo "[STEP 2/4] Re-running BGNLM calibration tuning suite (Experiment 0, Pure MPM)..."
  Rscript scripts/run_tuning.R --full --cores "$ABL_CORES"

  echo "[STEP 3/4] Re-running targeted ablation Jobs 1-4 (Pure MPM + Native PySR/PyOperon + BAS + WSINDy)..."
  ABLATION_JOBS="1,2,3,4" CORES="$ABL_CORES" ./scripts/run_targeted_ablation.sh "$ABL_CORES"
fi

# Note: In Experiments 1 & 2 (run_remote_server.sh across 44 workers), SR_Pool_Pareto & SR_Pool_MDL
# use the documented static-pool engine (PYSR_USE_JULIA=0, PYOPERON_NATIVE=0) so 44 concurrent workers
# never spawn 44 competing Julia runtimes; native Julia PySR & C++ PyOperon run in Step 3 above.
export PYSR_USE_JULIA=0
export PYOPERON_NATIVE=0

if [[ "$SUITE" == "all" || "$SUITE" == "fast" || "$SUITE" == "real_only" ]]; then
  echo "[STEP 4A] Clearing all 2,600 'real' checkpoints (post-split zero-leakage data) and running Experiment 2..."
  Rscript scripts/prepare_pure_mpm_checkpoints.R --suite real --scope all
  ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh real "$NUM_WORKERS" 1
fi

if [[ "$SUITE" == "all" || "$SUITE" == "synth_only" ]]; then
  echo "[STEP 4B] Clearing '$SYNTH_SCOPE' checkpoints for 'synth' and running Experiment 1..."
  Rscript scripts/prepare_pure_mpm_checkpoints.R --suite synth --scope "$SYNTH_SCOPE"
  ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh synth "$NUM_WORKERS" 1
fi

echo "========================================================================"
echo "[PURE-MPM SSH] ALL REQUESTED SUITES COMPLETE at $(date)."
echo "  Transfer back: results/aggregated/ results/ablation_native/ results/tables/ results/figures/ config/best_bgnlm_params.yaml"
echo "========================================================================"


=== FILE: scripts/run_remote_server.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Usage: ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh [SUITE: synth|real|both] [NUM_WORKERS] [THREADS_PER_WORKER] [START_TASK] [END_TASK]
# Recommended on nam-shub-01 (56 cores) via nohup:
#   nohup ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh both 40 1 > run_benchmarks.log 2>&1 &

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

SUITE=${1:-both}
NUM_WORKERS=${2:-36}
THREADS_PER_WORKER=${3:-1}
START_TASK_USER=${4:-}
END_TASK_USER=${5:-}

# Load R module ONLY if available (do NOT load Python module as it can conflict with .venv)
if command -v module >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
elif [ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if ! command -v Rscript >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERROR] Rscript not found! Run ./scripts/setup_server.sh or load R module first."
  exit 1
fi

# Enforce project-local R library (.r_lib) if present
if [ -d "$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib" ]; then
  export R_LIBS_USER="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
  export R_LIBS="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
fi

# Activate project-local Python .venv if present
if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate" ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate"
  export VIRTUAL_ENV="$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
  export PATH="$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin:$PATH"
fi

export OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
export OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1
export MKL_NUM_THREADS=1
export VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS=1
export JULIA_NUM_THREADS=1

mkdir -p results/checkpoints_synth results/checkpoints_real logs

echo "[INFO] Running pre-flight environment verification..."
Rscript scripts/verify_env.R

echo "[INFO] Ensuring real-world datasets are present..."
Rscript scripts/download_real_data.R

if [[ ! -f "config/manifest_synth.csv" || ! -f "config/manifest_real.csv" ]]; then
  echo "[INFO] Generating default manifests..."
  Rscript scripts/generate_manifest.R
fi

# Resolve OS timeout command if available (GNU coreutils on Linux)
# Note: N=2500 5D Lorenz-96 BGNLM/BGLM tasks take up to 620-1800s under 44-worker load
TASK_TIMEOUT=${TASK_TIMEOUT:-1800}
OS_TIMEOUT=$((TASK_TIMEOUT + 60))
TIMEOUT_CMD=""
if command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  TIMEOUT_CMD="timeout -k 15 ${OS_TIMEOUT}"
fi
export TASK_TIMEOUT
export TIMEOUT_CMD

run_suite() {
  local s=$1
  local manifest="config/manifest_${s}.csv"
  local ckpt_dir="results/checkpoints_${s}"
  local total_tasks
  total_tasks=$(tail -n +2 "$manifest" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')

  local start_task=${START_TASK_USER:-1}
  local end_task=${END_TASK_USER:-$total_tasks}
  if (( end_task > total_tasks )); then
    end_task=$total_tasks
  fi

  # Emit ONLY missing task_ids in a single fast pass (< 0.5s) to avoid spawning R for completed tasks
  local pending_file="logs/.pending_${s}_tasks.txt"
  Rscript -e "
    dir_p <- '$ckpt_dir'
    existing <- list.files(dir_p, pattern = '^task_[0-9]+[.]rds$')
    done_ids <- if (length(existing) > 0L) as.integer(gsub('[^0-9]', '', existing)) else integer(0)
    all_ids <- seq.int(${start_task}L, ${end_task}L)
    todo <- setdiff(all_ids, done_ids)
    writeLines(as.character(todo), '$pending_file')
    cat(sprintf('[INFO] Suite %s: %d total in range [%d..%d], %d already done, %d pending.',
                '$s', length(all_ids), ${start_task}L, ${end_task}L, length(all_ids) - length(todo), length(todo)), '\n')
  "

  local n_pending
  n_pending=$(wc -l < "$pending_file" | tr -d ' ')
  if (( n_pending == 0 )); then
    echo "[INFO] Suite '$s' is already 100% complete!"
    return 0
  fi

  local fail_log="logs/${s}_failures.log"
  : > "$fail_log"

  echo "[INFO] Launching $n_pending pending tasks for suite '$s' across $NUM_WORKERS workers ($THREADS_PER_WORKER thread(s) each)..."
  export SUITE_CUR="$s"
  export THREADS_PER_WORKER
  export FAIL_LOG="$fail_log"

  run_one_task() {
    local tid="$1"
    local tlog="logs/${SUITE_CUR}_task_${tid}.log"
    if ${TIMEOUT_CMD} Rscript scripts/run_single_task.R --suite "${SUITE_CUR}" --task_id "${tid}" --cores "${THREADS_PER_WORKER}" --timeout "${TASK_TIMEOUT}" > "${tlog}" 2>&1; then
      rm -f "${tlog}"
    else
      local rc=$?
      echo "[FAIL] suite=${SUITE_CUR} task_id=${tid} exit=${rc} (see ${tlog})" >> "${FAIL_LOG}"
    fi
  }
  export -f run_one_task

  xargs -n 1 -P "$NUM_WORKERS" bash -c 'run_one_task "$1"' _ < "$pending_file"

  rm -f "$pending_file"
}

if [[ "$SUITE" == "synth" || "$SUITE" == "both" ]]; then
  run_suite "synth"
fi

if [[ "$SUITE" == "real" || "$SUITE" == "both" ]]; then
  run_suite "real"
fi

echo "[INFO] Aggregating checkpoints and running mixed-effects evaluation..."
Rscript scripts/aggregate_results.R --suite "$SUITE"
Rscript scripts/run_mixed_models.R || echo "[WARN] Mixed-effects summary deferred or non-fatal warning."
echo "[INFO] Pipeline complete."


=== FILE: scripts/run_single_task.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_single_task.R
# Atomic CLI Worker for Synthetic & Real-World Benchmark Tasks with Checkpointing
# Usage: Rscript scripts/run_single_task.R --suite <synth|real> --task_id <int> [--cores <int>]
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
get_arg <- function(flag, default = NULL) {
  idx <- which(args == flag)
  if (length(idx) > 0L && length(args) > idx[1]) return(args[idx[1] + 1L])
  default
}

suite <- get_arg("--suite", "synth")
task_id <- as.integer(get_arg("--task_id", "1"))
cores <- as.integer(get_arg("--cores", "1"))
timeout_sec <- as.numeric(get_arg("--timeout", "300"))

ckpt_dir <- paste0("results/checkpoints_", suite)
dir.create(ckpt_dir, recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
ckpt_file <- file.path(ckpt_dir, sprintf("task_%06d.rds", task_id))

# Seamless resumption BEFORE loading heavy packages or manifest
if (file.exists(ckpt_file)) {
  message("[SKIP] Checkpoint already exists: ", ckpt_file)
  quit(save = "no", status = 0L)
}

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
  library(R.utils)
})

source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
source("R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
source("R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

manifest_path <- paste0("config/manifest_", suite, ".csv")
if (!file.exists(manifest_path)) {
  stop("Manifest file does not exist: ", manifest_path)
}

manifest <- fread(manifest_path)
if (task_id < 1L || task_id > nrow(manifest)) {
  stop("Invalid task_id ", task_id, " (manifest has ", nrow(manifest), " rows).")
}

row <- manifest[task_id]
method_id <- as.character(row$method_id)
fast_mode <- as.logical(row$fast_mode == 1L)

# Generate deterministic dataset split
if (suite == "synth") {
  T_sim <- if (fast_mode) 15.0 else NULL
  task_data <- generate_synthetic_task_data(
    system_id = as.character(row$system_id),
    N_train = as.integer(row$sample_size),
    noise_eta = as.numeric(row$noise_eta),
    noise_regime = as.character(row$noise_regime),
    seed = as.integer(row$seed),
    T_max = T_sim
  )
} else {
  task_data <- generate_real_task_data(
    dataset_id = as.character(row$dataset_id),
    diff_method = as.character(row$diff_method),
    fold_id = as.integer(row$fold_id),
    seed = as.integer(row$seed)
  )
}

# Execute method under wall-clock (elapsed) timeout guard (cpu = Inf avoids premature RUSAGE_CHILDREN kills in mclapply)
t_start <- proc.time()[3]
gc_before <- gc(reset = TRUE)

fit_res <- tryCatch(
  R.utils::withTimeout({
    if (method_id %in% c(
      "BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned", "BGNLM_Hybrid_Poly",
      "BGLM_Oracle_Basis", "BGLM_Poly_Basis", "BGLM_Overcomplete"
    )) {
      run_fbms_method(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data, cores = cores, fast_mode = fast_mode)
    } else if (method_id %in% c(
      "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
      "UQ_SINDy_Oracle", "UQ_SINDy_Poly", "SINDy_PI_Rational"
    )) {
      run_sindy_baseline(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data, n_boot = if (fast_mode) 40L else 80L)
    } else if (method_id %in% c("SR_PySR", "SR_Operon")) {
      run_sr_baseline(method_id = method_id, task_data = task_data)
    } else {
      stop("Unknown method_id: ", method_id)
    }
  }, timeout = timeout_sec, cpu = Inf, elapsed = timeout_sec, onTimeout = "error"),
  error = function(e) {
    warning("Method ", method_id, " encountered error on task ", task_id, ": ", e$message)
    NULL
  }
)

runtime_sec <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t_start)
gc_after <- gc()
peak_mem_mb <- sum(gc_after[, 6], na.rm = TRUE)

# Compute all structural, calibration, predictive, and trajectory rollout metrics
m <- ncol(task_data$Y_train)
if (is.null(fit_res)) {
  discovered_empty <- replicate(m, character(0), simplify = FALSE)
  st_metrics <- compute_structure_metrics(discovered_empty, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  # Do NOT reward crashed runs with FDR=0, OC=0, SHD=0; mark missing structure as NA
  st_metrics$FDR <- NA_real_
  st_metrics$OC <- NA_integer_
  st_metrics$SHD <- NA_integer_
  st_metrics$n_active_terms <- NA_integer_
  pip_brier <- NA_real_
  pred_tr <- list(R2_clipped = 0.0, R2_raw = 0.0, NRMSE = 1.0, log10_RMSE = 0.0)
  pred_in <- list(R2_clipped = 0.0, R2_raw = 0.0, NRMSE = 1.0, log10_RMSE = 0.0, Coverage_95 = NA_real_, Winkler_95 = NA_real_)
  pred_ood <- list(R2_clipped = 0.0, R2_raw = 0.0, NRMSE = 1.0, log10_RMSE = 0.0, Coverage_95 = NA_real_, Winkler_95 = NA_real_)
  rollout_m <- list(R2_traj = 0.0, NRMSE_traj = 1.0, T_valid = 0.0, T_valid_frac = 0.0)
  status_str <- "FAILED"
} else {
  st_metrics <- compute_structure_metrics(fit_res$discovered, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pip_brier <- compute_pip_brier_score(fit_res$pip_list, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  pred_tr <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_train, task_data$Y_train_clean)
  pred_in <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_in, task_data$Y_test_in_noisy, fit_res$lower_in, fit_res$upper_in)
  pred_ood <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_ood, task_data$Y_test_ood_noisy, fit_res$lower_ood, fit_res$upper_ood)
  pred_in_clean <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_in, task_data$Y_test_in_clean)
  pred_ood_clean <- compute_prediction_metrics(fit_res$pred_ood, task_data$Y_test_ood_clean)
  pred_in$R2_clipped <- pred_in_clean$R2_clipped
  pred_in$R2_raw <- pred_in_clean$R2_raw
  pred_ood$R2_clipped <- pred_ood_clean$R2_clipped
  pred_ood$R2_raw <- pred_ood_clean$R2_raw
  rollout_m <- evaluate_trajectory_rollout(fit_res$predict_rhs_fn, task_data$rollout)
  status_str <- "OK"
}

if (suite == "synth") {
  out_dt <- data.table(
    task_id = task_id,
    suite = "synth",
    System = as.character(row$system_id),
    SystemGroup = as.character(row$system_group),
    SampleSize = as.integer(row$sample_size),
    Noise = as.numeric(row$noise_eta),
    NoiseRegime = as.character(row$noise_regime),
    Rep_ID = as.integer(row$rep_id),
    Seed = as.integer(row$seed),
    Method = method_id,
    Status = status_str,
    TPR = st_metrics$TPR,
    FDR = st_metrics$FDR,
    F1 = st_metrics$F1,
    Exact_Recovery = st_metrics$Exact_Recovery,
    SHD = st_metrics$SHD,
    OC = st_metrics$OC,
    n_active_terms = st_metrics$n_active_terms,
    PIP_Brier = pip_brier,
    R2_train = pred_tr$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_in = pred_in$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_ood = pred_ood$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_ood_raw = pred_ood$R2_raw,
    NRMSE_ood = pred_ood$NRMSE,
    log10_RMSE_ood = pred_ood$log10_RMSE,
    Coverage_95_in = pred_in$Coverage_95,
    Winkler_95_in = pred_in$Winkler_95,
    Coverage_95_ood = pred_ood$Coverage_95,
    Winkler_95_ood = pred_ood$Winkler_95,
    R2_traj = rollout_m$R2_traj,
    NRMSE_traj = rollout_m$NRMSE_traj,
    T_valid = rollout_m$T_valid,
    T_valid_frac = rollout_m$T_valid_frac,
    Runtime_s = runtime_sec,
    Peak_Mem_MB = peak_mem_mb,
    Discovered_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_hat, collapse = ";"),
    True_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_star, collapse = ";")
  )
} else {
  out_dt <- data.table(
    task_id = task_id,
    suite = "real",
    Dataset = as.character(row$dataset_id),
    Domain = as.character(row$domain),
    DiffMethod = as.character(row$diff_method),
    Fold_ID = as.integer(row$fold_id),
    Seed = as.integer(row$seed),
    Method = method_id,
    Status = status_str,
    TPR = st_metrics$TPR,
    FDR = st_metrics$FDR,
    F1 = st_metrics$F1,
    Exact_Recovery = st_metrics$Exact_Recovery,
    SHD = st_metrics$SHD,
    OC = st_metrics$OC,
    n_active_terms = st_metrics$n_active_terms,
    PIP_Brier = pip_brier,
    R2_train = pred_tr$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_in = pred_in$R2_clipped,
    R2_test_ood = pred_ood$R2_clipped,
    NRMSE_deriv_ood = pred_ood$NRMSE,
    Coverage_95_ood = pred_ood$Coverage_95,
    Winkler_95_ood = pred_ood$Winkler_95,
    R2_traj = rollout_m$R2_traj,
    NRMSE_traj = rollout_m$NRMSE_traj,
    T_valid = rollout_m$T_valid,
    T_valid_frac = rollout_m$T_valid_frac,
    Runtime_s = runtime_sec,
    Peak_Mem_MB = peak_mem_mb,
    Discovered_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_hat, collapse = ";"),
    Reference_Atoms = paste(st_metrics$S_star, collapse = ";")
  )
}

saveRDS(out_dt, ckpt_file)
message(sprintf("[DONE] suite=%s task_id=%d method=%s F1=%.3f R2_ood=%.3f R2_traj=%.3f (%.2fs)",
                suite, task_id, method_id, out_dt$F1, out_dt$R2_test_ood, out_dt$R2_traj, runtime_sec))



=== FILE: scripts/run_targeted_ablation.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_targeted_ablation.R
# Targeted Follow-Up Ablation & Fair Native Benchmark Suite (Jobs 1-4):
#   Job 1: Large-N (N=500..2500) scaling diagnosis/fix (r=1/sqrt(N), scaled P/N_mcmc) + BMA vs MPM-OLS
#   Job 2: Fair Baselines Head-to-Head (Fixed Bag-STLSQ with lambda*, UQ_SINDy_Bayes
#          via BAS::bas.lm, WSINDy Galerkin integral, SINDy_PI_Rat deg-3,
#          Native PySR & PyOperon, sin_deg check on IWSM, & PIP calibration bins)
#   Job 3: Non-Unit Internal Parameter Expressivity Stress Test (sin(2*x1), Km=0.3)
#   Job 4: Zero-Leakage Authentic Real-World Empirical Benchmarks
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

suppressPackageStartupMessages({
  library(data.table)
  library(R.utils)
})

source("R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
source("R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
source("R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
source("R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

dir.create("results/ablation_native", recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)

Sys.setenv(
  PYSR_USE_JULIA = Sys.getenv("PYSR_USE_JULIA", "1"),
  PYOPERON_NATIVE = Sys.getenv("PYOPERON_NATIVE", "1"),
  SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH = "0",
  USE_AUTHENTIC_EMPIRICAL = "1"
)

smoke_mode <- (Sys.getenv("ABLATION_SMOKE", "0") == "1")
cores_arg <- as.integer(Sys.getenv("CORES", "2"))
timeout_sec <- as.numeric(Sys.getenv("TASK_TIMEOUT", "420"))
jobs_run <- Sys.getenv("ABLATION_JOBS", "1,2,3,4")
has_job <- function(j_id) grepl(j_id, jobs_run, fixed = TRUE)

run_safe_call <- function(expr_fn) {
  tryCatch(
    R.utils::withTimeout(expr_fn(), timeout = timeout_sec, cpu = Inf, elapsed = timeout_sec, onTimeout = "error"),
    error = function(e) {
      message("[WARN] Fit failed: ", e$message)
      NULL
    }
  )
}

score_fit_full <- function(fit_res, task_data, method_id, discovered_override = NULL, pred_ood_override = NULL) {
  if (is.null(fit_res)) {
    return(list(
      Status = "FAILED", Engine = "failed",
      F1 = 0.0, TPR = 0.0, FDR = NA_real_, Exact = 0L,
      SHD = NA_integer_, OC = NA_integer_,
      PIP_Brier = NA_real_, PIP_SSPE = NA_real_, PIP_AUROC = NA_real_, PIP_AUPRC = NA_real_,
      R2_ood = 0.0, R2_traj = 0.0, Coverage_95_ood = NA_real_,
      Discovered_Atoms = ""
    ))
  }
  eng <- if (!is.null(fit_res$engine)) as.character(fit_res$engine)[1L] else "r_native"
  disc_use <- if (!is.null(discovered_override)) discovered_override else fit_res$discovered
  st_m <- compute_structure_metrics(disc_use, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  cal_m <- compute_pip_calibration_metrics(fit_res$pip_list, task_data$system$ground_truth)
  po_mat <- if (!is.null(pred_ood_override)) pred_ood_override else fit_res$pred_ood
  pred_ood_noisy <- compute_prediction_metrics(po_mat, task_data$Y_test_ood_noisy, fit_res$lower_ood, fit_res$upper_ood)
  pred_ood_clean <- compute_prediction_metrics(po_mat, task_data$Y_test_ood_clean)
  roll_m <- if (is.null(discovered_override) && is.null(pred_ood_override)) {
    evaluate_trajectory_rollout(fit_res$predict_rhs_fn, task_data$rollout)
  } else {
    list(R2_traj = NA_real_)
  }
  list(
    Status = "OK", Engine = eng,
    F1 = st_m$F1, TPR = st_m$TPR, FDR = st_m$FDR, Exact = st_m$Exact_Recovery,
    SHD = st_m$SHD, OC = st_m$OC,
    PIP_Brier = cal_m$PIP_Brier, PIP_SSPE = cal_m$PIP_SSPE,
    PIP_AUROC = cal_m$PIP_AUROC, PIP_AUPRC = cal_m$PIP_AUPRC,
    R2_ood = pred_ood_clean$R2_clipped, R2_traj = roll_m$R2_traj,
    Coverage_95_ood = pred_ood_noisy$Coverage_95,
    Discovered_Atoms = paste(st_m$S_hat, collapse = ";"),
    cal_m = cal_m
  )
}

if (has_job("1")) {
message("=== [JOB 1] Large-N Budget Scaling (r=N^-1/2, Scaled P/N_mcmc) & BMA ===")
job1_rows <- list()

# Large-N scaling diagnosis & fix (Fixed budget r=1/N, P=15, N=500 vs Scaled budget r=1/sqrt(N), P=16, N=800)
j1b_specs <- if (smoke_mode) {
  list(list(sys = "sys11_goodwin_mm", N = 200L), list(sys = "sys11_goodwin_mm", N = 1000L))
} else {
  list(
    list(sys = "sys11_goodwin_mm", N = 500L),
    list(sys = "sys11_goodwin_mm", N = 1000L),
    list(sys = "sys11_goodwin_mm", N = 2500L),
    list(sys = "sys02_lorenz63",   N = 2500L)
  )
}
j1b_seeds <- if (smoke_mode) 101L else c(101L, 202L)

  for (sp in j1b_specs) {
    sys_id <- sp$sys
    N_tr <- sp$N
    for (sd in j1b_seeds) {
      td <- generate_synthetic_task_data(sys_id, N_train = N_tr, noise_eta = 0.05, noise_regime = "derivative_snr", seed = sd)

    # Baseline tuned budget
    t0 <- proc.time()[3]
    f_base <- run_safe_call(function() run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td, cores = cores_arg, tuning_override = list(runs = 4L), fast_mode = smoke_mode))
    ev_base <- score_fit_full(f_base, td, "BGNLM_FixedBudget")
    bma_base <- if (!is.null(f_base) && all(is.finite(f_base$pred_bma_ood))) {
      compute_prediction_metrics(f_base$pred_bma_ood, td$Y_test_ood_clean)$R2_clipped
    } else NA_real_
    job1_rows[[length(job1_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
      SubJob = "1_LargeN", System = sys_id, SampleSize = N_tr, Seed = sd,
      Variant = "BGNLM_Baseline_FixedBudget", F1 = ev_base$F1, FDR = ev_base$FDR, Exact = ev_base$Exact,
      R2_ood = ev_base$R2_ood, R2_ood_BMA = bma_base, R2_traj = ev_base$R2_traj, Runtime_s = as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t0)
    )

    # Scaled budget + sqrt(N) prior fix
    t1 <- proc.time()[3]
    ovr <- list(P_generations = if (smoke_mode) 8L else 16L, N_mcmc = if (smoke_mode) 300L else 800L, runs = 4L, model_prior = list(r = 1.0 / sqrt(N_tr)))
    f_scaled <- run_safe_call(function() run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td, cores = cores_arg, tuning_override = ovr, fast_mode = FALSE))
    ev_sc <- score_fit_full(f_scaled, td, "BGNLM_ScaledBudget")
    bma_sc <- if (!is.null(f_scaled) && all(is.finite(f_scaled$pred_bma_ood))) {
      compute_prediction_metrics(f_scaled$pred_bma_ood, td$Y_test_ood_clean)$R2_clipped
    } else NA_real_
    job1_rows[[length(job1_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
      SubJob = "1_LargeN", System = sys_id, SampleSize = N_tr, Seed = sd,
      Variant = "BGNLM_ScaledBudget_SqrtN", F1 = ev_sc$F1, FDR = ev_sc$FDR, Exact = ev_sc$Exact,
      R2_ood = ev_sc$R2_ood, R2_ood_BMA = bma_sc, R2_traj = ev_sc$R2_traj, Runtime_s = as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t1)
    )
    message(sprintf("[JOB 1] %s N=%d seed=%d | Base F1=%.3f | Scaled F1=%.3f", sys_id, N_tr, sd, ev_base$F1, ev_sc$F1))
    fwrite(rbindlist(job1_rows, fill = TRUE), "results/ablation_native/job1_largeN_scaling.csv")
  }
}

dt_job1 <- rbindlist(job1_rows, fill = TRUE)
fwrite(dt_job1, "results/ablation_native/job1_largeN_scaling.csv")
message("[OK] Saved results/ablation_native/job1_largeN_scaling.csv")
}

# ==============================================================================
# JOB 2: Fair Baselines Head-to-Head + sin_deg Check + PIP Calibration Reliability
# ==============================================================================
if (has_job("2")) {
  message("=== [JOB 2] Fair Baselines Head-to-Head & PIP Calibration ===")
  j2_systems <- if (smoke_mode) c("sys02_lorenz63", "sys09_damped_pendulum") else c("sys02_lorenz63", "sys08_rossler_lorenz_hybrid", "sys09_damped_pendulum", "sys11_goodwin_mm")
  j2_regimes <- c("derivative_snr", "state_noise")
  j2_seeds <- if (smoke_mode) 101L else c(101L, 202L)
  j2_methods <- c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned",
    "SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly",
    "UQ_SINDy_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Oracle",
    "WSINDy_Poly", "WSINDy_Oracle", "SINDy_PI_Rational",
    "SR_PySR", "SR_Operon"
  )

  job2_rows <- list()
  pip_calib_rows <- list()

  dispatch_j2 <- function(mid, td) {
    if (mid %in% c("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "BGNLM_IWSM_Untuned")) {
      run_fbms_method(mid, td, cores = cores_arg, tuning_override = list(runs = 4L), fast_mode = smoke_mode)
    } else if (mid %in% c("SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Poly", "SINDy_PI_Rational")) {
      run_sindy_baseline(mid, td, n_boot = if (smoke_mode) 25L else 80L)
    } else if (mid == "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Poly") {
      run_uq_sindy_bayes(td, dict_type = "poly")
    } else if (mid == "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Oracle") {
      run_uq_sindy_bayes(td, dict_type = "oracle")
    } else if (mid == "WSINDy_Poly") {
      run_wsindy_baseline(td, dict_type = "poly")
    } else if (mid == "WSINDy_Oracle") {
      run_wsindy_baseline(td, dict_type = "oracle")
    } else if (mid %in% c("SR_PySR", "SR_Operon")) {
      run_sr_baseline(mid, td, strict_native = TRUE)
    } else {
      stop("Unknown method: ", mid)
    }
  }

  for (sys_id in j2_systems) {
    base_tr <- simulate_base_trajectory(sys_id)
    for (reg in j2_regimes) {
      for (sd in j2_seeds) {
        td <- generate_synthetic_task_data(sys_id, N_train = 300L, noise_eta = 0.05, noise_regime = reg, seed = sd)
        if (reg == "state_noise") {
          # Provide N=300 equispaced contiguous noisy state trajectory from [0.05T, 0.55T] for WSINDy (same N & SNR, zero test leakage)
          idx_win <- which(base_tr$times > 0.05 * base_tr$T_max & base_tr$times < 0.55 * base_tr$T_max)
          idx_eq <- idx_win[round(seq(1L, length(idx_win), length.out = 300L))]
          X_eq <- base_tr$X[idx_eq, , drop = FALSE]
          set.seed(sd + 999L)
          X_eq_noisy <- X_eq + sapply(seq_len(ncol(X_eq)), function(c_i) rnorm(nrow(X_eq), 0.0, td$noise_sd_vec[c_i]))
          td$t_seq <- base_tr$times[idx_eq]
          td$X_seq <- X_eq_noisy
        }
        for (mid in j2_methods) {
          t0 <- proc.time()[3]
          fr <- run_safe_call(function() dispatch_j2(mid, td))
          el <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t0)
          ev <- score_fit_full(fr, td, mid)

        if (!is.null(ev$cal_m) && nrow(ev$cal_m$reliability) > 0L &&
            mid %in% c("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Poly")) {
          rel_dt <- as.data.table(ev$cal_m$reliability)
          rel_dt[, `:=`(System = sys_id, NoiseRegime = reg, Seed = sd, Method = mid)]
          pip_calib_rows[[length(pip_calib_rows) + 1L]] <- rel_dt
        }

        job2_rows[[length(job2_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
          System = sys_id, NoiseRegime = reg, Seed = sd, Method = mid, Engine = ev$Engine,
          Status = ev$Status, F1 = ev$F1, TPR = ev$TPR, FDR = ev$FDR, Exact = ev$Exact,
          SHD = ev$SHD, OC = ev$OC, PIP_Brier = ev$PIP_Brier, PIP_SSPE = ev$PIP_SSPE,
          PIP_AUROC = ev$PIP_AUROC, PIP_AUPRC = ev$PIP_AUPRC,
          R2_ood = ev$R2_ood, R2_traj = ev$R2_traj, Coverage_95_ood = ev$Coverage_95_ood,
          Runtime_s = el, Discovered_Atoms = ev$Discovered_Atoms
        )
        message(sprintf("[JOB 2] %s | %s | seed=%d | %s (%s) -> F1=%.3f FDR=%.3f R2_traj=%.3f (%.1fs)",
                        sys_id, reg, sd, mid, ev$Engine, ev$F1, ifelse(is.na(ev$FDR), -1, ev$FDR), ev$R2_traj, el))
        }
      }
    }
  }

  fwrite(rbindlist(job2_rows, fill = TRUE), "results/ablation_native/job2_fair_baselines_synth.csv")
  if (length(pip_calib_rows) > 0L) {
    fwrite(rbindlist(pip_calib_rows, fill = TRUE), "results/ablation_native/pip_calibration_reliability.csv")
  }
  message("[OK] Saved job2_fair_baselines_synth.csv & pip_calibration_reliability.csv")
}

# ==============================================================================
# JOB 3: Non-Unit Internal Parameter Expressivity Stress Test
#   System A: Forced Non-Unit Pendulum dx1 = x2, dx2 = -0.25*x2 - 5*sin(2*x1)
#   System B: Non-Unit Km Goodwin dx1 = 1/(1+x3^2) - 0.5*x1, dx2 = x1 - 0.8*x2/(0.3 + x2), dx3 = x2 - 0.5*x3
# ==============================================================================
if (has_job("3")) {
  message("=== [JOB 3] Non-Unit Internal Parameter Stress Test ===")
  build_custom_task <- function(sys_def, N_train = 300L, noise_eta = 0.03, seed = 101L) {
    times <- seq(0, sys_def$T_max, by = sys_def$dt)
    sol <- as.data.frame(deSolve::ode(y = sys_def$y0, times = times, func = sys_def$rhs, parms = sys_def$parms, method = "lsoda"))
    X_all <- as.matrix(sol[, -1, drop = FALSE])
    colnames(X_all) <- sys_def$state_names
    t_all <- sol[, 1]
    dX_all <- sys_def$analytical_deriv(X_all, sys_def$parms)
    set.seed(seed)
    n_tot <- nrow(X_all)
    idx_int <- which(t_all > 0.05 * sys_def$T_max & t_all < 0.90 * sys_def$T_max)
    idx_ood_all <- which(t_all >= 0.90 * sys_def$T_max)
    samp <- sort(sample(idx_int, size = min(length(idx_int), N_train + 300L)))
    idx_tr <- samp[seq_len(N_train)]
    idx_in <- setdiff(samp, idx_tr)
    idx_ood <- idx_ood_all[round(seq(1, length(idx_ood_all), length.out = 300L))]

    m <- ncol(X_all)
    sd_d <- pmax(apply(dX_all[idx_int, , drop = FALSE], 2, sd), 1e-6)
    eps_tr <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(length(idx_tr), 0, noise_eta * sd_d[j]))
    eps_in <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(length(idx_in), 0, noise_eta * sd_d[j]))
    eps_ood <- sapply(seq_len(m), function(j) rnorm(length(idx_ood), 0, noise_eta * sd_d[j]))
    roll_idx <- idx_ood_all[seq(1L, min(length(idx_ood_all), 2000L), by = 10L)]

    list(
      system = list(
        sys_id = sys_def$sys_id, id = sys_def$id, name = sys_def$name,
        dim = m, state_names = sys_def$state_names, feature_names = sys_def$state_names,
        input_names = character(0), ground_truth = sys_def$ground_truth,
        oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
      ),
      seed = seed, N_train = N_train,
      t_train = t_all[idx_tr],
      X_train = X_all[idx_tr, , drop = FALSE], Y_train = dX_all[idx_tr, , drop = FALSE] + eps_tr,
      Y_train_clean = dX_all[idx_tr, , drop = FALSE],
      X_test_in = X_all[idx_in, , drop = FALSE],
      Y_test_in_clean = dX_all[idx_in, , drop = FALSE], Y_test_in_noisy = dX_all[idx_in, , drop = FALSE] + eps_in,
      X_test_ood = X_all[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE],
      Y_test_ood_clean = dX_all[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE], Y_test_ood_noisy = dX_all[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE] + eps_ood,
      rollout = list(times = t_all[roll_idx] - t_all[roll_idx[1]], X_true = X_all[roll_idx, , drop = FALSE], x0 = X_all[roll_idx[1], ])
    )
  }

  nonunit_systems <- list(
    nonunit_pendulum_2theta = list(
      sys_id = 901L, id = "nonunit_pendulum_2theta", name = "Non-Unit Frequency Pendulum sin(2*x1)",
      dt = 0.002, T_max = 20.0, y0 = c(x1 = 1.8, x2 = 0.0), parms = c(g = 0.25, a = 5.0),
      state_names = c("x1", "x2"),
      rhs = function(t, s, p) list(c(s[2], -p["g"] * s[2] - p["a"] * sin(2.0 * s[1]))),
      analytical_deriv = function(X, p) cbind(dx1 = X[, 2], dx2 = -p["g"] * X[, 2] - p["a"] * sin(2.0 * X[, 1])),
      ground_truth = list(c("x2"), c("x2", "sin(2*x1)"))
    ),
    nonunit_mm_km03 = list(
      sys_id = 902L, id = "nonunit_mm_km03", name = "Non-Unit Michaelis-Menten (Km=0.3)",
      dt = 0.002, T_max = 30.0, y0 = c(x1 = 0.5, x2 = 0.5, x3 = 0.5), parms = c(k = 0.5),
      state_names = c("x1", "x2", "x3"),
      rhs = function(t, s, p) list(c(1.0 / (1.0 + s[3]^2) - 0.5 * s[1], s[1] - 0.8 * s[2] / (0.3 + abs(s[2])), s[2] - 0.5 * s[3])),
      analytical_deriv = function(X, p) cbind(dx1 = 1.0 / (1.0 + X[, 3]^2) - 0.5 * X[, 1], dx2 = X[, 1] - 0.8 * X[, 2] / (0.3 + abs(X[, 2])), dx3 = X[, 2] - 0.5 * X[, 3]),
      ground_truth = list(c("x1", "1/(1+x3^2)"), c("x1", "x2/(0.3+x2)"), c("x2", "x3"))
    )
  )

  j3_seeds <- if (smoke_mode) 101L else c(101L, 202L)
  job3_rows <- list()

  for (s_nm in names(nonunit_systems)) {
    for (sd in j3_seeds) {
      td <- build_custom_task(nonunit_systems[[s_nm]], N_train = 300L, noise_eta = 0.03, seed = sd)

      variants <- list(
        list(id = "BGNLM_Full_Tuned_pproj0", fn = function() run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td, cores = cores_arg, fast_mode = smoke_mode)),
        list(id = "BGNLM_Full_Proj005", fn = function() run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td, cores = cores_arg, tuning_override = list(p_inter = 0.50, p_mod = 0.35, p_proj = 0.05, p_new = 0.10), fast_mode = smoke_mode)),
        list(id = "SR_PySR", fn = function() run_sr_baseline("SR_PySR", td, strict_native = TRUE)),
        list(id = "SR_Operon", fn = function() run_sr_baseline("SR_Operon", td, strict_native = TRUE))
      )
      for (v in variants) {
        t0 <- proc.time()[3]
        fr <- run_safe_call(v$fn)
        el <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t0)
        ev <- score_fit_full(fr, td, v$id)
        job3_rows[[length(job3_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
          System = s_nm, Seed = sd, Method = v$id, Engine = ev$Engine, Status = ev$Status,
          F1 = ev$F1, FDR = ev$FDR, R2_ood = ev$R2_ood, R2_traj = ev$R2_traj,
          OC = ev$OC, Runtime_s = el, Discovered_Atoms = ev$Discovered_Atoms
        )
        message(sprintf(
          "[JOB 3] %s | seed=%d | %s (%s) -> F1=%.3f R2_ood=%.3f R2_traj=%.3f (%.1fs)",
          s_nm, sd, v$id, ev$Engine, ev$F1, ev$R2_ood, ev$R2_traj, el
        ))
      }
    }
  }
  fwrite(rbindlist(job3_rows, fill = TRUE), "results/ablation_native/job3_nonunit_scale_expressivity.csv")
  message("[OK] Saved results/ablation_native/job3_nonunit_scale_expressivity.csv")
}

# ==============================================================================
# JOB 4: Zero-Leakage Authentic Empirical Suite
#   1. auth_lynx_hare_1900_1920 (raw 21 yrs split FIRST, then spline strictly within train/OOD)
#   2. auth_huffaker_1963 (gauseR::huffaker_1963 60w real lab predator-prey mites)
#   3. auth_gause_1934_mixture (gauseR::gause_1934_book_f22 real Paramecium mixture)
#   4. real03_schmidt_pendulum & real05_yeast_glycolysis (zero pre-split leakage)
# ==============================================================================
if (has_job("4")) {
  message("=== [JOB 4] Zero-Leakage Authentic Real-World Empirical Benchmarks ===")

  build_leakfree_auth_lynx_hare <- function(fold_id = 1L, seed = 1900L, diff_method = "sgolay_spline") {
    df_raw <- read.csv("data/real_world/lynx_hare_annual_raw_1900_1920.csv", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
    t_raw <- as.numeric(df_raw$time) # 0..20
    X_raw <- as.matrix(df_raw[, c("x1", "x2")])
    n_raw <- nrow(df_raw) # 21

    set.seed(seed)
    frac <- 0.65 + 0.10 * ((fold_id - 1L) %% 5L) / 4.0
    k_split <- max(12L, min(n_raw - 5L, floor(frac * n_raw)))

    # Split RAW years FIRST, then spline-upsample strictly within train vs OOD
    t_tr_raw <- t_raw[1:k_split]
    X_tr_raw <- X_raw[1:k_split, , drop = FALSE]
    t_ood_raw <- t_raw[(k_split + 1L):n_raw]
    X_ood_raw <- X_raw[(k_split + 1L):n_raw, , drop = FALSE]

    t_tr_grid <- seq(t_tr_raw[1], t_tr_raw[length(t_tr_raw)], by = 0.25)
    t_ood_grid <- seq(t_ood_raw[1], t_ood_raw[length(t_ood_raw)], by = 0.25)

    X_tr_dense <- cbind(
      x1 = pmax(1.0, stats::spline(t_tr_raw, X_tr_raw[, 1], xout = t_tr_grid, method = "natural")$y),
      x2 = pmax(1.0, stats::spline(t_tr_raw, X_tr_raw[, 2], xout = t_tr_grid, method = "natural")$y)
    )
    X_ood_dense <- cbind(
      x1 = pmax(1.0, stats::spline(t_ood_raw, X_ood_raw[, 1], xout = t_ood_grid, method = "natural")$y),
      x2 = pmax(1.0, stats::spline(t_ood_raw, X_ood_raw[, 2], xout = t_ood_grid, method = "natural")$y)
    )

    diff_fn <- if (diff_method == "sgolay_spline") estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline else estimate_derivatives_tvregdiff
    Y_tr_dense <- diff_fn(X_tr_dense, t_tr_grid)
    Y_ood_dense <- diff_fn(X_ood_dense, t_ood_grid)

    n_tr <- nrow(X_tr_dense)
    idx_tr <- sort(sample(seq_len(n_tr), size = floor(0.85 * n_tr), replace = FALSE))
    idx_in <- setdiff(seq_len(n_tr), idx_tr)
    if (length(idx_in) < 3L) idx_in <- idx_tr

    list(
      system = list(
        sys_id = 101L, id = "auth_lynx_hare_1900_1920", name = "Authentic 1900-1920 Lynx-Hare (Zero-Leakage)",
        dim = 2L, state_names = c("x1", "x2"), feature_names = c("x1", "x2"), input_names = character(0),
        ground_truth = list(c("x1", "x1*x2"), c("x2", "x1*x2")), oracle_extra_basis = character(0)
      ),
      seed = seed, N_train = length(idx_tr),
      t_train = t_tr_grid[idx_tr], t_seq = t_tr_grid, X_seq = X_tr_dense,
      X_train = X_tr_dense[idx_tr, , drop = FALSE], Y_train = Y_tr_dense[idx_tr, , drop = FALSE],
      Y_train_clean = Y_tr_dense[idx_tr, , drop = FALSE],
      X_test_in = X_tr_dense[idx_in, , drop = FALSE], Y_test_in_clean = Y_tr_dense[idx_in, , drop = FALSE],
      Y_test_in_noisy = Y_tr_dense[idx_in, , drop = FALSE],
      X_test_ood = X_ood_dense, Y_test_ood_clean = Y_ood_dense, Y_test_ood_noisy = Y_ood_dense,
      rollout = list(times = t_ood_grid - t_ood_grid[1], X_true = X_ood_dense, x0 = X_ood_dense[1, ])
    )
  }

  build_leakfree_csv_task <- function(csv_path, ds_id, ds_name, gt_list, oracle_extra = character(0), fold_id = 1L, seed = 2000L) {
    df <- read.csv(csv_path, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)
    times <- as.numeric(df$time)
    X_states <- as.matrix(df[, c("x1", "x2")])
    n_tot <- nrow(X_states)
    set.seed(seed)
    frac <- 0.65 + 0.10 * ((fold_id - 1L) %% 5L) / 4.0
    n_tr_win <- max(14L, min(n_tot - 6L, floor(frac * n_tot)))
    idx_pool <- seq_len(n_tr_win)
    idx_ood <- seq(n_tr_win + 1L, n_tot)

    # Differentiate train & OOD strictly separately
    Y_tr_win <- estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline(X_states[idx_pool, , drop = FALSE], times[idx_pool])
    Y_ood_win <- estimate_derivatives_sgolay_spline(X_states[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE], times[idx_ood])

    n_sub <- max(12L, floor(0.85 * length(idx_pool)))
    idx_tr_loc <- sort(sample(seq_along(idx_pool), size = n_sub, replace = FALSE))
    idx_in_loc <- setdiff(seq_along(idx_pool), idx_tr_loc)
    if (length(idx_in_loc) < 3L) idx_in_loc <- idx_tr_loc

    list(
      system = list(
        sys_id = 200L, id = ds_id, name = ds_name,
        dim = 2L, state_names = c("x1", "x2"), feature_names = c("x1", "x2"), input_names = character(0),
        ground_truth = gt_list, oracle_extra_basis = oracle_extra
      ),
      seed = seed, N_train = length(idx_tr_loc),
      t_train = times[idx_pool[idx_tr_loc]], t_seq = times[idx_pool], X_seq = X_states[idx_pool, , drop = FALSE],
      X_train = X_states[idx_pool[idx_tr_loc], , drop = FALSE], Y_train = Y_tr_win[idx_tr_loc, , drop = FALSE],
      Y_train_clean = Y_tr_win[idx_tr_loc, , drop = FALSE],
      X_test_in = X_states[idx_pool[idx_in_loc], , drop = FALSE],
      Y_test_in_clean = Y_tr_win[idx_in_loc, , drop = FALSE], Y_test_in_noisy = Y_tr_win[idx_in_loc, , drop = FALSE],
      X_test_ood = X_states[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE], Y_test_ood_clean = Y_ood_win, Y_test_ood_noisy = Y_ood_win,
      rollout = list(times = times[idx_ood] - times[idx_ood[1]], X_true = X_states[idx_ood, , drop = FALSE], x0 = X_states[idx_ood[1], ])
    )
  }

  j4_folds <- if (smoke_mode) 1L else 1:3
  j4_methods <- c(
    "BGNLM_Full_Tuned", "SINDy_STLSQ_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Poly",
    "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Poly", "WSINDy_Poly", "SR_PySR", "SR_Operon"
  )
  job4_rows <- list()

  dispatch_j4 <- function(mid, td) {
    if (mid == "BGNLM_Full_Tuned") {
      run_fbms_method(mid, td, cores = cores_arg, fast_mode = smoke_mode)
    } else if (mid %in% c("SINDy_STLSQ_Poly", "UQ_SINDy_Poly")) {
      run_sindy_baseline(mid, td, n_boot = if (smoke_mode) 25L else 80L)
    } else if (mid == "UQ_SINDy_Bayes_Poly") {
      run_uq_sindy_bayes(td, dict_type = "poly")
    } else if (mid == "WSINDy_Poly") {
      run_wsindy_baseline(td, dict_type = "poly")
    } else if (mid %in% c("SR_PySR", "SR_Operon")) {
      run_sr_baseline(mid, td, strict_native = TRUE)
    } else {
      stop("Unknown method: ", mid)
    }
  }

  j4_datasets <- c("auth_lynx_hare", "auth_huffaker_1963", "auth_gause_mixture", "real03_schmidt_pendulum", "real05_yeast_glycolysis")

  for (ds in j4_datasets) {
    for (fid in j4_folds) {
      td <- if (ds == "auth_lynx_hare") {
        build_leakfree_auth_lynx_hare(fold_id = fid, seed = 1900L + fid)
      } else if (ds == "auth_huffaker_1963") {
        build_leakfree_csv_task("data/real_world/huffaker_predator_prey_authentic.csv",
          "auth_huffaker_1963", "Authentic Huffaker 1963 Mite Predator-Prey",
          list(c("x1", "x1*x2"), c("x2", "x1*x2")),
          fold_id = fid, seed = 1963L + fid
        )
      } else if (ds == "auth_gause_mixture") {
        build_leakfree_csv_task("data/real_world/gause_paramecium_mixture_authentic.csv",
          "auth_gause_mixture", "Authentic Gause 1934 Paramecium Competition",
          list(c("x1", "x1^2", "x1*x2"), c("x2", "x2^2", "x1*x2")),
          fold_id = fid, seed = 1934L + fid
        )
      } else {
        generate_real_task_data(ds, diff_method = "sgolay_spline", fold_id = fid, seed = 5000L + fid)
      }

      for (mid in j4_methods) {
        t0 <- proc.time()[3]
        fr <- run_safe_call(function() dispatch_j4(mid, td))
        el <- as.numeric(proc.time()[3] - t0)
        ev <- score_fit_full(fr, td, mid)
        job4_rows[[length(job4_rows) + 1L]] <- data.table(
          Dataset = ds, Fold_ID = fid, Method = mid, Engine = ev$Engine, Status = ev$Status,
          F1 = ev$F1, TPR = ev$TPR, FDR = ev$FDR, OC = ev$OC,
          R2_ood = ev$R2_ood, R2_traj = ev$R2_traj, Coverage_95_ood = ev$Coverage_95_ood,
          Runtime_s = el, Discovered_Atoms = ev$Discovered_Atoms
        )
        message(sprintf(
          "[JOB 4] %s fold=%d | %s (%s) -> F1=%.3f FDR=%.3f R2_traj=%.3f (%.1fs)",
          ds, fid, mid, ev$Engine, ev$F1, ifelse(is.na(ev$FDR), -1, ev$FDR), ev$R2_traj, el
        ))
      }
    }
  }
  fwrite(rbindlist(job4_rows, fill = TRUE), "results/ablation_native/job4_authentic_real_world.csv")
  message("[OK] Saved results/ablation_native/job4_authentic_real_world.csv")
}
message("=== ALL REQUESTED ABLATION JOBS COMPLETE ===")


=== FILE: scripts/run_targeted_ablation.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Targeted server ablation: Pure-MPM BGNLM + native PySR/PyOperon + standardized Bag_STLSQ
# + BAS Bayesian SINDy + Galerkin WSINDy + authentic zero-leakage empirical datasets
# Recommended via nohup:
#   nohup ./scripts/run_targeted_ablation.sh 6 > run_targeted_ablation.log 2>&1 &

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

CORES="${1:-6}"

export PATH="$HOME/.juliaup/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"

# Load EasyBuild R (and Julia if available) module on nam-shub-01
if command -v module >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
  module load Julia 2>/dev/null || true
elif [ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
  module load Julia 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if ! command -v Rscript >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERROR] Rscript not found! Load the R module or run ./scripts/setup_server.sh first."
  exit 1
fi

# Enforce project-local R library (.r_lib) if present
if [ -d "$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib" ]; then
  export R_LIBS_USER="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
  export R_LIBS="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
fi

# Ensure BAS (and optional gauseR) exist in R library for Job 2 & Job 4
Rscript -e '
  lib_t <- Sys.getenv("R_LIBS_USER")
  if (!nzchar(lib_t) || !dir.exists(lib_t)) lib_t <- .libPaths()[1]
  for (pkg in c("BAS", "gauseR")) {
    if (!requireNamespace(pkg, quietly = TRUE)) {
      message("[ABLATION SETUP] Installing missing R package: ", pkg)
      try(install.packages(pkg, lib = lib_t, repos = "https://cloud.r-project.org"), silent = TRUE)
    }
  }
  stopifnot(requireNamespace("BAS", quietly = TRUE))
'

# Activate project-local Python .venv if present
if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate" ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate"
  export VIRTUAL_ENV="$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
  export PATH="$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin:$PATH"
fi

# Ensure pyoperon and pysr are installed inside .venv
PY_BIN="${VIRTUAL_ENV:+$VIRTUAL_ENV/bin/python}"
PY_BIN="${PY_BIN:-python3}"
if ! "$PY_BIN" -c "import pyoperon.sklearn, pysr" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ABLATION SETUP] Installing pyoperon & pysr into .venv..."
  if command -v uv >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -n "${VIRTUAL_ENV:-}" ]; then
    uv pip install --python "$PY_BIN" pyoperon pysr || true
  elif [ -n "${VIRTUAL_ENV:-}" ]; then
    "$VIRTUAL_ENV/bin/pip" install pyoperon pysr || true
  fi
fi

# Ensure Julia is available for native PySR
if ! command -v julia >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ABLATION SETUP] 'julia' not found on PATH; bootstrapping standalone juliaup in ~/.juliaup..."
  curl -fsSL https://install.julialang.org | sh -s -- -y --add-to-path=no || true
  export PATH="$HOME/.juliaup/bin:$PATH"
fi

if command -v julia >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  "$PY_BIN" -c "import pysr; pysr.install(quiet=True)" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
fi

export PYSR_USE_JULIA=1
export PYOPERON_NATIVE=1
export SINDY_LEGACY_UNSTD_THRESH=0
export USE_AUTHENTIC_EMPIRICAL=1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
export OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1
export JULIA_NUM_THREADS=1
export CORES="${CORES}"

echo "[ABLATION PREFLIGHT] Checking native PyOperon and PySR/Julia bridges..."
"$PY_BIN" -c '
import shutil
try:
    from pyoperon.sklearn import SymbolicRegressor
    print("  [OK] pyoperon native C++ backend ready.")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"  [WARN] pyoperon unavailable: {e}")
try:
    import pysr
    print(f"  [OK] pysr package ready (julia={shutil.which(\"julia\")}).")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"  [WARN] pysr unavailable: {e}")
'

echo "[ABLATION] Starting targeted server ablation with CORES=${CORES}..."
Rscript scripts/run_targeted_ablation.R
echo "[ABLATION] Complete. Outputs in results/ablation_native/."


=== FILE: scripts/run_tuning.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/run_tuning.R
# CLI Entrypoint to Execute Phase 4 Systematic BGNLM Prior & GMJMCMC Tuning
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")
source("R/07_tuning_engine.R")

args <- commandArgs(trailingOnly = TRUE)
full_mode <- "--full" %in% args
cores_idx <- which(args == "--cores")
cores <- if (length(cores_idx) > 0L && length(args) > cores_idx) as.integer(args[cores_idx + 1L]) else 2L

res <- execute_bgnlm_tuning(cores = cores, fast_calibration = !full_mode)
print(res$summary[, .(config_id, beta_prior_type, r_rule, p_proj, Mean_F1, Mean_FDR, Exact_Rate, Mean_PIP_Brier, Mean_R2_ood, Composite_Score)])



=== FILE: scripts/setup_server.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# ==============================================================================
# scripts/setup_server.sh
# One-shot bootstrap for remote Linux servers (e.g., nam-shub-01):
# Creates self-contained project-local R (.r_lib/) and Python (.venv/) environments
# ==============================================================================

REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

echo "[SETUP] Repository root: $REPO_ROOT"

# Load R module on EasyBuild/Lmod clusters if Rscript isn't already available or module exists
if command -v module >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
elif [ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh
  module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if ! command -v Rscript >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[ERROR] Rscript not found! Please run 'module load R/4.5.2-gfbf-2025b' (or equivalent) first."
  exit 1
fi

echo "[SETUP] Using Rscript: $(command -v Rscript)"
mkdir -p "$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib" results/checkpoints_synth results/checkpoints_real logs

export R_LIBS_USER="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
export R_LIBS="$REPO_ROOT/.r_lib"
export R_MAKEVARS_USER=/dev/null

# Ensure project-local Python .venv uses Python >= 3.9 (RHEL8 default python3 is 3.6 + pip 9.0.3)
venv_needs_rebuild=0
if [ ! -x "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" ]; then
  venv_needs_rebuild=1
else
  py_ok=$("$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" -c 'import sys; print(int(sys.version_info >= (3, 9)))' 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
  if [ "$py_ok" != "1" ]; then
    echo "[SETUP] Existing .venv uses Python < 3.9; rebuilding with Python 3.11..."
    rm -rf "$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
    venv_needs_rebuild=1
  fi
fi

if [ "$venv_needs_rebuild" = "1" ]; then
  export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
  if ! command -v uv >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[SETUP] Installing uv locally to bootstrap standalone Python 3.11..."
    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh
    export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
  fi
  if command -v uv >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    uv venv "$REPO_ROOT/.venv" --python 3.11
    uv pip install --python "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" numpy scipy scikit-learn sympy pysindy pandas pyyaml
    uv pip install --python "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/python" pysr pyoperon || true
  else
    echo "[WARN] uv unavailable; falling back to module load Python..."
    module load Python 2>/dev/null || true
    python3 -m venv "$REPO_ROOT/.venv"
    "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/pip" install --upgrade pip
    "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/pip" install numpy scipy scikit-learn sympy pysindy pandas pyyaml
    "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/pip" install pysr pyoperon || true
  fi
fi

# Activate .venv so install_dependencies.R sees it
if [ -f "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate" ]; then
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  source "$REPO_ROOT/.venv/bin/activate"
fi

echo "[SETUP] Installing R packages into $REPO_ROOT/.r_lib and verifying .venv..."
Rscript scripts/install_dependencies.R

echo "[SETUP] Running pre-flight environment verification..."
Rscript scripts/verify_env.R

echo "[SETUP] Preparing real-world datasets..."
Rscript scripts/download_real_data.R

echo "[SETUP] Generating manifests if missing..."
if [[ ! -f "config/manifest_synth.csv" || ! -f "config/manifest_real.csv" ]]; then
  Rscript scripts/generate_manifest.R
fi

echo "[SETUP] Running unit test suite..."
Rscript -e 'testthat::test_dir("tests/testthat")'

echo "[OK] Server setup complete! You can now launch benchmarks with:"
echo "     nohup ./scripts/run_remote_server.sh both 40 1 > run_benchmarks.log 2>&1 &"



=== FILE: scripts/verify_env.R ===
#!/usr/bin/env Rscript
# ==============================================================================
# scripts/verify_env.R
# Fast Pre-Flight Verification of Project-Local R (.r_lib) & Python (.venv)
# ==============================================================================

if (file.exists("R/00_env_setup.R")) source("R/00_env_setup.R")

message("[VERIFY] Active R version: ", R.version.string)
message("[VERIFY] Active .libPaths():\n  - ", paste(.libPaths(), collapse = "\n  - "))

required_r_pkgs <- c(
  "FBMS", "deSolve", "glmnet", "signal", "data.table", "yaml", "jsonlite",
  "lme4", "lmerTest", "glmmTMB", "emmeans", "broom.mixed", "BAS",
  "ggplot2", "dplyr", "tidyr", "reticulate", "R.utils", "testthat"
)

for (pkg in required_r_pkgs) {
  ok <- tryCatch({
    suppressPackageStartupMessages(library(pkg, character.only = TRUE))
    TRUE
  }, error = function(e) {
    message("[FAIL] Cannot load R package '", pkg, "': ", e$message)
    FALSE
  })
  if (!ok) stop("Pre-flight R check failed on package: ", pkg)
}

# Verify Python .venv bridge
source("R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
py_bin <- find_python_bin()
message("[VERIFY] Python interpreter: ", py_bin)

py_chk <- system2(
  py_bin,
  c("-c", shQuote("import numpy, scipy, sklearn, sympy, pysindy, pandas, yaml; print('PYTHON_ENV_OK')")),
  stdout = TRUE, stderr = TRUE
)
if (!any(grepl("PYTHON_ENV_OK", py_chk, fixed = TRUE))) {
  stop("Pre-flight Python check failed! Output:\n", paste(py_chk, collapse = "\n"))
}

# Quick sanity check on FBMS + symbolic parser
source("R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
t_dicts <- register_fbms_transforms()
deep_test <- extract_canonical_atoms("p3(1+1*x1+1*x2+1*p3(1+1*x1+1*x2+1*p2(x1)))")
stopifnot(length(deep_test) >= 1L)

message("[OK] Pre-flight verification PASSED (R + FBMS ", as.character(packageVersion("FBMS")), " + Python .venv ready).")



=== FILE: tests/testthat/test_methods_and_mixed_models.R ===
library(testthat)
source("../../R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("../../R/06_metrics_consistency.R")
source("../../R/03_fbms_wrappers.R")
source("../../R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
source("../../R/04b_sr_baselines.R")
source("../../R/08_mixed_effects_eval.R")

test_that("FBMS, SINDy, and Symbolic Regression wrappers return standardized output objects", {
  td <- generate_synthetic_task_data(
    system_id = "sys04_lotka_volterra",
    N_train = 150,
    noise_eta = 0.01,
    seed = 42,
    T_max = 10.0
  )

  res_bgnlm <- run_fbms_method("BGNLM_Full_Tuned", td, cores = 1L, fast_mode = TRUE)
  expect_equal(length(res_bgnlm$discovered), 2L)
  expect_true(is.function(res_bgnlm$predict_rhs_fn))
  st_bgnlm <- compute_structure_metrics(res_bgnlm$discovered, td$system$ground_truth)
  expect_gte(st_bgnlm$TPR, 0.5)

  res_sindy <- run_sindy_baseline("SINDy_STLSQ_Oracle", td)
  expect_equal(length(res_sindy$discovered), 2L)
  st_sindy <- compute_structure_metrics(res_sindy$discovered, td$system$ground_truth)
  expect_equal(st_sindy$TPR, 1.0)

  res_sr <- run_sr_baseline("SR_PySR", td)
  expect_equal(length(res_sr$discovered), 2L)
  expect_equal(dim(res_sr$pred_ood), dim(td$Y_test_ood_clean))
})



=== FILE: tests/testthat/test_simulators.R ===
library(testthat)
source("../../R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("../../R/10_real_data_experiments.R")

test_that("All 12 synthetic ODE systems integrate cleanly and match analytical derivatives with R2 > 0.9999", {
  reg <- get_systems_registry()
  expect_equal(length(reg), 12L)

  for (sys_id in names(reg)) {
    traj <- simulate_base_trajectory(sys_id, T_max = 5.0, use_cache = FALSE)
    expect_true(all(is.finite(traj$X)), info = paste("Non-finite states in", sys_id))
    expect_true(all(is.finite(traj$dX_analytical)), info = paste("Non-finite analytical deriv in", sys_id))

    # Verify finite difference vs analytical derivative has R2 > 0.9999 at dt = 1e-4
    for (j in seq_len(traj$system$dim)) {
      r2_fd <- cor(traj$dX_fd[, j], traj$dX_analytical[, j])^2
      expect_gt(r2_fd, 0.9999, label = paste(sys_id, "eq", j, "FD vs analytical R2"))

      # Verify ground-truth basis atoms in Oracle library yield R2 > 0.9999 against analytical derivative
      Phi_oracle <- build_oracle_library(traj$X[1:1000, , drop = FALSE], extra_atoms = traj$system$oracle_extra_basis)
      gt_atoms <- traj$system$ground_truth[[j]]
      expect_true(all(gt_atoms %in% colnames(Phi_oracle)),
                  info = paste(sys_id, "eq", j, "missing atoms in oracle:", paste(setdiff(gt_atoms, colnames(Phi_oracle)), collapse = ",")))
      fit_lm <- lm(traj$dX_analytical[1:1000, j] ~ Phi_oracle[, gt_atoms, drop = FALSE])
      r2_gt <- suppressWarnings(summary(fit_lm)$r.squared)
      expect_gt(r2_gt, 0.9999, label = paste(sys_id, "eq", j, "ground-truth basis R2"))
    }
  }
})

test_that("Overcomplete library has > 60 candidate columns and finite values", {
  traj <- simulate_base_trajectory("sys02_lorenz63", T_max = 2.0, use_cache = FALSE)
  Phi_over <- build_overcomplete_library(traj$X[1:200, , drop = FALSE])
  expect_gt(ncol(Phi_over), 60L)
  expect_true(all(is.finite(Phi_over)))
})


=== FILE: tests/testthat/test_symbolic_and_metrics.R ===
library(testthat)
source("../../R/01_ode_simulators.R")
source("../../R/02_basis_builders.R")
source("../../R/05_symbolic_canonicalize.R")
source("../../R/06_metrics_consistency.R")

test_that("Algebraically equivalent strings across FBMS, PySR, PyOperon, and PySINDy canonicalize identically", {
  # Commutative products
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x2 * x1"), "x1*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("(x.2 * x.1)"), "x1*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x1 x2"), "x1*x2")

  # Powers and FBMS transforms
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("p2(x1) * x2"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x2 * (x1^2)"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("square(x_1) * x_2"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x1**2 * x2"), "x1^2*x2")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("p3(x.1)"), "x1^3")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("cube(x1)"), "x1^3")

  # Trigonometric and fractional transforms (sin_deg is degree-scaled and MUST NOT match radian sin)
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("sin_rad(x1)"), "sin(x1)")
  expect_false(identical(extract_canonical_atoms("sin_deg(x.1)"), "sin(x1)"))
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("p05(x.1)"), "sqrt(x1)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("sqroot(x1)"), "sqrt(x1)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x1^(-1)"), "1/(x1)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x1^(0.5)"), "sqrt(x1)")

  # Rational / Hill transforms and rational complement equivalence modulo intercept
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("inv_sq(x.3)"), "1/(1+x3^2)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x.3 * inv_pos(x.3)"), "x3/(1+x3)")
  expect_equal(extract_canonical_atoms("x.1 * hill_2(x.2)"), "x1*x2^2/(1+x2^2)")
  expect_equal(normalize_modulo_intercept("x2^2/(1+x2^2)"), "1/(1+x2^2)")
  expect_equal(normalize_modulo_intercept("x1/(1+x1)"), "1/(1+x1)")

  # Full expanded equation from PySR / PyOperon
  expect_equal(
    extract_canonical_atoms("x1 * (28.0 - x3) - 1.0 * x2"),
    c("x1", "x1*x3", "x2")
  )
})

test_that("Selection consistency metrics produce TPR=1, FDR=0, Exact_Recovery=1 for equivalent representations", {
  gt_lorenz <- list(
    c("x1", "x2"),
    c("x1", "x2", "x1*x3"),
    c("x3", "x1*x2")
  )
  discovered_fbms <- list(
    c("x.2", "x.1"),
    c("(x.3 * x.1)", "x.1", "x.2"),
    c("(x.2*x.1)", "x.3")
  )
  m <- compute_structure_metrics(discovered_fbms, gt_lorenz)
  expect_equal(m$TPR, 1.0)
  expect_equal(m$FDR, 0.0)
  expect_equal(m$F1, 1.0)
  expect_equal(m$Exact_Recovery, 1L)
  expect_equal(m$SHD, 0L)
})

test_that("Jaccard and Nogueira stability estimators work properly", {
  sets_perfect <- replicate(10, c("eq1::x1", "eq1::x2", "eq2::x1*x3"), simplify = FALSE)
  expect_equal(compute_jaccard_stability(sets_perfect), 1.0)
  nog <- compute_nogueira_stability(sets_perfect, d_total = 100)
  expect_equal(nog$phi, 1.0)
})

test_that("STLSQ returns lambda and PIP calibration metrics are well-formed", {
  source("../../R/04_sindy_baselines.R")
  set.seed(1)
  X <- matrix(rnorm(100 * 2), ncol = 2); colnames(X) <- c("x1", "x2")
  Phi <- build_poly_library(X, max_deg = 2L)
  y <- 2.0 * X[, 1] - 1.5 * X[, 1] * X[, 2] + rnorm(100, 0, 0.05)
  cv_fit <- cv_stlsq_fit(Phi, y)
  expect_true(!is.null(cv_fit$lambda) && is.finite(cv_fit$lambda))

  pips <- list(c("x1" = 0.99, "x2" = 0.02, "x1*x2" = 0.98))
  gt <- list(c("x1", "x1*x2"))
  cal <- compute_pip_calibration_metrics(pips, gt)
  expect_true(is.finite(cal$PIP_SSPE) && cal$PIP_SSPE < 0.01)
  expect_equal(cal$PIP_AUROC, 1.0)
})
